# AGENTS.md - Read Before Any Code Change

Every agent must read this file before editing this project. Do not skip it. Most breakage in this app has come from 
mixing current values with historical values, or from treating the US and India refresh flows as one shared flow.

> **Also read [`SPEED.md`](SPEED.md) before touching any data-loading, refresh, download, filter, or DB code.** 
Owner's standing order: the download/refresh flow and every filter must stay super-duper fast. AGENTS.md owns 
correctness; SPEED.md owns speed. The `get_db()` pragmas (esp. `mmap_size`) gave a measured 6.8├âΓÇö on the 
date-filter query ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ never weaken them.

## Project Map

- `run.py`: starts the Flask app on port `8474`.
- `dumbmoney/app.py`: Flask routes and `/api/*` endpoints. The main table endpoint is `/api/screener`.
- `dumbmoney/refresh.py`: the only full-site data update path. Refresh downloads data, recomputes stats, fills 
history, and updates derived tables.
- `dumbmoney/engine.py`: indicator computation, current `stats`, `historical_screener`, signal matrix, and aggregates.
- `dumbmoney/indicators.py`: SuperTrend, Accel, weighted alpha, probabilities, streaks, ATRP, confluence helpers.
- `dumbmoney/db.py`: SQLite schema, indexes, WAL mode, DB connections.
- `dumbmoney/templates/base.html`: market switcher and refresh polling UI.
- `dumbmoney/templates/screener.html`: screener filters, sorting, columns, table rendering.
- `/api/screener/columns`: machine-readable source/meaning contract for visible screener columns. Update it whenever 
columns are added, renamed, or re-sourced.
- `screukener.db` is not a valid table or DB name. The real US DB must be `screener.db`.
- Real DBs:
  - US: `screener.db`
  - India: `india.db`

## Non-Negotiable Refresh Invariants

- Refresh is market-scoped. US and India must never share thread state, cancel events, or persisted status.
- US refresh status is stored in US `screener.db`, key `settings.refresh_status`.
- India refresh status is stored in India `india.db`, key `settings.refresh_status`.
- `/api/refresh/status?market=US` must return US status only.
- `/api/refresh/status?market=INDIA` must return India status only.
- `/api/refresh/cancel` must receive and honor the market.
- The frontend must poll status with `MARKET`: `/api/refresh/status?market=${MARKET}`.
- The frontend must cancel with `{market: MARKET}`.
- A normal refresh should recompute current `stats` for updated symbols and historical rows for updated symbols only.
- A full historical rebuild is allowed only when the historical logic/schema version changes or the user explicitly 
asks.
- A no-change fast refresh must not start a full historical rebuild just because the stored historical version is 
missing. Use the explicit `/api/historical/rebuild` maintenance path for full rebuilds.
- Treat `only_symbols=[]` as a deliberate no-op. Never convert an empty updated-symbol list to `None`; `None` means 
full-market recompute.
- Normal refresh must not perform deep 1970/2016 backfill for symbols that are otherwise current. Backfill is a 
separate explicit maintenance/full-rebuild job.

## Refresh Step Rules

Current refresh order:

1. Sync universe.
2. Download daily bars.
3. Vectorized stats.
4. Fundamentals / asset info.
5. Pre/post market, US only.
6. AI scores.
7. Aggregates.
8. Background history and signal probability matrix.

Fast normal refresh contract:

- Universe sync may reuse a recent populated `assets` cache, currently up to 7 days old; do not hit Alpaca/NSE on 
every refresh click unless forced.
- Download daily bars only for symbols whose latest stored daily bar is stale or missing.
- Stale-date cutoff is market-aware: weekends are skipped for both markets, and US observed federal holidays are 
skipped for US so holiday closures do not trigger full downloads.
- Normal refresh seeds brand-new/no-bar symbols from a recent indicator warm-up window, currently 450 calendar days. 
It must not deep-download from 1970/2016 for every newly listed or newly discovered symbol.
- Group incremental downloads by each symbol's own next needed start date. Do not let one very stale symbol force a 
whole mixed batch to download years of extra bars.
- US normal bar refresh uses the fast listed-equity IEX path. Do not include OTC assets in that normal path unless the 
data feed is changed to one that reliably supports them.
- For full default market refresh planning, compute latest/oldest bar dates with indexed per-asset lookups against 
`(symbol,timeframe,date)`. Use `symbol IN (...)` only for genuinely scoped symbol lists; avoid full historical `GROUP 
BY symbol` scans.
- Pass the exact `updated_symbols` list through stats, asset info, pre/post snapshots, earnings, historical screener, 
and any new derived feature.
- If `updated_symbols` is empty, stats, asset info, snapshots, earnings, and incremental history should finish as 
no-ops.
- Normal refresh must not rebuild the full signal probability matrix. The matrix is full-history maintenance and is 
recomputed by explicit `/api/historical/rebuild`.
- If a historical version mismatch exists, one explicit full historical rebuild is expected; do not hide that cost 
inside unrelated current-only feature work.
- `/api/historical/rebuild` is the explicit maintenance endpoint for rebuilding `historical_screener` and 
`signal_prob_matrix`; it can be slow and should not be called by normal fast refresh UI clicks.

When adding a website feature, decide if it needs refresh:

- If the feature displays data derived from downloaded bars, assets, earnings, snapshots, portfolios, strings, or 
historical rows, it must be added to refresh.
- If the feature changes any screener, stock detail, portfolio, string screener, paper trading, or AI strategy value, 
define the refresh step that computes it.
- BTST Dashboard is read-only over current `stats` and generated `ss_strategies`; it must not invent/mock candidates. 
Empty string picks mean String Screener backtest has not generated strategies yet.
- String Screener generation should produce 10k+ real strategy variants from current `stats` plus 
`historical_screener` backtests; keep filters/sorts SQL-backed in `get_strategies`.
- If the feature is visible in date-filter mode, compute and store the value as of each historical date. Do not copy 
current `stats` into old dates.
- If the feature is current-only, return `NULL` or clearly current-only values in historical mode and document that 
here.
- Any new refresh step needs a progress callback, cancellation boundary, market argument, and DB write strategy.
- Never add a long network or pandas operation without progress updates.

## Download & Refresh Speed Contract

These are the things that keep a normal refresh fast. Do not regress any of them.

- Universe sync reuses a populated `assets` cache up to 7 days old. Never hit Alpaca/NSE per refresh click; 
`force=True` is the explicit universe re-sync.
- Plan downloads with one indexed per-asset `MAX(bars.date)` correlated subquery. Skip the `MIN(date)` oldest-date 
subquery unless `allow_backfill=True`; it doubles planning time on the huge bars table for data the normal path never 
reads.
- Download only stale symbols: latest stored bar older than the market-aware last-weekday cutoff. Weekends skip for 
both markets; US federal holidays skip for US.
- Group incremental downloads by each symbol's own next start date (latest bar + 1 day). New/no-bar symbols seed from 
the 450-day warm-up window, never 1970/2016.
- US normal path is the Alpaca multi-symbol IEX feed (`feed=iex`, 200-symbol batches, paged) with OTC excluded. India 
is pooled pre-authenticated Yahoo sessions with a background writer thread so downloads never block on DB writes.
- Keep the symbol list and its per-asset date lookup on the same asset filter (`status='active'`, and for US 
`tradable=1`, non-OTC). A symbol in the list but missing from the date map is treated as brand new and re-downloads 
450 days on every refresh.
- Bulk insert bars with `executemany` per API page/batch. No row-by-row commits inside download loops.
- Downloader helpers return `[]` when nothing was downloaded ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ never a bare `return`. `None` downstream means 
full-market recompute; `[]` means no-op.
- Pass the returned `updated_symbols` list unchanged into stats, asset info, snapshots, earnings, and historical 
screener.
- Persist progress at least once per download batch and every ~50 symbols during stats. `_persist_status` already 
throttles to one DB write per second, so callbacks may be frequent; the UI must never sit on a stale phase for minutes.
- Normal refresh appends only the latest basket-history date. If the string universe changed size, say so in the 
status phase and stop; the full basket history rebuild belongs to the explicit Basket Screener generate/historical 
endpoints, never to normal refresh.

## Date Filter Atomic Semantics

`/api/screener?date_cutoff=YYYY-MM-DD` means: show values as they were on that selected trading date.

Authoritative source for date-filter mode:

- Use `historical_screener h` for all historical indicator/table values.
- Join `assets a` only for metadata: `name`, `exchange`, `asset_class`, `fractionable`, `marginable`.
- Join `stats s` only for explicitly current-only fields such as `profit_status` until a historical fundamentals table 
exists.

Column meanings in date-filter mode:

- `symbol`: `historical_screener.symbol`
- `date`: selected date
- `price`: `bars.close` for that exact date, stored as `historical_screener.price`
- `volume`: `bars.volume` for that exact date
- `change_pct`: close(selected date) vs previous available trading close for the same symbol
- `next_day_return`: next available trading close vs selected date close
- `next_5d_return`: close five trading rows later vs selected date close
- `weighted_alpha`: computed using only bars up to and including the selected date
- `atrp`: computed using only bars up to and including the selected date
- `streak`: computed using only closes up to and including the selected date
- `atr_signal`, `atr_stop`, `atr_value`, `atr_streak`, `atr_crossed_above`, `atr_crossed_below`: SuperTrend state as 
of the selected date
- `accel_a`, `accel_base`, `accel_signal`, `accel_crossed_up`, `accel_crossed_down`: Accel state as of the selected 
date
- `prob_up_1d`, `prob_up_5d`: trailing completed-period probabilities using bars available as of the selected date
- `confluence`: computed from the as-of-date row
- `ai_*`: local vectorized AI score computed as of the selected date
- `pre_price`, `post_price`, `pre_change_pct`, `post_change_pct`: current-session only; return `NULL` in historical 
mode unless historical extended-hours data is added
- `last_updated`: the selected historical date, not today's stats timestamp

Never do this in date-filter mode:

- Do not query old `bars` and join current `stats` for indicators.
- Do not sort/filter a page in Python after SQL pagination.
- Do not sort historical `change_pct` by `bars.close`.
- Do not copy today's `weighted_alpha`, `streak`, ATR, Accel, probability, or next-day-return into old dates.

## Sorting And Filtering Rules

- Current mode uses `stats`.
- Historical mode uses `historical_screener`.
- Keep current and historical screener responses field-compatible. If a visible current-only field has no historical 
source, return it as `NULL` in historical mode and document it in `/api/screener/columns`.
- Keep `/api/screener/columns` synchronized with `templates/screener.html` and the SQL SELECT lists.
- The visible table columns, `/api/screener/columns`, current SELECT list, and historical SELECT list must move 
together in one atomic patch. If one changes and the others do not, stop and fix the contract before testing UI.
- Apply filters in SQL before `COUNT`, `ORDER BY`, `LIMIT`, and `OFFSET`.
- Apply sorting in SQL before pagination.
- Python may format returned rows, but must not change which rows belong on a page.
- Use allowlists for sort columns. Never concatenate raw user sort input into SQL.
- Order with `<col> <dir> NULLS LAST`, never `CASE WHEN <col> IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END, <col> <dir>`. Both produce 
identical rows, but the CASE wrapper forces a temp B-tree sort while NULLS LAST lets current mode use the stats 
indexes (measured 74ms to 4ms on US).
- Historical `change_pct` sort must use `h.change_pct`.
- Historical `weighted_alpha` sort must use `h.weighted_alpha`.
- Historical `next_day_return` sort must use `h.next_day_return`.

## Historical Rebuild Rules

- Historical logic version is stored in `settings.historical_screener_version`.
- Current version: `asof-v2`.
- If the code changes historical meanings, bump `HISTORICAL_SCREENER_VERSION` in `engine.py`.
- A version mismatch should delete and rebuild `historical_screener` and `signal_prob_matrix` once.
- After a successful rebuild, write the new version to settings.
- Incremental refresh should recompute symbols passed through `only_symbols`.
- If no symbols changed and the version is current, historical recompute should no-op.
- `only_symbols=[...]` must remain scoped even when the stored historical version is missing; do not delete/rebuild 
the whole table from a scoped validation or incremental refresh.
- `only_symbols=[...]` must compare `MAX(bars.date)` to `MAX(historical_screener.date)` for those symbols before 
computing. If the historical rows are already current and the version is current, return immediately.
- When appending historical rows for changed scoped symbols, compute indicators with the full symbol history but 
insert only dates after that symbol's latest existing historical date unless the version changed or a forced rebuild 
is running.
- Only a full rebuild (`only_symbols is None`) may write `settings.historical_screener_version`.

## Performance Rules

- SQLite is the bottleneck more often than math. Check query plans before adding Python work.
- Keep WAL mode and `busy_timeout`.
- Use compound indexes for date-filter paths.
- Benchmark and inspect `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN` before adding heavyweight compound indexes to huge historical tables; do 
not create many startup indexes blindly.
- Existing DBs should keep only lean historical indexes by default: primary key `(symbol,date)`, `idx_hs_sym_date`, 
and `idx_hs_date`. Extra date+sort indexes made historical writes much slower and were removed from the current DBs.
- Select only needed columns from huge tables.
- COUNT queries must drop joins that cannot change the row count (a LEFT JOIN on a unique key that no filter 
references).
- Never `SELECT DISTINCT` or `COUNT(DISTINCT)` straight over the multi-million-row bars table on a request path. Use a 
recursive loose index scan (one indexed MAX/MIN seek per distinct value) as in `/api/hs-dates` and `/api/market-stats` 
(measured 3.6s to 26ms on India dates, 18s to 0.2s on India symbol count).
- `/api/market-stats` runs on every page load; its distinct-symbol counts stay behind a 60s cache keyed by latest bar 
date. Keep cheap queries live, keep heavy ones cached.
- Never `SELECT * FROM historical_screener` for signal probability matrix.
- Bulk insert historical rows in chunks.
- Avoid row-by-row DB writes inside symbol/date loops.
- Use pandas/NumPy vectorization before considering JAX.
- Do not add JAX to the app runtime unless a benchmark proves it beats pandas/NumPy for the exact workload including 
startup/JIT cost.

## Future Anti-Break Checks

- Before changing refresh, write down the exact market, table, source rows, recompute step, and visible columns 
affected.
- Before changing date filters, test at least one older non-latest date where `next_day_return` is not zero and 
compare it to the next trading close.
- Before changing sorting, test both `sort_dir=asc` and `sort_dir=desc` for current and historical mode with 
`per_page` small enough to inspect.
- Before adding a column, add it to `SCREENER_COLUMN_REFERENCE`, both SQL SELECT lists, the UI `COLUMNS` array, and 
this file if the meaning is new.
- Keep ordinary refresh fast by passing `updated_symbols`; use `/api/historical/rebuild` only for explicit full 
historical maintenance.
- Do not put full-table signal matrix rebuild back into normal refresh. It scans millions of historical rows and makes 
small daily refreshes slow.

## Signal Probability Matrix

Only these columns are needed from `historical_screener`:

- `atr_signal`
- `atr_streak`
- `accel_signal`
- `accel_crossed_up`
- `accel_crossed_down`
- `weighted_alpha`
- `next_day_return`

Do not load unrelated historical columns for this job.
Compute the matrix SQL-first with grouped aggregates. Do not load millions of historical rows into pandas just to 
group a small state/bucket matrix.
Even SQL-first, it is full-history maintenance; call it from `/api/historical/rebuild`, not normal refresh.

## Validation Checklist Before Finishing

- Run `python -m py_compile` on changed Python files.
- Check `/api/screener?market=US` and `/api/screener?market=INDIA`.
- Check date-filter mode for both markets.
- Verify date-filter sorting by `change_pct`, `weighted_alpha`, `next_day_return`, `price`, and `volume`.
- Verify filters apply before pagination.
- Compare one symbol across three dates and confirm historical indicators are not repeated current values.
- Confirm latest historical row matches current bar price and volume.
- Confirm `next_day_return` equals next trading close vs selected close for old rows.
- Confirm `/api/refresh/status?market=US` and `/api/refresh/status?market=INDIA` are independent.
- Confirm cancel sends and honors the selected market.
- Use `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN` for slow screener queries.

## Sources For Performance Guidance

- SQLite WAL: https://sqlite.org/wal.html
- SQLite query planner: https://sqlite.org/queryplanner.html
- SQLite `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN`: https://www.sqlite.org/eqp.html
- pandas performance: https://pandas.pydata.org/docs/user_guide/enhancingperf.html
- JAX JIT: https://docs.jax.dev/en/latest/jit-compilation.html
# SPEED.md ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ READ THIS BEFORE ANY WEBSITE CHANGE

Owner's standing order: **the download/refresh flow and every filter must stay super-duper fast.**
Any change that touches data loading, refresh, screener filters, or the DBs must obey this file.
This complements `AGENTS.md` (which owns correctness/invariants); this file owns *speed*.

## The golden rules (in priority order)

1. **SQLite pragmas are already tuned in `dumbmoney/db.py::get_db()` ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ do not weaken them.**
   - `mmap_size=4GB` (OS caps at 2GB on this Windows box) ├óΓÇáΓÇÖ measured **6.8├âΓÇö faster** on the date-filter 
screener query (720ms ├óΓÇáΓÇÖ 106ms). If you add a new connection factory, copy ALL of: `journal_mode=WAL`, 
`synchronous=NORMAL`, `busy_timeout=30000`, `temp_store=MEMORY`, `cache_size=-262144`, `mmap_size=4294967296`.
   - Never open a raw `sqlite3.connect()` for a read path without these pragmas.

2. **Filter ├óΓÇáΓÇÖ Count ├óΓÇáΓÇÖ Order ├óΓÇáΓÇÖ Limit, all in SQL. Never in Python.**
   - The screener already does this (`app.py` ~line 356/484). Keep it. Never `fetchall()` a big table and slice in 
pandas/Python.
   - Sort columns must go through the allowlist. Never string-concat user sort input into SQL.

3. **Refresh is incremental. Never full-scan for a normal refresh.**
   - Download bars only for symbols whose latest stored daily bar is stale (market-aware cutoff: skip weekends + US 
holidays).
   - Group incremental downloads by each symbol's own next-needed start date. One stale symbol must NOT drag a whole 
batch into years of history.
   - Pass `updated_symbols` through every step (stats, asset info, snapshots, earnings, historical). Empty list = 
no-op, NOT full recompute.
   - Never trigger `/api/historical/rebuild` or the signal-matrix rebuild from a normal refresh. Those are explicit 
maintenance only.

4. **Downloads: batch, cache, parallelize.**
   - Reuse the `assets` universe cache (up to 7 days) ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ do not hit Alpaca/NSE on every click.
   - Use the fast IEX listed-equity path for US; no OTC in the normal path.
   - Alpaca bars: request multiple symbols per HTTP call where the API allows; page with `next_page_token`; never 
one-symbol-per-request loops for the whole universe.
   - Bulk-insert bars with `executemany` in chunks (5├óΓé¼ΓÇ£10k rows) inside ONE transaction. Never row-by-row 
`INSERT` inside a symbol/date loop.

5. **Progress cadence: emit a progress callback at least every ~1├óΓé¼ΓÇ£2 seconds of work** so the UI never looks 
frozen. Every long network/pandas step needs a progress update and a cancellation checkpoint.

6. **Select only needed columns.** Never `SELECT *` on `bars` or `historical_screener`. Never load unrelated columns 
for the signal matrix (only the 7 it needs).

7. **Indexes: measure before adding.** The huge historical tables keep ONLY: PK `(symbol,date)`, `idx_hs_sym_date`, 
`idx_hs_date`, `idx_hs_date_sym`. Run `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN` before adding any index ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ extra indexes previously 
made historical writes much slower and were removed.

## Fast-path checklist before you finish a website change
- [ ] Did I keep all `get_db()` pragmas (esp. `mmap_size`)?
- [ ] Is every filter/sort/paginate in SQL, not Python?
- [ ] Does a no-change refresh finish as a near-instant no-op (no downloads, no rebuild)?
- [ ] Are new downloads incremental + batched + chunked-insert?
- [ ] Does every slow step report progress and honor cancel + market scope?
- [ ] Did I `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN` any query I changed on the 10M-row tables?

## Known measured numbers (baseline ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ beat these, never regress)
- Date-filter screener query (filter+order+limit on 10.5M-row US `historical_screener`): **~106 ms** warm with mmap. 
If yours is slower, you broke something.
- Current-mode screener (stats, ~10k rows): should be single-digit ms.

131	def generate_string_universe(market="US", n=STRING_COUNT, force=False):
132	    """Pre-generate fixed random baskets. Each string has exactly 10 stocks.
133	    US: $1000 allocation, fractional for fractionable stocks.
134	    India: Rs 100000 allocation, all whole-number weights."""
135	    ALLOCATION = 1000.0 if market == "US" else 100000.0
136	    TARGET_STOCKS = 10
137	    conn = get_db(market)
138	    try:
139	        existing = conn.execute(
140	            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM string_universe WHERE market=?", (market,)).fetchone()[0]
141	        if existing >= n and not force:
142	            logger.info(f"[{market}] string_universe already has {existing} strings; skipping")
143	            return existing
144	        if force:
145	            conn.execute("DELETE FROM string_constituents WHERE string_id IN "
146	                         "(SELECT string_id FROM string_universe WHERE market=?)", (market,))
147	            conn.execute("DELETE FROM string_universe WHERE market=?", (market,))
148	            conn.commit()
149	
150	        syms = _eligible_symbols(market)
151	        if len(syms) < TARGET_STOCKS:
152	            logger.warning(f"[{market}] too few eligible symbols ({len(syms)})")
153	            return 0
154	
155	        price_rows = conn.execute(
156	            f"SELECT symbol, price FROM stats WHERE symbol IN ({','.join('?' * len(syms))})",
157	            syms).fetchall()
158	        price_map = {r[0]: float(r[1]) for r in price_rows if r[1] and float(r[1]) > 0}
159	
160	        frac_rows = conn.execute(
161	            f"SELECT symbol, fractionable FROM assets WHERE symbol IN ({','.join('?' * len(syms))})",
162	            syms).fetchall()
163	        frac_map = {r[0]: bool(r[1]) for r in frac_rows}
164	
165	        priced_syms = [s for s in syms if s in price_map]
166	        if len(priced_syms) < TARGET_STOCKS:
167	            logger.warning(f"[{market}] too few symbols with prices ({len(priced_syms)})")
168	            return 0
169	
170	        rng = np.random.default_rng(20240707 if market == "US" else 20240708)
171	        sym_arr = np.array(priced_syms, dtype=object)
172	
173	        start = conn.execute(
174	            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(CAST(SUBSTR(string_id,2) AS INTEGER)),0) "
175	            "FROM string_universe WHERE string_id LIKE 'S%'").fetchone()[0]
176	        count = 0
177	        batch_univ = []
178	        batch_cons = []
179	        per_stock = ALLOCATION / TARGET_STOCKS
180	
181	        circuit_checked = set()
182	        circuit_excluded = set()
183	        _import_time = __import__('time')
184	
185	        for i in range(start + 1, start + 1 + n):
186	            idx = rng.choice(len(sym_arr), size=TARGET_STOCKS, replace=False)
187	            picked = sym_arr[idx]
188	
189	            if market == "INDIA":
190	                filtered_picked = []
191	                for j in range(TARGET_STOCKS):
192	                    sym = str(picked[j])
193	                    price = price_map.get(sym, 0)
194	                    if sym not in circuit_checked and price > 0:
195	                        circuit_checked.add(sym)
196	                        _import_time.sleep(0.02)
197	                        if _is_at_circuit(sym, price):
198	                            circuit_excluded.add(sym)
199	                    if sym not in circuit_excluded:
200	                        filtered_picked.append(picked[j])
201	                if len(filtered_picked) < TARGET_STOCKS:
202	                    continue
203	                picked = np.array(filtered_picked[:TARGET_STOCKS])
204	
205	            weights = np.zeros(TARGET_STOCKS)
206	            nonfrac_count = 0
207	            for j in range(TARGET_STOCKS):
208	                sym = str(picked[j])
209	                price = price_map[sym]
210	                is_frac = frac_map.get(sym, False) if market == "US" else False
211	                raw_w = per_stock / price
212	                if market == "INDIA" or not is_frac:
213	                    weights[j] = max(1.0, round(raw_w))
214	                    nonfrac_count += 1
215	                else:
216	                    weights[j] = round(raw_w, 4)
217	
218	            if market == "US" and nonfrac_count >= 3:
219	                for j in range(TARGET_STOCKS):
220	                    if frac_map.get(str(picked[j]), False):
221	                        weights[j] = round(weights[j])
222	
223	            weights = np.maximum(weights, 0.001)
224	            sid = f"S{i:06d}"
225	            expr = " + ".join(f"{picked[j]}*{weights[j]:g}" for j in range(TARGET_STOCKS))
226	            batch_univ.append((sid, market, TARGET_STOCKS, expr, datetime.utcnow().isoformat(), 1))
227	            for j in range(TARGET_STOCKS):
228	                batch_cons.append((sid, str(picked[j]), float(weights[j])))
229	            count += 1
230	            if len(batch_univ) >= 2000:
231	                conn.executemany(
232	                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_universe "
233	                    "(string_id, market, num_stocks, expression, created_at, active) "
234	                    "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)", batch_univ)
235	                conn.executemany(
236	                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_constituents (string_id, symbol, weight) "
237	                    "VALUES (?,?,?)", batch_cons)
238	                conn.commit()
239	                batch_univ.clear(); batch_cons.clear()
240	        if batch_univ:
241	            conn.executemany(
242	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_universe "
243	                "(string_id, market, num_stocks, expression, created_at, active) "
244	                "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)", batch_univ)
245	            conn.executemany(
246	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_constituents (string_id, symbol, weight) "
247	                "VALUES (?,?,?)", batch_cons)
248	            conn.commit()
249	        logger.info(f"[{market}] generated {count} strings (10 stocks, ${ALLOCATION:.0f} allocation)")
250	        return count
251	    finally:
252	        conn.close()
253

255	def _load_composition(market, string_ids=None):
256	    """Return (string_ids, sym_list, indices, weights).
257	    indices: (n_strings, 10) int array ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ column indices into close_pivot
258	    weights: (n_strings, 10) float array ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ corresponding weights"""
259	    conn = get_db(market)
260	    try:
--- dumbmoney/basket_screener.py:780-1200

783	def _compute_basket_indicators(basket_ohlc, period=14, multiplier=1.0):
784	    """Compute SuperTrend and Accel for all baskets. Returns dict of (n_strings, n_dates) arrays.
785	    Uses numba prange parallelism when available (100x+ speedup)."""
786	    n_strings, n_dates = basket_ohlc['close'].shape
787	    keys_st = ['atr_signal', 'atr_stop', 'atr_value', 'atr_streak',
788	               'atr_crossed_above', 'atr_crossed_below', 'atr_multiplier']
789	    keys_ac = ['accel_a', 'accel_base', 'accel_signal', 'accel_crossed_up',
790	               'accel_crossed_down', 'accel_streak']
791	    result = {k: np.zeros((n_strings, n_dates), dtype=np.float32) for k in keys_st + keys_ac}
792	
793	    if HAVE_NUMBA and n_strings > 100:
794	        hi_all = basket_ohlc['high'].astype(np.float64)
795	        lo_all = basket_ohlc['low'].astype(np.float64)
796	        cl_all = basket_ohlc['close'].astype(np.float64)
797	
798	        _compute_all_numba(hi_all, lo_all, cl_all,
799	                           result['atr_signal'], result['atr_stop'], result['atr_value'],
800	                           result['atr_streak'], result['atr_crossed_above'], result['atr_crossed_below'],
801	                           result['atr_multiplier'],
802	                           result['accel_a'], result['accel_base'], result['accel_signal'],
803	                           result['accel_crossed_up'], result['accel_crossed_down'], result['accel_streak'],
804	                           n_strings, n_dates, period, multiplier)
805	        logger.info(f"  Basket indicators computed with numba parallel ({n_strings} strings)")
806	    else:
807	        for i in range(n_strings):
808	            h = basket_ohlc['high'][i]
809	            l = basket_ohlc['low'][i]
810	            c = basket_ohlc['close'][i]
811	            st = _supertrend_vec(h, l, c, period, multiplier)
812	            ac = _accel_vec(c)
813	            for k in keys_st:
814	                result[k][i] = st[k].astype(np.float32)
815	            for k in keys_ac:
816	                result[k][i] = ac[k].astype(np.float32)
817	            if (i + 1) % 5000 == 0:
818	                logger.info(f"  Basket indicators: {i+1}/{n_strings}")
819	
820	    return result
821

823	if HAVE_NUMBA:
824	    @njit(cache=True, fastmath=True, parallel=True)
825	    def _compute_all_numba(hi_all, lo_all, cl_all,
826	                           r_atr_signal, r_atr_stop, r_atr_value, r_atr_streak,
827	                           r_atr_ca, r_atr_cb, r_atr_mult,
828	                           r_accel_a, r_accel_base, r_accel_signal,
829	                           r_accel_cu, r_accel_cd, r_accel_streak,
830	                           n_strings, n_dates, period, multiplier):
831	        """Parallel SuperTrend + Accel for all strings via prange."""
832	        for i in prange(n_strings):
833	            hi = hi_all[i]
834	            lo = lo_all[i]
835	            cl = cl_all[i]
836	
837	            (dir_arr, st_stop, atr_val, st_streak, ca, cb, mult) = _supertrend_standalone(hi, lo, cl, period, 
multiplier)
838	            (aa, ab, asig, acu, acd, astreak) = _accel_standalone(cl)
839	
840	            for t in range(n_dates):
841	                r_atr_signal[i, t] = dir_arr[t]
842	                r_atr_stop[i, t] = st_stop[t]
843	                r_atr_value[i, t] = atr_val[t]
844	                r_atr_streak[i, t] = st_streak[t]
845	                r_atr_ca[i, t] = ca[t]
846	                r_atr_cb[i, t] = cb[t]
847	                r_atr_mult[i, t] = mult
848	                r_accel_a[i, t] = aa[t]
849	                r_accel_base[i, t] = ab[t]
850	                r_accel_signal[i, t] = asig[t]
851	                r_accel_cu[i, t] = acu[t]
852	                r_accel_cd[i, t] = acd[t]
853	                r_accel_streak[i, t] = astreak[t]

856	def _weighted_current(indices, weights, market, sym_list):
857	    """Current weighted metrics -> dict col -> 1D array (n_strings) using gather+einsum."""
858	    conn = get_db(market)
859	    try:
860	        out = {}
861	        df = _chunked_read(
862	            "SELECT symbol, " + ", ".join(STATS_COLS) + " FROM stats WHERE symbol IN (__PH__)",
863	            conn, sym_list)
864	        ai = _chunked_read(
865	            "SELECT symbol, " + ", ".join(AI_COLS) + " FROM ai_analysis WHERE symbol IN (__PH__)",
866	            conn, sym_list)
867	        df = df.set_index("symbol").reindex(sym_list).fillna(0)
868	        ai = ai.set_index("symbol").reindex(sym_list).fillna(0)
869	        weight_sums = weights.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
870	        weight_sums[weight_sums == 0] = 1.0
871	        Wn = weights / weight_sums
872	        for col in STATS_COLS:
873	            vals = df[col].values.astype(np.float32)
874	            gathered = vals[indices]
875	            if col == "volume":
876	                out[col] = np.einsum('bi,bi->b', weights, gathered)
877	            else:
878	                out[col] = np.einsum('bi,bi->b', Wn, gathered)
879	        aim = pd.to_numeric(ai["ai_matrix"], errors="coerce").fillna(0).values.astype(np.float32)
880	        out["ai_matrix"] = np.einsum('bi,bi->b', Wn, aim[indices])
881	        for col in ["overall_score", "tech_score", "momentum_score",
882	                    "volume_score", "events_score", "volume_profile_score",
883	                    "trendline_score", "sentiment_score"]:
884	            vals = pd.to_numeric(ai[col], errors="coerce").fillna(0).values.astype(np.float32)
885	            out[f"ai_{col}"] = np.einsum('bi,bi->b', Wn, vals[indices])
886	        return out
887	    finally:
888	        conn.close()
889

891	def compute_current_metrics(market="US"):
892	    """Recompute string_screener_metrics for all strings (fast, matrix ops).
893	    SuperTrend and Accel are computed on the basket value itself, not averaged from components."""
894	    t0 = time.time()
895	    sids, sym_list, indices, weights = _load_composition(market)
896	    if not sids:
897	        logger.info(f"[{market}] no strings; generate universe first")
898	        return 0
899	    _, dates, close = _load_close_pivot(market, sym_list, date_limit=80)
900	    if close.shape[1] == 0:
901	        logger.warning(f"[{market}] no close data")
902	        return 0
903	    V = _gather_einsum(close, indices, weights)
904	    series = _series_metrics(V)
905	    wm = _weighted_current(indices, weights, market, sym_list)
906	
907	    n = len(sids)
908	    Vn = series["V"]
909	    ret = series["ret"]
910	    price = Vn[:, -1].astype(np.float32)
911	    change_pct = ret[:, -1].astype(np.float32) if ret.shape[1] >= 1 else np.zeros(n, np.float32)
912	    next_day = ret[:, -1].astype(np.float32) if ret.shape[1] >= 2 else np.zeros(n, np.float32)
913	    if Vn.shape[1] >= 6:
914	        sub = Vn[:, -6:]
915	        next_5d = (((sub[:, -1] - sub[:, 0]) / np.where(sub[:, 0] == 0, 1e-9, sub[:, 0])) * 
100.0).astype(np.float32)
916	    else:
917	        next_5d = np.zeros(n, np.float32)
918	    streak = series["streak_series"][:, -1].astype(np.float32) if series["streak_series"].shape[1] >= 1 else 
np.zeros(n, np.float32)
919	    volume = wm.get("volume", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
920	    wa = wm.get("weighted_alpha", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
921	    atrp = wm.get("atrp", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
922	    prob1 = wm.get("prob_up_1d", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
923	    prob5 = wm.get("prob_up_5d", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
924	    confluence = wm.get("confluence", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
925	    atr_mult = np.ones(n, np.float32)
926	
927	    ohlc = _load_ohlc_pivots(market, sym_list, date_limit=80)
928	    if ohlc is not None:
929	        high_p, low_p, open_p = ohlc
930	        basket_ohlc = _compute_basket_ohlc(close, high_p, low_p, open_p, indices, weights)
931	        basket_ind = _compute_basket_indicators(basket_ohlc, period=14, multiplier=1.0)
932	        atr_signal = basket_ind["atr_signal"][:, -1].astype(np.int32)
933	        atr_stop = basket_ind["atr_stop"][:, -1].astype(np.float32)
934	        atr_value = basket_ind["atr_value"][:, -1].astype(np.float32)
935	        atr_streak = basket_ind["atr_streak"][:, -1].astype(np.float32)
936	        atr_cross_up = basket_ind["atr_crossed_above"][:, -1].astype(np.int32)
937	        atr_cross_down = basket_ind["atr_crossed_below"][:, -1].astype(np.int32)
938	        accel_a = basket_ind["accel_a"][:, -1].astype(np.float32)
939	        accel_base = basket_ind["accel_base"][:, -1].astype(np.float32)
940	        accel_signal = basket_ind["accel_signal"][:, -1].astype(np.int32)
941	        accel_cross_up = basket_ind["accel_crossed_up"][:, -1].astype(np.int32)
942	        accel_cross_down = basket_ind["accel_crossed_down"][:, -1].astype(np.int32)
943	        accel_streak = basket_ind["accel_streak"][:, -1].astype(np.float32)
944	    else:
945	        atr_signal = _majority_signal(wm.get("atr_signal", np.zeros(n, np.float32)))
946	        accel_signal = _majority_signal(wm.get("accel_signal", np.zeros(n, np.float32)))
947	        atr_cross_up = _cross_flag(wm.get("atr_crossed_above", np.zeros(n, np.float32)))
948	        atr_cross_down = _cross_flag(wm.get("atr_crossed_below", np.zeros(n, np.float32)))
949	        accel_cross_up = _cross_flag(wm.get("accel_crossed_up", np.zeros(n, np.float32)))
950	        accel_cross_down = _cross_flag(wm.get("accel_crossed_down", np.zeros(n, np.float32)))
951	        atr_stop = wm.get("atr_stop", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
952	        atr_value = wm.get("atr_value", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
953	        atr_streak = np.round(wm.get("atr_streak", np.zeros(n, np.float32))).astype(np.float32)
954	        accel_a = wm.get("accel_a", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
955	        accel_base = wm.get("accel_base", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
956	        accel_streak = np.round(wm.get("accel_streak", np.zeros(n, np.float32))).astype(np.float32)
957	
958	    ai_matrix = wm.get("ai_matrix", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
959	    ai_overall = ai_matrix
960	    ai_bias = np.where(ai_matrix > 55, "bullish", np.where(ai_matrix < 45, "bearish", "neutral")).astype(object)
961	    ai_vol = wm.get("ai_volume_profile_score", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
962	    ai_trend = wm.get("ai_trendline_score", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
963	    ai_sent = wm.get("ai_sentiment_score", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
964	    ai_tech = wm.get("ai_tech_score", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
965	    ai_mom = wm.get("ai_momentum_score", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
966	    ai_volsc = wm.get("ai_volume_score", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
967	    ai_evt = wm.get("ai_events_score", np.zeros(n, np.float32))
968	    ai_concl = np.where(ai_matrix > 60, "BUY", np.where(ai_matrix < 40, "SELL", "HOLD")).astype(object)
969	
970	    now = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
971	    conn = get_db(market)
972	    try:
973	        def _safe_int(v):
974	            try:
975	                f = float(v)
976	                return 0 if not np.isfinite(f) else int(f)
977	            except (TypeError, ValueError):
978	                return 0
979	        def _safe_float(v):
980	            try:
981	                f = float(v)
982	                return 0.0 if not np.isfinite(f) else f
983	            except (TypeError, ValueError):
984	                return 0.0
985	        rows = []
986	        for i in range(n):
987	            rows.append((
988	                sids[i], market, sids[i], "BASKET", "basket",
989	                _safe_float(price[i]), _safe_float(change_pct[i]), _safe_float(volume[i]), _safe_float(wa[i]),
990	                _safe_float(atrp[i]), _safe_int(streak[i]), _safe_int(atr_signal[i]), _safe_float(atr_stop[i]),
991	                _safe_float(atr_value[i]), _safe_int(atr_streak[i]), _safe_int(atr_cross_up[i]), 
_safe_int(atr_cross_down[i]),
992	                _safe_float(atr_mult[i]), _safe_float(next_day[i]), _safe_float(next_5d[i]), 
_safe_float(prob1[i]), _safe_float(prob5[i]),
993	                None, None, None, None, None, 0, 0,
994	                _safe_float(accel_a[i]), _safe_float(accel_base[i]), _safe_int(accel_signal[i]), 
_safe_int(accel_cross_up[i]),
995	                _safe_int(accel_cross_down[i]), _safe_int(accel_streak[i]), _safe_float(confluence[i]),
996	                _safe_float(ai_overall[i]), str(ai_bias[i]), _safe_float(ai_tech[i]), _safe_float(ai_mom[i]),
997	                _safe_float(ai_volsc[i]), _safe_float(ai_evt[i]), _safe_float(ai_vol[i]), _safe_float(ai_trend[i]),
998	                _safe_float(ai_sent[i]), str(ai_concl[i]), _safe_float(ai_matrix[i]), now,
999	            ))
1000	        cols = ["string_id", "market", "name", "exchange", "asset_class", "price", "change_pct",
1001	                "volume", "weighted_alpha", "atrp", "streak", "atr_signal", "atr_stop", "atr_value",
1002	                "atr_streak", "atr_crossed_above", "atr_crossed_below", "atr_multiplier",
1003	                "next_day_return", "next_5d_return", "prob_up_1d", "prob_up_5d",
1004	                "pre_price", "pre_change_pct", "post_price", "post_change_pct", "profit_status",
1005	                "fractionable", "marginable", "accel_a", "accel_base", "accel_signal",
1006	                "accel_crossed_up", "accel_crossed_down", "accel_streak", "confluence",
1007	                "ai_overall_score", "ai_bias", "ai_tech_score", "ai_momentum_score", "ai_volume_score",
1008	                "ai_events_score", "ai_volume_profile_score", "ai_trendline_score", "ai_sentiment_score",
1009	                "ai_conclusion", "ai_matrix", "updated_at"]
1010	        placeholders = ",".join(["?"] * len(cols))
1011	        conn.execute(f"DELETE FROM string_screener_metrics WHERE market=?", (market,))
1012	        for j in range(0, len(rows), 5000):
1013	            conn.executemany(
1014	                f"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_screener_metrics ({','.join(cols)}) "
1015	                f"VALUES ({placeholders})", rows[j:j + 5000])
1016	        conn.commit()
1017	        logger.info(f"[{market}] current string metrics done: {n} in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s")
1018	        return n
1019	    finally:
1020	        conn.close()
1021

1023	def _majority_signal(vec):
1024	    return np.sign(vec).astype(np.int32)

1027	def _cross_flag(vec):
1028	    return (vec > 0.25).astype(np.int32)

1031	def generate_long_short_strings(market="US", n=25000):
1032	    """Generate long+short strings. Each has 5 long (highest WA) + 5 short (lowest WA).
1033	    Only stocks, marginable, shortable, volume >= 100000.
1034	    Total 10 stocks per string. 60/40 allocation ensures positive initial basket value:
1035	    $600 long (120/stock) + -$400 short (-80/stock) = $200 net initial."""
1036	    ALLOCATION = 1000.0
1037	    LONG_PCT = 0.60
1038	    SHORT_PCT = 0.40
1039	    LONG_N = 5
1040	    SHORT_N = 5
1041	    TARGET_STOCKS = LONG_N + SHORT_N
1042	    conn = get_db(market)
1043	    try:
1044	        existing = conn.execute(
1045	            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM string_universe WHERE market=? AND string_id LIKE 'LS%'",
1046	            (market,)).fetchone()[0]
1047	        if existing >= n:
1048	            logger.info(f"[{market}] ls_string_universe already has {existing} strings; skipping")
1049	            return existing
1050	
1051	        conn.execute("DELETE FROM string_constituents WHERE string_id IN "
1052	                     "(SELECT string_id FROM string_universe WHERE market=? AND string_id LIKE 'LS%')",
1053	                     (market,))
1054	        conn.execute("DELETE FROM string_universe WHERE market=? AND string_id LIKE 'LS%'", (market,))
1055	        conn.commit()
1056	
1057	        syms = [r[0] for r in conn.execute(
1058	            "SELECT a.symbol FROM assets a JOIN stats s ON s.symbol=a.symbol "
1059	            "WHERE a.status='active' AND a.tradable=1 "
1060	            "AND LOWER(COALESCE(a.asset_class,'')) = 'stock' "
1061	            "AND COALESCE(a.marginable,0) = 1 "
1062	            "AND COALESCE(a.shortable,0) = 1 "
1063	            "AND COALESCE(s.volume,0) >= 100000 "
1064	            "AND COALESCE(s.price,0) > 0").fetchall()]
1065	
1066	        if len(syms) < TARGET_STOCKS * 2:
1067	            logger.warning(f"[{market}] too few marginable+shortable symbols ({len(syms)})")
1068	            return 0
1069	
1070	        wa_rows = conn.execute(
1071	            f"SELECT symbol, weighted_alpha, price FROM stats WHERE symbol IN ({','.join('?' * len(syms))})",
1072	            syms).fetchall()
1073	        wa_map = {r[0]: float(r[1] or 0) for r in wa_rows}
1074	        price_map = {r[0]: float(r[2] or 0) for r in wa_rows}
1075	
1076	        frac_rows = conn.execute(
1077	            f"SELECT symbol, fractionable FROM assets WHERE symbol IN ({','.join('?' * len(syms))})",
1078	            syms).fetchall()
1079	        frac_map = {r[0]: bool(r[1]) for r in frac_rows}
1080	
1081	        scored = [(s, wa_map.get(s, 0)) for s in syms if price_map.get(s, 0) > 0]
1082	        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
1083	
1084	        half = len(scored) // 2
1085	        long_pool = scored[:half]
1086	        short_pool = scored[half:]
1087	
1088	        rng = np.random.default_rng(20240710)
1089	        start = conn.execute(
1090	            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(CAST(SUBSTR(string_id,3) AS INTEGER)),0) "
1091	            "FROM string_universe WHERE string_id LIKE 'LS%'").fetchone()[0]
1092	        count = 0
1093	        batch_univ = []
1094	        batch_cons = []
1095	
1096	        long_per_stock = (ALLOCATION * LONG_PCT) / LONG_N
1097	        short_per_stock = (ALLOCATION * SHORT_PCT) / SHORT_N
1098	
1099	        for i in range(start + 1, start + 1 + n):
1100	            long_picked = [long_pool[j] for j in rng.choice(len(long_pool), size=LONG_N, replace=False)]
1101	            short_picked = [short_pool[j] for j in rng.choice(len(short_pool), size=SHORT_N, replace=False)]
1102	
1103	            weights = np.zeros(TARGET_STOCKS)
1104	            picked = []
1105	            for j, (sym, _) in enumerate(long_picked):
1106	                picked.append(sym)
1107	                price = price_map[sym]
1108	                raw_w = long_per_stock / price
1109	                weights[j] = round(raw_w, 4)
1110	            for j, (sym, _) in enumerate(short_picked):
1111	                picked.append(sym)
1112	                price = price_map[sym]
1113	                raw_w = short_per_stock / price
1114	                weights[LONG_N + j] = -round(raw_w, 4)
1115	
1116	            weights = np.where(weights == 0, 0.001, weights)
1117	            sid = f"LS{i:06d}"
1118	            long_part = " + ".join(f"{picked[j]}*{weights[j]:g}" for j in range(LONG_N))
1119	            short_part = " + ".join(f"{picked[j]}*{weights[j]:g}" for j in range(LONG_N, TARGET_STOCKS))
1120	            expr = f"LONG({long_part}) SHORT({short_part})"
1121	            batch_univ.append((sid, market, TARGET_STOCKS, expr, datetime.utcnow().isoformat(), 1))
1122	            for j in range(TARGET_STOCKS):
1123	                batch_cons.append((sid, str(picked[j]), float(weights[j])))
1124	            count += 1
1125	            if len(batch_univ) >= 500:
1126	                conn.executemany(
1127	                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_universe "
1128	                    "(string_id, market, num_stocks, expression, created_at, active) "
1129	                    "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)", batch_univ)
1130	                conn.executemany(
1131	                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_constituents (string_id, symbol, weight) "
1132	                    "VALUES (?,?,?)", batch_cons)
1133	                conn.commit()
1134	                batch_univ.clear(); batch_cons.clear()
1135	        if batch_univ:
1136	            conn.executemany(
1137	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_universe "
1138	                "(string_id, market, num_stocks, expression, created_at, active) "
1139	                "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)", batch_univ)
1140	            conn.executemany(
1141	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_constituents (string_id, symbol, weight) "
1142	                "VALUES (?,?,?)", batch_cons)
1143	            conn.commit()
1144	        logger.info(f"[{market}] generated {count} long+short strings")
1145	        return count
1146	    finally:
1147	        conn.close()
1148

1150	def update_historical_string_screener(market="US", only_strings=None, force_rebuild=False,
1151	                                       date_limit=None, progress_callback=None, only_latest=False,
1152	                                       string_id_like=None):
1153	    """Compute historical_string_screener for all dates.
1154	    Uses .npy cache + gather+einsum + single-query metrics loading.
1155	    If only_latest=True, loads full history for indicator computation but only inserts the latest date.
1156	    """
1157	    t0 = time.time()

1165	        mask = [fnmatch.fnmatch(s, string_id_like) for s in sids]

1167	        sids = [s for s, m in zip(sids, mask) if m]
1168	        indices = indices[keep]
1169	        weights = weights[keep]
1170	        if not sids:
1171	            logger.info(f"[{market}] no strings matching {string_id_like}")
1172	            return 0
1173	        logger.info(f"[{market}] filtered to {len(sids)} strings matching {string_id_like}")
1174

1178	        logger.warning(f"[{market}] no dates in close_pivot")

1275	        ai_concl_full = np.where(ai_m_full > 60, "BUY", np.where(ai_m_full < 40, "SELL", "HOLD"))
1276	        ai_bias_full = np.where(ai_m_full > 55, "bullish", np.where(ai_m_full < 45, "bearish", "neutral"))

1368	        return total_rows

1373	HIST_TEXT_COLS = {"ai_bias", "ai_conclusion"}
1374	
1375	
1376	def _load_hist_metrics_chunk(conn, market, sym_list, chunk_dates, W=None, Wn=None, indices=None, weights=None):
1377	    """Load ALL metric columns in ONE query (batched by 500 dates for SQLite limit), project via gather+einsum.
1378	    Optimized: uses pd.read_sql_query for bulk load instead of per-row Python loops."""
1379	    import pandas as pd
1380	    num_cols = [c for c in HIST_COLS if c not in HIST_TEXT_COLS]
1381	    cols_sql = ", ".join(num_cols)
1382	    n_sym = len(sym_list)
1383	    n_dates = len(chunk_dates)
1384	    raw = {c: np.zeros((n_sym, n_dates), dtype=np.float32) for c in num_cols}
1385	    date_idx = {d: i for i, d in enumerate(chunk_dates)}
1386	    sym_idx = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(sym_list)}
1387	
1388	    sql_batch = 500
1389	    sym_batch = 400  # SQLite 999-variable limit: 400 symbols + 2 dates = 402 params
1390	    all_dfs = []
1391	    for sb in range(0, len(chunk_dates), sql_batch):
1392	        batch_dates = chunk_dates[sb:sb+sql_batch]
1393	        min_date = batch_dates[0]
1394	        max_date = batch_dates[-1]
1395	        for si in range(0, len(sym_list), sym_batch):
1396	            batch_syms = sym_list[si:si+sym_batch]
1397	            placeholders = ",".join(["?"] * len(batch_syms))
1398	            q = (f"SELECT symbol, date, {cols_sql} FROM historical_screener "
1399	                 f"WHERE date >= ? AND date <= ? AND symbol IN ({placeholders})")
1400	            df = pd.read_sql_query(q, conn, params=[min_date, max_date] + list(batch_syms))
1401	            all_dfs.append(df)
1402	            del df
1403	
1404	    if not all_dfs:
1405	        out = {c: np.zeros((n_sym, n_dates), dtype=np.float32) for c in num_cols}
1406	        return out
1407	
1408	    rows_df = pd.concat(all_dfs, ignore_index=True)
1409	    del all_dfs
1410	
1411	    si = rows_df["symbol"].map(sym_idx).fillna(-1).astype(np.int32).values
1412	    di = rows_df["date"].map(date_idx).fillna(-1).astype(np.int32).values
1413	    valid = (si >= 0) & (di >= 0)
1414	    si_v = si[valid]
1415	    di_v = di[valid]
1416	
1417	    for col in num_cols:
1418	        if col in rows_df.columns:
1419	            vals = rows_df[col].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32, na_value=np.nan)
1420	        else:
1421	            vals = np.zeros(len(rows_df), dtype=np.float32)
1422	        vals_v = vals[valid]
1423	        mask = np.isfinite(vals_v)
1424	        raw[col][si_v[mask], di_v[mask]] = vals_v[mask]
1425	
1426	    del rows_df
1427	
1428	    out = {}
1429	    if indices is not None and weights is not None:
1430	        weight_sums = weights.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
1431	        weight_sums[weight_sums == 0] = 1.0
1432	        Wn_local = weights / weight_sums
1433	        for col in num_cols:
1434	            vals = raw[col]
1435	            gathered = vals[indices]
1436	            if col == "volume":
1437	                out[col] = np.einsum('bij,bi->bj', gathered, weights)
1438	            else:
1439	                out[col] = np.einsum('bij,bi->bj', gathered, Wn_local)
1440	    elif W is not None:
1441	        weight_sums = np.array(W.sum(axis=1)).ravel().astype(np.float32)
1442	        weight_sums[weight_sums == 0] = 1.0
1443	        Wn_mat = W / weight_sums[:, None]
1444	        for col in num_cols:
1445	            vals = raw[col]
1446	            if col == "volume":
1447	                out[col] = W @ vals
1448	            else:
1449	                out[col] = Wn_mat @ vals
1450	    return out
1451	
1452	
1453	def get_string_screener(market="US", page=1, per_page=50, sort="weighted_alpha", sort_dir="desc",
1454	                        search="", exchange="", asset_type="", date_cutoff="", args=None,
1455	                        string_id_like=None):
1456	    args = args or {}
1457	    conn = get_db(market)
1458	    try:
1459	        if date_cutoff:
1460	            where = ["h.date = ?", "h.string_id IN (SELECT string_id FROM string_universe WHERE market=?)"]
1461	            params = [date_cutoff, market]
1462	            if string_id_like:
1463	                where.append(f"h.string_id LIKE ?"); params.append(string_id_like)
1464	            if search:
1465	                where.append("(h.string_id LIKE ? OR h.name LIKE ?)")
1466	                params.extend([f"%{search}%", f"%{search}%"])
1467	            if exchange:
1468	                where.append("'BASKET' = ?"); params.append(exchange)
1469	            if asset_type:
1470	                where.append("'basket' = ?"); params.append(asset_type)
1471	            _apply_numeric_filters(where, params, args, "h.")
1472	            _apply_signal_filters(where, params, args, "h.")
1473	            where_str = " AND ".join(where)
1474	            total = conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM historical_string_screener h WHERE {where_str}", 
params).fetchone()[0]
1475	            order_col = _map_sort(sort, "h.")
1476	            direction = "DESC" if sort_dir == "desc" else "ASC"
1477	            rows = conn.execute(
1478	                f"SELECT h.string_id as symbol, h.name, 'BASKET' as exchange, 'basket' as asset_class, h.price, 
h.change_pct, "
1479	                f"h.volume, h.weighted_alpha, h.atrp, h.streak, h.atr_signal, h.atr_stop, h.atr_value, "
1480	                f"h.atr_streak, h.atr_crossed_above, h.atr_crossed_below, h.atr_multiplier, "
1481	                f"h.next_day_return, h.next_5d_return, h.prob_up_1d, h.prob_up_5d, h.pre_price, h.pre_change_pct, 
"
1482	                f"h.post_price, h.post_change_pct, h.accel_a, h.accel_base, h.accel_signal, "
1483	                f"h.accel_crossed_up, h.accel_crossed_down, h.accel_streak, h.confluence, "
1484	                f"h.ai_overall_score, h.ai_bias, h.ai_tech_score, h.ai_momentum_score, "
1485	                f"h.ai_volume_score, h.ai_events_score, h.ai_volume_profile_score, "
1486	                f"h.ai_trendline_score, h.ai_sentiment_score, h.ai_conclusion, h.ai_matrix, "
1487	                f"h.date as last_updated "
1488	                f"FROM historical_string_screener h WHERE {where_str} ORDER BY {order_col} {direction} "
1489	                f"LIMIT ? OFFSET ?", params + [per_page, (page - 1) * per_page]).fetchall()
1490	            data = [dict(r) for r in rows]
1491	            return {"data": data, "total": total, "page": page, "per_page": per_page,
1492	                    "total_pages": (total + per_page - 1) // per_page, "historical": True, "date": date_cutoff}
1493	        else:
1494	            where = ["m.market = ?"]
1495	            params = [market]
1496	            if string_id_like:
1497	                where.append(f"m.string_id LIKE ?"); params.append(string_id_like)
1498	            if search:
1499	                where.append("(m.string_id LIKE ? OR m.name LIKE ?)")
1500	                params.extend([f"%{search}%", f"%{search}%"])
1501	            if exchange:
1502	                where.append("m.exchange = ?"); params.append(exchange)
1503	            if asset_type:
1504	                where.append("m.asset_class = ?"); params.append(asset_type)
1505	            _apply_numeric_filters(where, params, args, "m.")
1506	            _apply_signal_filters(where, params, args, "m.")
1507	            where_str = " AND ".join(where)
1508	            total = conn.execute(
1509	                f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM string_screener_metrics m WHERE {where_str}", params).fetchone()[0]
1510	            order_col = _map_sort(sort, "m.")
1511	            direction = "DESC" if sort_dir == "desc" else "ASC"
1512	            rows = conn.execute(
1513	                f"SELECT m.string_id as symbol, m.name, m.exchange, m.asset_class, m.price, m.change_pct, "
1514	                f"m.volume, m.weighted_alpha, m.atrp, m.streak, m.atr_signal, m.atr_stop, m.atr_value, "
1515	                f"m.atr_streak, m.atr_crossed_above, m.atr_crossed_below, m.atr_multiplier, "
1516	                f"m.next_day_return, m.next_5d_return, m.prob_up_1d, m.prob_up_5d, m.pre_price, m.pre_change_pct, 
"
1517	                f"m.post_price, m.post_change_pct, m.accel_a, m.accel_base, m.accel_signal, "
1518	                f"m.accel_crossed_up, m.accel_crossed_down, m.accel_streak, m.confluence, "
1519	                f"m.ai_overall_score, m.ai_bias, m.ai_tech_score, m.ai_momentum_score, "
1520	                f"m.ai_volume_score, m.ai_events_score, m.ai_volume_profile_score, "
1521	                f"m.ai_trendline_score, m.ai_sentiment_score, m.ai_conclusion, m.ai_matrix, "
1522	                f"m.updated_at as last_updated "
1523	                f"FROM string_screener_metrics m WHERE {where_str} ORDER BY {order_col} {direction} "
1524	                f"LIMIT ? OFFSET ?", params + [per_page, (page - 1) * per_page]).fetchall()
1525	            data = [dict(r) for r in rows]
1526	            return {"data": data, "total": total, "page": page, "per_page": per_page,
1527	                    "total_pages": (total + per_page - 1) // per_page, "historical": False}
1528	    finally:
1529	        conn.close()
1530	
1531	
1532	def _apply_numeric_filters(where, params, args, pfx):
1533	    m = {
1534	        "min_price": "price", "max_price": "price", "min_wa": "weighted_alpha",
1535	        "max_wa": "weighted_alpha", "min_change": "change_pct", "max_change": "change_pct",
1536	        "min_streak": "streak", "min_volume": "volume",
1537	    }
1538	    for arg, col in m.items():
1539	        v = args.get(arg)
1540	        if v in (None, ""):
1541	            continue
1542	        v = float(v)
1543	        if arg.startswith("min"):
1544	            where.append(f"{pfx}{col} >= ?"); params.append(v)
1545	        else:
1546	            where.append(f"{pfx}{col} <= ?"); params.append(v)
1547	
1548	
1549	def _apply_signal_filters(where, params, args, pfx):
1550	    atr = args.get("atr_status", "")
1551	    if atr == "above":
1552	        where.append(f"{pfx}atr_signal = 1")
1553	    elif atr == "below":
1554	        where.append(f"{pfx}atr_signal = -1")
1555	    elif atr == "crossed-above":
1556	        where.append(f"{pfx}atr_crossed_above = 1")
1557	    elif atr == "crossed-below":
1558	        where.append(f"{pfx}atr_crossed_below = 1")
1559	    accel = args.get("accel_status", "")
1560	    if accel == "up":
1561	        where.append(f"{pfx}accel_signal = 1")
1562	    elif accel == "down":
1563	        where.append(f"{pfx}accel_signal = -1")
1564	    elif accel == "crossed-up":
1565	        where.append(f"{pfx}accel_crossed_up = 1")
1566	    elif accel == "crossed-down":
1567	        where.append(f"{pfx}accel_crossed_down = 1")
1568	
1569	
1570	def _map_sort(sort, pfx):
1571	    allowed = {"symbol", "name", "price", "change_pct", "weighted_alpha", "volume", "streak",
1572	               "atr_signal", "atr_stop", "atr_value", "atr_streak", "atrp",
1573	               "atr_crossed_above", "atr_crossed_below", "prob_up_1d", "prob_up_5d",
1574	               "next_day_return", "next_5d_return", "confluence", "accel_a", "accel_base",
1575	               "accel_signal", "accel_streak", "accel_crossed_up", "accel_crossed_down",
1576	               "ai_overall_score", "ai_bias", "ai_tech_score", "ai_volume_profile_score",
1577	               "ai_trendline_score", "ai_sentiment_score", "ai_conclusion", "ai_matrix"}
1578	    if sort not in allowed:
1579	        sort = "weighted_alpha"
1580	    if sort == "symbol":
1581	        sort = "string_id"
1582	    return f"{pfx}{sort}"
1583

1585	def get_string_detail(string_id, market="US"):
1586	    conn = get_db(market)
1587	    try:
1588	        univ = conn.execute(
1589	            "SELECT string_id, market, num_stocks, expression, created_at FROM string_universe WHERE string_id=?",
1590	            (string_id,)).fetchone()
1591	        if not univ:
1592	            return None
1593	        cons = conn.execute(
1594	            "SELECT symbol, weight FROM string_constituents WHERE string_id=?", (string_id,)).fetchall()
1595	        metrics = conn.execute(
1596	            "SELECT * FROM string_screener_metrics WHERE string_id=?", (string_id,)).fetchone()
1597	        symbols = [c[0] for c in cons]
1598	        contrib = []
1599	        if symbols:
1600	            placeholders = ",".join("?" * len(symbols))
1601	            rows = conn.execute(
1602	                f"SELECT s.symbol, s.price, s.change_pct, s.weighted_alpha, s.atr_signal, s.prob_up_1d, 
a.ai_matrix "
1603	                f"FROM stats s LEFT JOIN ai_analysis a ON s.symbol=a.symbol "
1604	                f"WHERE s.symbol IN ({placeholders})", symbols).fetchall()
1605	            smap = {r[0]: r for r in rows}
1606	            for sym, w in cons:
1607	                r = smap.get(sym)
1608	                if r:
1609	                    contrib.append({"symbol": sym, "weight": w, "price": r[1], "change_pct": r[2],
1610	                                    "weighted_alpha": r[3], "atr_signal": r[4], "prob_up_1d": r[5],
1611	                                    "ai_matrix": r[6]})
1612	        hist = conn.execute(
1613	            "SELECT date, price, change_pct, ai_matrix, atr_signal, weighted_alpha "
1614	            "FROM historical_string_screener WHERE string_id=? ORDER BY date", (string_id,)).fetchall()
1615	        return {
1616	            "universe": dict(univ),
1617	            "constituents": [{"symbol": c[0], "weight": c[1]} for c in cons],
1618	            "metrics": dict(metrics) if metrics else {},
1619	            "contributions": contrib,
1620	            "history": [dict(r) for r in hist],
1621	        }
1622	    finally:
1623	        conn.close()
1624

1696	def build_leaderboard_cache(market="US"):
1697	    """Build pre-aggregated leaderboard cache from historical_string_screener.
1698	    Called during refresh so the leaderboard API can read from a small cache table."""
1699	    t0 = time.time()
1700	    conn = get_db(market)
1701	    try:
1702	        exists = conn.execute(
1703	            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='string_leaderboard_cache'"
1704	        ).fetchone()
1705	        if not exists:
1706	            conn.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS string_leaderboard_cache (
1707	                string_id TEXT, market TEXT, expression TEXT, n_dates INTEGER,
1708	                win_rate REAL, avg_return REAL, total_return REAL, sharpe REAL,
1709	                best_day REAL, worst_day REAL, avg_ai REAL,
1710	                PRIMARY KEY (string_id, market))""")
1711	            conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_slc_market ON string_leaderboard_cache(market)")
1712	            conn.commit()
1713	
1714	        hist_exists = conn.execute(
1715	            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='historical_string_screener'"
1716	        ).fetchone()
1717	        if not hist_exists:
1718	            logger.info(f"[{market}] no historical_string_screener, skipping leaderboard cache")
1719	            return 0
1720	
1721	        rows = conn.execute(f"""
1722	            SELECT string_id,
1723	                COUNT(*) as n_dates,
1724	                ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN next_day_return > 0 THEN 100.0 ELSE 0 END), 2) as win_pct,
1725	                ROUND(AVG(next_day_return), 4) as avg_ret,
1726	                ROUND(MAX(next_day_return), 2) as best_day,
1727	                ROUND(MIN(next_day_return), 2) as worst_day,
1728	                ROUND(AVG(CAST(ai_matrix AS REAL)), 1) as avg_ai,
1729	                MIN(price) as first_price,
1730	                MAX(price) as last_price
1731	            FROM historical_string_screener
1732	            WHERE next_day_return IS NOT NULL
1733	            GROUP BY string_id
1734	            HAVING COUNT(*) >= 20
1735	        """).fetchall()
1736	
1737	        univ = conn.execute(
1738	            "SELECT string_id, expression FROM string_universe WHERE market=?", (market,)
1739	        ).fetchall()
1740	        expr_map = {r[0]: r[1] for r in univ}
1741	
1742	        conn.execute("DELETE FROM string_leaderboard_cache WHERE market=?", (market,))
1743	        batch = []
1744	        for r in rows:
1745	            sid = r[0]
1746	            n_dates = r[1]
1747	            win_pct = r[2] or 0
1748	            avg_ret = r[3] or 0
1749	            best_day = r[4] or 0
1750	            worst_day = r[5] or 0
1751	            avg_ai = r[6] or 50
1752	            fp = float(r[7]) if r[7] else 0
1753	            lp = float(r[8]) if r[8] else 0
1754	            total_ret = round(((lp / fp - 1) * 100), 2) if fp > 0 else 0
1755	            sharpe = round(avg_ret * (win_pct / 50.0), 2)
1756	            expr = expr_map.get(sid, sid)
1757	            short_expr = expr[:60] + ("..." if len(expr) > 60 else "")
1758	            batch.append((sid, market, short_expr, n_dates, win_pct, avg_ret,
1759	                          total_ret, sharpe, best_day, worst_day, avg_ai))
1760	
1761	        if batch:
1762	            conn.executemany(
1763	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_leaderboard_cache "
1764	                "(string_id, market, expression, n_dates, win_rate, avg_return, "
1765	                "total_return, sharpe, best_day, worst_day, avg_ai) "
1766	                "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)", batch)
1767	            conn.commit()
1768	
1769	        logger.info(f"[{market}] leaderboard cache built: {len(batch)} strings in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s")
1770	        return len(batch)
1771	    finally:
1772	        conn.close()

13	_ss_state = {"status": "idle", "phase": "", "progress": "", "cancel_requested": False}
14	_lock = threading.Lock()

17	def get_ss_status():
18	    with _lock:
19	        return dict(_ss_state)
