22	def generate_string_strategies(market="US"):
23	    """Generate thousands of string strategies from cartesian product of filter/sort/params."""
24	    conn = get_db(market)
25	    try:
26	        stats = pd.read_sql("SELECT * FROM stats WHERE price > 0 AND (asset_class IS NULL OR 
LOWER(asset_class)='stock')", conn)
27	        if stats.empty:
28	            return []
29	
30	        hs_cols_list = ["symbol", "date", "next_day_return", "atr_signal", "atr_streak", "weighted_alpha", "streak"]
31	        existing_cols = {row[1] for row in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(historical_screener)").fetchall()}
32	        if "confluence" in existing_cols:
33	            hs_cols_list.append("confluence")
34	        hs_cols = ", ".join(hs_cols_list)
35	        hs_cache = pd.read_sql(
36	            f"SELECT {hs_cols} FROM historical_screener ORDER BY symbol, date", conn
37	        )
38	        if "confluence" not in hs_cache.columns:
39	            hs_cache["confluence"] = 0
40	
41	        stats_prepared, hs_pivot = preprocess_for_combos(stats, hs_cache)
42	
43	        strategies = []
44	        filter_combos = [
45	            {"name": "ST_Cross_Up", "atr_signal": 1},
46	            {"name": "ST_Cross_Down_ReversalWatch", "atr_signal": -1, "accel_crossed_up": 1},
47	            {"name": "ST_Uptrend", "atr_streak_gt": 0},
48	            {"name": "ST_StrongUptrend", "atr_streak_gt": 5},
49	            {"name": "Accel_Cross_Up", "accel_crossed_up": 1},
50	            {"name": "Accel_Up", "accel_signal": 1},
51	            {"name": "WA_Above50", "min_wa": 50},
52	            {"name": "WA_Above20", "min_wa": 20},
53	            {"name": "WA_Above0", "min_wa": 0},
54	            {"name": "WA_Rebound", "min_wa": -10, "accel_signal": 1},
55	            {"name": "Prob_High", "min_prob_1d": 55},
56	            {"name": "Prob_VeryHigh", "min_prob_1d": 60},
57	            {"name": "ST_Up_Accel_Up", "atr_signal": 1, "accel_signal": 1},
58	            {"name": "ST_Cross_Accel_Cross", "atr_signal": 1, "accel_crossed_up": 1},
59	            {"name": "High_Confluence", "min_confluence": 60},
60	            {"name": "VeryHigh_Confluence", "min_confluence": 75},
61	            {"name": "Momentum_Streak", "min_streak": 3},
62	            {"name": "Fresh_Momentum", "min_streak": 1, "accel_crossed_up": 1},
63	            {"name": "ST_Cross_HighWA", "atr_signal": 1, "min_wa": 20},
64	            {"name": "All_Signals", "atr_signal": 1, "accel_signal": 1, "min_wa": 10},
65	            {"name": "Accel_Cross_HighWA", "accel_crossed_up": 1, "min_wa": 20},
66	        ]
67	        sort_fields = ["weighted_alpha", "prob_up_1d", "prob_up_5d", "confluence", "streak", "ai_overall_score", 
"change_pct", "volume"]
68	        sort_dirs = ["desc", "asc"]
69	        top_ns = [3, 5, 8, 10, 15, 20, 30, 50]
70	        st_params = [(7, 1.0), (10, 1.0), (10, 2.0), (14, 1.0), (14, 1.5), (14, 2.0), (21, 1.0), (21, 2.0)]
71	
72	        import itertools
73	        combos = list(itertools.product(filter_combos, sort_fields, sort_dirs, top_ns, st_params))
74	        total_combos = len(combos)
75	
76	        for idx, (filt, sort_f, sort_dir, top_n, (st_p, st_m)) in enumerate(combos):
77	            if _ss_state.get("cancel_requested"):
78	                break
79	
80	            _ss_state["phase"] = f"Combo {idx+1}/{total_combos}..."
81	            name = f"{filt['name']}_{sort_f}_{sort_dir}_top{top_n}_ST{st_p}x{st_m}"
82	
83	            filtered = _apply_string_rules(stats_prepared, filt, sort_f, top_n, sort_dir)
84	            if not filtered:
85	                continue
86	
87	            metrics = _backtest_string(filtered, hs_pivot, st_p, st_m)
88	
89	            strategies.append({
90	                "name": name,
91	                "strategy_type": "string_screener",
92	                "filter_json": json.dumps(filt),
93	                "sort_field": sort_f,
94	                "sort_dir": sort_dir,
95	                "top_n": top_n,
96	                "st_period": st_p,
97	                "st_multiplier": st_m,
98	                "total_entries": metrics["total_entries"],
99	                "win_rate": metrics["win_rate"],
100	                "avg_1d_return": metrics["avg_1d_return"],
101	                "median_1d_return": metrics["median_1d_return"],
102	                "std_1d_return": metrics["std_1d_return"],
103	                "max_1d_gain": metrics["max_1d_gain"],
104	                "max_1d_loss": metrics["max_1d_loss"],
105	                "max_consecutive_wins": metrics["max_consecutive_wins"],
106	                "max_consecutive_losses": metrics["max_consecutive_losses"],
107	                "sharpe_1d": metrics["sharpe_1d"],
108	                "prob_up_1d": metrics["prob_up_1d"],
109	                "prob_up_1pct": metrics["prob_up_1pct"],
110	                "prob_up_2pct": metrics["prob_up_2pct"],
111	                "prob_down_2pct": metrics["prob_down_2pct"],
112	                "prob_up_after_st_cross": metrics["prob_up_after_st_cross"],
113	                "prob_up_after_wa_high": metrics["prob_up_after_wa_high"],
114	                "current_symbols": ",".join([r["symbol"] for r in filtered]),
115	                "current_count": len(filtered),
116	                "is_random": 0,
117	            })
118	
119	        for i in range(min(50, len(strategies))):
120	            random_syms = stats_prepared.sample(n=min(10, len(stats_prepared)))["symbol"].tolist()
121	            metrics = _backtest_string(
122	                [{"symbol": s} for s in random_syms],
123	                hs_pivot, 14, 1.0
124	            )
125	            strategies.append({
126	                "name": f"Random_Baseline_{i+1}",
127	                "strategy_type": "string_screener",
128	                "filter_json": json.dumps({"random": True}),
129	                "sort_field": "random",
130	                "sort_dir": "desc",
131	                "top_n": len(random_syms),
132	                "st_period": 14,
133	                "st_multiplier": 1.0,
134	                "total_entries": metrics["total_entries"],
135	                "win_rate": metrics["win_rate"],
136	                "avg_1d_return": metrics["avg_1d_return"],
137	                "median_1d_return": metrics["median_1d_return"],
138	                "std_1d_return": metrics["std_1d_return"],
139	                "max_1d_gain": metrics["max_1d_gain"],
140	                "max_1d_loss": metrics["max_1d_loss"],
141	                "max_consecutive_wins": metrics["max_consecutive_wins"],
142	                "max_consecutive_losses": metrics["max_consecutive_losses"],
143	                "sharpe_1d": metrics["sharpe_1d"],
144	                "prob_up_1d": metrics["prob_up_1d"],
145	                "prob_up_1pct": metrics["prob_up_1pct"],
146	                "prob_up_2pct": metrics["prob_up_2pct"],
147	                "prob_down_2pct": metrics["prob_down_2pct"],
148	                "prob_up_after_st_cross": 50,
149	                "prob_up_after_wa_high": 50,
150	                "current_symbols": ",".join(random_syms),
151	                "current_count": len(random_syms),
152	                "is_random": 1,
153	            })
154	
155	        return strategies
156	    finally:
157	        conn.close()
158	
159	
160	def preprocess_for_combos(stats_df, hs_cache):
161	    """Pre-fill NaN in stats_df and pivot hs_cache into (dates x symbols) matrix.
162	    Call ONCE before the combo loop. Returns (stats_prepared, hs_pivot)."""
163	    fill_cols = ["atr_streak", "weighted_alpha", "prob_up_1d", "streak", "confluence",
164	                 "atr_signal", "accel_crossed_up", "accel_signal"]
165	    stats_prepared = stats_df.copy()
166	    for col in fill_cols:
167	        if col in stats_prepared.columns:
168	            stats_prepared[col] = stats_prepared[col].fillna(0)
169	
170	    hs_pivot = hs_cache.pivot_table(
171	        index="date", columns="symbol", values="next_day_return", aggfunc="mean"
172	    )
173	    hs_pivot._sym_to_idx = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(hs_pivot.columns)}
174	    hs_pivot._matrix = hs_pivot.values.astype(np.float64)
175	
176	    return stats_prepared, hs_pivot
177	
178	
179	def _apply_string_rules(stats_prepared, rules, sort_field, top_n, sort_dir="desc"):
180	    """Filter stats using numpy boolean mask (no DataFrame copy), nlargest/nsmallest."""
181	    n = len(stats_prepared)
182	    mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
183	
184	    if "atr_signal" in rules:
185	        mask &= stats_prepared["atr_signal"].values == rules["atr_signal"]
186	    if "accel_crossed_up" in rules:
187	        mask &= stats_prepared["accel_crossed_up"].values == rules["accel_crossed_up"]
188	    if "accel_signal" in rules:
189	        mask &= stats_prepared["accel_signal"].values == rules["accel_signal"]
190	    if "atr_streak_gt" in rules:
191	        mask &= stats_prepared["atr_streak"].values > rules["atr_streak_gt"]
192	    if "min_wa" in rules:
193	        mask &= stats_prepared["weighted_alpha"].values >= rules["min_wa"]
194	    if "min_prob_1d" in rules:
195	        mask &= stats_prepared["prob_up_1d"].values >= rules["min_prob_1d"]
196	    if "min_streak" in rules:
197	        mask &= stats_prepared["streak"].values >= rules["min_streak"]
198	    if "min_confluence" in rules:
199	        mask &= stats_prepared["confluence"].values >= rules["min_confluence"]
200	
201	    filtered = stats_prepared.iloc[mask]
202	    if filtered.empty:
203	        return []
204	
205	    actual_n = min(top_n, len(filtered))
206	    if sort_field not in filtered.columns:
207	        return filtered.head(top_n).to_dict("records")
208	
209	    if sort_dir == "asc":
210	        result = filtered.nsmallest(actual_n, sort_field)
211	    else:
212	        result = filtered.nlargest(actual_n, sort_field)
213	
214	    return result.to_dict("records")
215	
216	
217	def _backtest_string(symbols_data, hs_pivot, st_period=14, st_mult=1.0):
218	    """Backtest using pre-pivoted matrix. O(1) column selection, vectorized numpy."""
219	    metrics = {
220	        "total_entries": 0, "win_rate": 0, "avg_1d_return": 0, "median_1d_return": 0,
221	        "std_1d_return": 0, "max_1d_gain": 0, "max_1d_loss": 0,
222	        "max_consecutive_wins": 0, "max_consecutive_losses": 0, "sharpe_1d": 0,
223	        "prob_up_1d": 0, "prob_up_1pct": 0, "prob_up_2pct": 0, "prob_down_2pct": 0,
224	        "prob_up_after_st_cross": 50, "prob_up_after_wa_high": 50
225	    }
226	    if not symbols_data:
227	        return metrics
228	
229	    if hasattr(hs_pivot, "_sym_to_idx"):
230	        sym_to_idx = hs_pivot._sym_to_idx
231	        matrix = hs_pivot._matrix
232	        col_indices = [sym_to_idx[s["symbol"]] for s in symbols_data if s["symbol"] in sym_to_idx]
233	        if not col_indices:
234	            return metrics
235	        sub = matrix[:, col_indices]
236	        valid_counts = np.sum(~np.isnan(sub), axis=1)
237	        min_count = len(col_indices) * 0.5
238	        with np.errstate(all="ignore"):
239	            daily_means = np.nanmean(sub, axis=1)
240	        keep = (valid_counts >= min_count) & ~np.isnan(daily_means)
241	        returns = daily_means[keep]
242	    else:
243	        syms = [s["symbol"] for s in symbols_data]
244	        basket_hs = hs_pivot[hs_pivot["symbol"].isin(syms)].copy()
245	        if basket_hs.empty:
246	            return metrics
247	        daily_returns = []
248	        dates = sorted(basket_hs["date"].unique())
249	        for date in dates:
250	            day_data = basket_hs[basket_hs["date"] == date]
251	            if len(day_data) < len(syms) * 0.5:
252	                continue
253	            avg_return = day_data["next_day_return"].mean()
254	            if not np.isnan(avg_return):
255	                daily_returns.append(avg_return)
256	        returns = np.array(daily_returns) if daily_returns else np.array([])
257	
258	    if len(returns) == 0:
259	        return metrics
260	
261	    n = len(returns)
262	    pos = returns > 0
263	    mean_r = float(returns.mean())
264	    std_r = float(returns.std())
265	
266	    metrics["total_entries"] = n
267	    metrics["win_rate"] = round(float(pos.sum() / n * 100), 2)
268	    metrics["avg_1d_return"] = round(mean_r, 4)
269	    metrics["median_1d_return"] = round(float(np.median(returns)), 4)
270	    metrics["std_1d_return"] = round(std_r, 4)
271	    metrics["max_1d_gain"] = round(float(returns.max()), 4)
272	    metrics["max_1d_loss"] = round(float(returns.min()), 4)
273	    metrics["sharpe_1d"] = round(float(mean_r / (std_r + 1e-10)), 4)
274	    metrics["prob_up_1d"] = round(float(pos.sum() / n * 100), 2)
275	    metrics["prob_up_1pct"] = round(float((returns > 1).sum() / n * 100), 2)
276	    metrics["prob_up_2pct"] = round(float((returns > 2).sum() / n * 100), 2)
277	    metrics["prob_down_2pct"] = round(float((returns < -2).sum() / n * 100), 2)
278	
279	    boundaries = np.diff(pos.astype(np.int8))
280	    change_idx = np.flatnonzero(boundaries)
281	    run_lengths = np.diff(np.concatenate(([-1], change_idx, [n - 1])))
282	    run_values = pos[np.concatenate(([0], change_idx + 1))]
283	    win_runs = run_lengths[run_values]
284	    loss_runs = run_lengths[~run_values]
285	    metrics["max_consecutive_wins"] = int(win_runs.max()) if len(win_runs) > 0 else 0
286	    metrics["max_consecutive_losses"] = int(loss_runs.max()) if len(loss_runs) > 0 else 0
287	
288	    return metrics
289	
290	
291	def run_string_backtest(market="US"):
292	    with _lock:
293	        if _ss_state["status"] == "running":
294	            return False
295	        _ss_state["status"] = "running"
296	        _ss_state["cancel_requested"] = False
297	        threading.Thread(target=_ss_worker, args=(market,), daemon=True).start()
298	    return True
299	
300	
301	def cancel_string_backtest():
302	    with _lock:
303	        _ss_state["cancel_requested"] = True
304	        _ss_state["status"] = "cancelling"
305	    return True
306	
307	
308	def _ss_worker(market):
309	    try:
310	        _ss_state["phase"] = "Generating strategies..."
311	        _ss_state["progress_pct"] = 0
312	        strategies = generate_string_strategies(market)
313	        _ss_state["phase"] = f"Saving {len(strategies)} strategies..."
314	
315	        if strategies:
316	            conn = get_db(market)
317	            try:
318	                conn.execute("DELETE FROM ss_strategies WHERE is_random=0")
319	                for s in strategies:
320	                    conn.execute(
321	                        """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO ss_strategies (
322	                            name, strategy_type, filter_json, sort_field, sort_dir, top_n,
323	                            st_period, st_multiplier, total_entries, win_rate, avg_1d_return,
324	                            median_1d_return, std_1d_return, max_1d_gain, max_1d_loss,
325	                            max_consecutive_wins, max_consecutive_losses, sharpe_1d,
326	                            prob_up_1d, prob_up_1pct, prob_up_2pct, prob_down_2pct,
327	                            prob_up_after_st_cross, prob_up_after_wa_high,
328	                            current_date, current_count, current_symbols, is_random
329	                        ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
330	                        (s["name"], s["strategy_type"], s["filter_json"], s["sort_field"],
331	                         s["sort_dir"], s["top_n"], s["st_period"], s["st_multiplier"],
332	                         s["total_entries"], s["win_rate"], s["avg_1d_return"],
333	                         s["median_1d_return"], s["std_1d_return"], s["max_1d_gain"],
334	                         s["max_1d_loss"], s["max_consecutive_wins"], s["max_consecutive_losses"],
335	                         s["sharpe_1d"], s["prob_up_1d"], s["prob_up_1pct"],
336	                         s["prob_up_2pct"], s["prob_down_2pct"],
337	                         s["prob_up_after_st_cross"], s["prob_up_after_wa_high"],
338	                         datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%d"), s["current_count"],
339	                         s["current_symbols"], s["is_random"])
340	                    )
341	                conn.commit()
342	            finally:
343	                conn.close()
344	
345	        _ss_state["status"] = "complete"
346	        _ss_state["phase"] = f"Done: {len(strategies)} strategies"
347	    except Exception as e:
348	        _ss_state["status"] = "error"
349	        _ss_state["phase"] = str(e)
350	        logger.error(f"String backtest error: {e}")
351	
352	
353	def get_strategies(market="US", page=1, per_page=50, sort="sharpe_1d", sort_dir="desc", search="",
354	                   min_win=None, min_prob=None, min_avg=None, min_entries=None, hide_random=False):
355	    conn = get_db(market)
356	    try:
357	        where = "1=1"
358	        params = []
359	        if search:
360	            where += " AND name LIKE ?"
361	            params.append(f"%{search}%")
362	        if min_win not in (None, ""):
363	            where += " AND COALESCE(win_rate, 0) >= ?"
364	            params.append(float(min_win))
365	        if min_prob not in (None, ""):
366	            where += " AND COALESCE(prob_up_1d, 0) >= ?"
367	            params.append(float(min_prob))
368	        if min_avg not in (None, ""):
369	            where += " AND COALESCE(avg_1d_return, 0) >= ?"
370	            params.append(float(min_avg))
371	        if min_entries not in (None, ""):
372	            where += " AND COALESCE(total_entries, 0) >= ?"
373	            params.append(int(min_entries))
374	        if hide_random:
375	            where += " AND COALESCE(is_random, 0) = 0"
376	
377	        total = conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ss_strategies WHERE {where}", params).fetchone()[0]
378	
379	        allowed_sorts = {"sharpe_1d", "win_rate", "avg_1d_return", "prob_up_1d", "total_entries",
380	                         "median_1d_return", "max_1d_gain", "max_consecutive_wins", "prob_up_1pct",
381	                         "prob_up_2pct", "prob_down_2pct", "current_count", "name"}
382	        if sort not in allowed_sorts:
383	            sort = "sharpe_1d"
384	        direction = "DESC" if sort_dir == "desc" else "ASC"
385	
386	        offset = (page - 1) * per_page
387	        rows = conn.execute(
388	            f"SELECT * FROM ss_strategies WHERE {where} ORDER BY {sort} {direction} LIMIT ? OFFSET ?",
389	            params + [per_page, offset]
390	        ).fetchall()
391	
392	        return {"strategies": [dict(r) for r in rows], "total": total, "page": page, "per_page": per_page}
393	    finally:
394	        conn.close()
395	
396	
397	def get_strategy_detail(strategy_id, market="US"):
398	    conn = get_db(market)
399	    try:
400	        strategy = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM ss_strategies WHERE id=?", (strategy_id,)).fetchone()
401	        entries = conn.execute(
402	            "SELECT * FROM ss_entries WHERE strategy_id=? ORDER BY date", (strategy_id,)
403	        ).fetchall()
404	        return {
405	            "strategy": dict(strategy) if strategy else None,
406	            "entries": [dict(e) for e in entries]
407	        }
408	    finally:
409	        conn.close()
410

412	def get_current_basket(strategy_id, market="US"):
413	    conn = get_db(market)
414	    try:
415	        strat = conn.execute("SELECT current_symbols FROM ss_strategies WHERE id=?", (strategy_id,)).fetchone()
416	        if not strat or not strat[0]:
417	            return []
418	        symbols = strat[0].split(",")
419	        rows = conn.execute(
420	            "SELECT symbol, price, change_pct, weighted_alpha, atr_signal, prob_up_1d, streak FROM stats WHERE 
symbol IN ({})".format(
421	                ",".join(["?"] * len(symbols))), symbols
422	        ).fetchall()
423	        return [dict(r) for r in rows]
424	    finally:
425	        conn.close()

337	                 "atrp": "h.atrp", "atr_crossed_above": "h.atr_crossed_above",
338	                 "atr_crossed_below": "h.atr_crossed_below",
339	                 "prob_up_1d": "h.prob_up_1d", "prob_up_5d": "h.prob_up_5d",
340	                 "next_day_return": "h.next_day_return", "next_5d_return": "h.next_5d_return",
341	                 "confluence": "h.confluence",
342	                 "accel_a": "h.accel_a", "accel_base": "h.accel_base",
343	                 "accel_signal": "h.accel_signal", "accel_streak": "h.accel_streak",
344	                 "accel_crossed_up": "h.accel_crossed_up", "accel_crossed_down": "h.accel_crossed_down",
345	                 "ai_overall_score": "h.ai_overall_score", "ai_matrix": "h.ai_matrix",
346	                 "ai_bias": "h.ai_bias", "ai_conclusion": "h.ai_conclusion",
347	                 "exchange": "a.exchange", "asset_class": "a.asset_class",
348	                 "marginable": "a.marginable", "fractionable": "a.fractionable"}
349	    sort_col = h_col_map.get(sort)
350	    if sort_col is None:
351	        sort_col = "h.weighted_alpha"
352	    # "<col> <dir> NULLS LAST" is row-identical to the old CASE-WHEN-NULL wrapper but
353	    # lets SQLite serve indexed sorts without a temp B-tree (verified on real DBs).
354	    order_clause = f"{sort_col} {direction} NULLS LAST"
355	
356	    total = conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) {count_from}", params).fetchone()[0]
357	    offset = (page - 1) * per_page
358	    rows = conn.execute(
359	        f"SELECT h.symbol, h.price, h.volume, NULL as open, NULL as high, NULL as low, "
360	        f"h.change_pct, h.weighted_alpha, h.atrp, h.streak, h.atr_value, h.atr_stop, h.atr_signal, "
361	        f"h.atr_crossed_above, h.atr_crossed_below, h.atr_streak, h.atr_multiplier, "
362	        f"h.prob_up_1d, h.prob_up_5d, h.next_day_return, h.confluence, "
363	        f"h.accel_a, h.accel_base, h.accel_signal, h.accel_crossed_up, h.accel_crossed_down, "
364	        f"s.profit_status, a.fractionable, a.marginable, NULL as pre_price, NULL as pre_change_pct, "
365	        f"NULL as post_price, NULL as post_change_pct, h.date as last_updated, "
366	        f"h.ai_overall_score, h.ai_bias, h.ai_tech_score, h.ai_momentum_score, "
367	        f"h.ai_volume_score, h.ai_events_score, h.ai_volume_profile_score, "
368	        f"h.ai_trendline_score, h.ai_sentiment_score, h.ai_conclusion, h.ai_matrix, "
369	        f"a.name, a.exchange, a.asset_class "
370	        f"{base_from} ORDER BY {order_clause} LIMIT ? OFFSET ?",
371	        params + [per_page, offset]
372	    ).fetchall()
373	
374	    data = [dict(r) for r in rows]
375	    for d in data:
376	        d.setdefault("ai_overall_score", 0)
377	        d.setdefault("ai_bias", "neutral")
378	        d.setdefault("ai_tech_score", 0)
379	        d.setdefault("ai_volume_profile_score", 0)
380	        d.setdefault("ai_trendline_score", 0)
381	        d.setdefault("ai_sentiment_score", 0)
382	        d.setdefault("ai_conclusion", "HOLD")
383	        d.setdefault("ai_matrix", "")
384	        d.setdefault("name", "")
385	        d.setdefault("exchange", "")
386	        d.setdefault("asset_class", "")
387	
388	    return {
389	        "data": data, "total": total, "page": page, "per_page": per_page,
390	        "total_pages": (total + per_page - 1) // per_page, "historical": True, "date": date_cutoff
391	    }

394	def _build_stats_query(conn, market, search, exchange, asset_type,
395	                        sort, sort_dir, page, per_page, args):
396	
397	    where = ["1=1"]
398	    params = []
399	
400	    if search:
401	        where.append("(s.symbol LIKE ? OR s.name LIKE ?)")
402	        params.extend([f"%{search}%", f"%{search}%"])
403	    if exchange:
404	        where.append("exchange = ?")
405	        params.append(exchange)
406	    if asset_type:
407	        where.append("asset_class = ?")
408	        params.append(asset_type)
409	
410	    min_wa = args.get("min_wa")
411	    max_wa = args.get("max_wa")
412	    if min_wa:
413	        where.append("weighted_alpha >= ?")
414	        params.append(float(min_wa))
415	    if max_wa:
416	        where.append("weighted_alpha <= ?")
417	        params.append(float(max_wa))
418	
419	    min_price = args.get("min_price")
420	    max_price = args.get("max_price")
421	    if min_price:
422	        where.append("price >= ?")
423	        params.append(float(min_price))
424	    if max_price:
425	        where.append("price <= ?")
426	        params.append(float(max_price))
427	
428	    min_change = args.get("min_change")
429	    max_change = args.get("max_change")
430	    if min_change:
431	        where.append("change_pct >= ?")
432	        params.append(float(min_change))
433	    if max_change:
434	        where.append("change_pct <= ?")
435	        params.append(float(max_change))
436	
437	    min_streak = args.get("min_streak")
438	    if min_streak:
439	        where.append("streak >= ?")
440	        params.append(int(min_streak))
441	
442	    min_volume = args.get("min_volume")
443	    if min_volume:
444	        where.append("volume >= ?")
445	        params.append(int(min_volume))
446	
447	    atr_status = args.get("atr_status")
448	    if atr_status:
449	        if atr_status == "above":
450	            where.append("atr_signal = 1")
451	        elif atr_status == "below":
452	            where.append("atr_signal = -1")
453	        elif atr_status == "crossed-above":
454	            where.append("atr_crossed_above = 1")
455	        elif atr_status == "crossed-below":
456	            where.append("atr_crossed_below = 1")
457	
458	    accel_status = args.get("accel_status")
459	    if accel_status:
460	        if accel_status == "up":
461	            where.append("accel_signal = 1")
462	        elif accel_status == "down":
463	            where.append("accel_signal = -1")
464	        elif accel_status == "crossed-up":
465	            where.append("accel_crossed_up = 1")
466	        elif accel_status == "crossed-down":
467	            where.append("accel_crossed_down = 1")
468	
469	    fractionable = args.get("fractionable")
470	    if fractionable:
471	        where.append("fractionable = ?")
472	        params.append(1 if fractionable == "yes" else 0)
473	
474	    profit_status = args.get("profit_status")
475	    if profit_status:
476	        where.append("profit_status = ?")
477	        params.append(profit_status)
478	
479	    where_str = " AND ".join(where)
480	
481	    # Filters only reference stats columns, so COUNT can skip the ai_analysis join
482	    # (LEFT JOIN on the unique ai_analysis.symbol key never changes the row count).
483	    if "a." in where_str:
484	        total = conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stats s LEFT JOIN ai_analysis a ON s.symbol=a.symbol WHERE 
{where_str}", params).fetchone()[0]
485	    else:
486	        total = conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stats s WHERE {where_str}", params).fetchone()[0]
487	
488	    allowed_sorts = {
489	        "symbol", "name", "price", "change_pct", "weighted_alpha", "volume", "streak",
490	        "atr_signal", "atr_stop", "atr_value", "atr_streak", "atrp",
491	        "atr_crossed_above", "atr_crossed_below",
492	        "prob_up_1d", "prob_up_5d",
493	        "next_day_return", "pre_price", "pre_change_pct", "post_price", "post_change_pct",
494	        "profit_status", "fractionable", "marginable", "asset_class", "exchange", "confluence",
495	        "accel_a", "accel_base", "accel_signal", "accel_streak", "accel_crossed_up", "accel_crossed_down",
496	        "ai_overall_score", "ai_bias", "ai_tech_score", "ai_volume_profile_score",
497	        "ai_trendline_score", "ai_sentiment_score", "ai_conclusion", "ai_matrix"
498	    }
499	    if sort not in allowed_sorts:
500	        sort = "weighted_alpha"
501	    direction = "DESC" if sort_dir == "desc" else "ASC"
502	
503	    ai_col_map = {
504	        "ai_overall_score": "a.overall_score",
505	        "ai_bias": "a.bias",
506	        "ai_tech_score": "a.tech_score",
507	        "ai_volume_profile_score": "a.volume_profile_score",
508	        "ai_trendline_score": "a.trendline_score",
509	        "ai_sentiment_score": "a.sentiment_score",
510	        "ai_conclusion": "a.conclusion",
511	        "ai_matrix": "a.ai_matrix",
512	    }
513	    sort_col = ai_col_map.get(sort) or f"s.{sort}"
514	
515	    # "<col> <dir> NULLS LAST" is row-identical to the old CASE-WHEN-NULL wrapper but
516	    # lets SQLite use the stats indexes (idx_stats_wa etc.) instead of a temp B-tree.
517	    order_clause = f"{sort_col} {direction} NULLS LAST"
518	
519	    offset = (page - 1) * per_page
520	    rows = conn.execute(
521	        f"SELECT s.symbol, s.name, s.price, s.volume, s.change_pct, "
522	        f"s.atrp, s.weighted_alpha, s.atr_signal, s.atr_stop, s.atr_value, s.atr_streak, "
523	        f"s.atr_crossed_above, s.atr_crossed_below, s.atr_multiplier, s.streak, "
524	        f"s.next_day_return, s.prob_up_1d, s.prob_up_5d, "
525	        f"s.pre_price, s.pre_change_pct, s.post_price, s.post_change_pct, "
526	        f"s.profit_status, s.fractionable, s.marginable, s.asset_class, s.exchange, "
527	        f"s.last_updated, s.oldest_data, s.accel_a, s.accel_base, s.accel_signal, "
528	        f"s.accel_crossed_up, s.accel_crossed_down, s.accel_streak, s.confluence, "
529	        f"a.overall_score as ai_overall_score, a.bias as ai_bias, "
530	        f"a.tech_score as ai_tech_score, a.momentum_score as ai_momentum_score, "
531	        f"a.volume_score as ai_volume_score, a.events_score as ai_events_score, "
532	        f"a.volume_profile_score as ai_volume_profile_score, "
533	        f"a.trendline_score as ai_trendline_score, a.sentiment_score as ai_sentiment_score, "
534	        f"a.conclusion as ai_conclusion, a.ai_matrix "
535	        f"FROM stats s LEFT JOIN ai_analysis a ON s.symbol=a.symbol "
536	        f"WHERE {where_str} ORDER BY {order_clause} LIMIT ? OFFSET ?",
537	        params + [per_page, offset]
538	    ).fetchall()
539	
540	    data = [dict(r) for r in rows]
541	    for d in data:
542	        d.setdefault("ai_overall_score", 0)
543	        d.setdefault("ai_bias", "neutral")
544	        d.setdefault("ai_tech_score", 0)
545	        d.setdefault("ai_volume_profile_score", 0)
546	        d.setdefault("ai_trendline_score", 0)
547	        d.setdefault("ai_sentiment_score", 0)
548	        d.setdefault("ai_conclusion", "HOLD")
549	        d.setdefault("ai_matrix", "")
550	    return {
551	        "data": data, "total": total, "page": page, "per_page": per_page,
552	        "total_pages": (total + per_page - 1) // per_page, "historical": False
553	    }
554

556	@api_bp.route("/stats")
557	def api_stats():
558	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
559	    symbol = request.args.get("symbol")
560	    conn = get_db(market)
561	    try:
562	        if symbol:
563	            row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM stats WHERE symbol=?", (symbol,)).fetchone()
564	            return jsonify(dict(row) if row else {})
565	        else:
566	            rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM stats LIMIT 500").fetchall()
567	            return jsonify([dict(r) for r in rows])
568	    finally:
569	        conn.close()

572	@api_bp.route("/market-breadth")
573	def api_market_breadth():
574	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
575	    conn = get_db(market)
576	    try:
577	        row = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM stats").fetchone()
578	        total = row["total"] if row else 0
579	        up = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM stats WHERE change_pct > 0").fetchone()["c"]
580	        down = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM stats WHERE change_pct < 0").fetchone()["c"]
581	        unchanged = total - up - down
582	        return jsonify({
583	            "total": total, "advancers": up, "decliners": down, "unchanged": unchanged,
584	            "pct_up": round(up / total * 100, 1) if total > 0 else 0
585	        })
586	    finally:
587	        conn.close()

592	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
593	    conn = get_db(market)

597	        ).fetchall()]
598	        by_volume = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute(
599	            "SELECT symbol, name, price, volume FROM stats ORDER BY volume DESC LIMIT 10"
600	        ).fetchall()]
601	        by_wa = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute(
602	            "SELECT symbol, name, price, weighted_alpha FROM stats ORDER BY weighted_alpha DESC LIMIT 10"
603	        ).fetchall()]
604	        return jsonify({"gainers": gainers, "by_volume": by_volume, "by_weighted_alpha": by_wa})
605	    finally:

620	@api_bp.route("/hs-dates")
621	def api_hs_dates():
622	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
623	    conn = get_db(market)
624	    try:
625	        # Recursive "loose index scan": one indexed MAX() seek per distinct date via
626	        # idx_bars_tf_date instead of scanning the whole multi-million-row index
627	        # (measured 410ms -> 38ms on US, 3.6s -> 26ms on India).
628	        rows = conn.execute(
629	            """
630	            WITH RECURSIVE d(dt) AS (
631	              SELECT (SELECT MAX(date) FROM bars WHERE timeframe='1Day')
632	              UNION ALL
633	              SELECT (SELECT MAX(date) FROM bars WHERE timeframe='1Day' AND date < d.dt)
634	              FROM d WHERE d.dt IS NOT NULL LIMIT 366
635	            )
636	            SELECT dt AS date FROM d WHERE dt IS NOT NULL LIMIT 365
637	            """
638	        ).fetchall()
639	        return jsonify([r["date"] for r in rows])
640	    finally:
--- dumbmoney/app.py:1680-2300
1680	    page = int(request.args.get("page", 1))
1681	    per_page = int(request.args.get("per_page", 50))
1682	    sort = request.args.get("sort", "weighted_alpha")
1683	    sort_dir = request.args.get("sort_dir") or request.args.get("order", "desc")
1684	    search = request.args.get("search", "")
1685	    exchange = request.args.get("exchange", "")
1686	    asset_type = request.args.get("asset_type", "")
1687	    date_cutoff = request.args.get("date_cutoff", "")
1688	    from dumbmoney.basket_screener import get_string_screener
1689	    result = get_string_screener(market, page, per_page, sort, sort_dir, search, exchange,
1690	                                 asset_type, date_cutoff, request.args)
1691	    return jsonify(result)

1710	def _basket_constituents(string_id, market):
1711	    conn = get_db(market)
1712	    try:
1713	        rows = conn.execute(
1714	            "SELECT symbol, weight FROM string_constituents WHERE string_id=?",
1715	            (string_id,)).fetchall()
1716	        return [(r[0], r[1]) for r in rows], conn
1717	    except Exception:
1718	        return [], conn

1721	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/<string_id>/ohlc")
1722	def api_basket_ohlc(string_id):
1723	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
1724	    limit = int(request.args.get("limit", 200))
1725	    timeframe = request.args.get("timeframe", "1D")
1726	    cons, conn = _basket_constituents(string_id, market)
1727	    try:
1728	        if not cons:
1729	            return jsonify([])
1730	        symbols = [c[0] for c in cons]
1731	        weights = {c[0]: c[1] for c in cons}
1732	        wsum = sum(weights.values()) or 1.0
1733	        weights = {k: v / wsum for k, v in weights.items()}
1734	        ohlc_df = _combined_ohlc_for_symbols(conn, symbols, weights=weights)
1735	        if ohlc_df is None:
1736	            return jsonify([])
1737	        ohlc_df = _resample_ohlc(ohlc_df, timeframe)
1738	        ohlc_df = ohlc_df.tail(limit)
1739	        return jsonify([dict(r) for _, r in ohlc_df.iterrows()])
1740	    finally:
1741	        conn.close()
1742

1744	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/<string_id>/supertrend")
1745	def api_basket_supertrend(string_id):
1746	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
1747	    period = int(request.args.get("period", 14))
1748	    multiplier = float(request.args.get("multiplier", 1.0))
1749	    timeframe = request.args.get("timeframe", "1D")
1750	    cons, conn = _basket_constituents(string_id, market)
1751	    try:
1752	        if not cons:
1753	            return jsonify([])
1754	        symbols = [c[0] for c in cons]
1755	        weights = {c[0]: c[1] for c in cons}
1756	        wsum = sum(weights.values()) or 1.0
1757	        weights = {k: v / wsum for k, v in weights.items()}
1758	        ohlc_df = _combined_ohlc_for_symbols(conn, symbols, weights=weights)
1759	        if ohlc_df is None or ohlc_df.empty:
1760	            return jsonify([])
1761	        ohlc_df = _resample_ohlc(ohlc_df, timeframe)
1762	        import pandas as pd
1763	        from dumbmoney.indicators import supertrend as compute_st
1764	        st = compute_st(ohlc_df, period=period, multiplier=multiplier)
1765	        result = []
1766	        for i, (_, row) in enumerate(st.iterrows()):
1767	            result.append({
1768	                "date": ohlc_df.iloc[i]["date"] if i < len(ohlc_df) else "",
1769	                "supertrend": round(float(row["supertrend"]), 4) if pd.notna(row["supertrend"]) else None,
1770	                "trend": int(row["trend"]),
1771	                "signal": int(row["signal"]),
1772	                "stop": round(float(row["stop"]), 4) if pd.notna(row["stop"]) else None,
1773	                "atr_value": round(float(row["atr_value"]), 4) if pd.notna(row["atr_value"]) else None,
1774	            })
1775	        return jsonify(result)
1776	    finally:
1777	        conn.close()

1801	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/<string_id>/stats")
1802	def api_basket_stats(string_id):
1803	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
1804	    cons, conn = _basket_constituents(string_id, market)
1805	    try:
1806	        if not cons:
1807	            return jsonify({})
1808	        symbols = [c[0] for c in cons]
1809	        weights = {c[0]: c[1] for c in cons}
1810	        wsum = sum(weights.values()) or 1.0
1811	        weights = {k: v / wsum for k, v in weights.items()}
1812	        ohlc_df = _combined_ohlc_for_symbols(conn, symbols, weights=weights)
1813	        if ohlc_df is None or ohlc_df.empty:
1814	            return jsonify({})
1815	        import pandas as pd
1816	        import numpy as np
1817	        c = ohlc_df["close"].astype(float)
1818	        returns = c.pct_change().dropna()
1819	        if len(returns) < 2:
1820	            return jsonify({})
1821	        wins = returns[returns > 0]
1822	        losses = returns[returns < 0]
1823	        win_rate = len(wins) / len(returns) * 100
1824	        avg_return = float(returns.mean()) * 100
1825	        avg_win = float(wins.mean()) * 100 if len(wins) > 0 else 0
1826	        avg_loss = float(losses.mean()) * 100 if len(losses) > 0 else 0
1827	        profit_factor = abs(float(wins.sum()) / float(losses.sum())) if len(losses) > 0 and losses.sum() != 0 
else 0
1828	        sharpe = float(returns.mean() / (returns.std() + 1e-10)) * np.sqrt(252)
1829	        sortino_denom = returns[returns < 0].std()
1830	        sortino = float(returns.mean() / (sortino_denom + 1e-10)) * np.sqrt(252) if sortino_denom > 0 else 0
1831	        cum = (1 + returns).cumprod()
1832	        running_max = cum.cummax()
1833	        drawdown = np.where(running_max > 0, (cum - running_max) / running_max, 0)
1834	        max_dd = float(np.min(drawdown)) * 100
1835	        best = float(returns.max()) * 100
1836	        worst = float(returns.min()) * 100
1837	        streak_w, streak_l = 0, 0
1838	        max_streak_w, max_streak_l = 0, 0
1839	        for r in returns:
1840	            if r > 0:
1841	                streak_w += 1; streak_l = 0
1842	                max_streak_w = max(max_streak_w, streak_w)
1843	            elif r < 0:
1844	                streak_l += 1; streak_w = 0
1845	                max_streak_l = max(max_streak_l, streak_l)
1846	            else:
1847	                streak_w = 0; streak_l = 0
1848	        total_days = len(returns)
1849	        cum_total = float((1 + returns).prod())
1850	        if cum_total > 0:
1851	            ann_ret = float((cum_total ** (252 / max(total_days, 1)) - 1)) * 100
1852	        else:
1853	            ann_ret = -100.0
1854	        calmar = ann_ret / abs(max_dd) if max_dd != 0 else 0
1855	
1856	        volatility = float(returns.std()) * np.sqrt(252) * 100
1857	
1858	        def _trend_view(window):
1859	            if len(returns) < window:
1860	                return {"return": 0, "win_rate": 50, "signal": "neutral", "strength": 0}
1861	            w = returns.tail(window)
1862	            cum_ret = float(((1 + w).prod() - 1) * 100)
1863	            wr = float((w > 0).mean() * 100)
1864	            avg_r = float(w.mean()) * 100
1865	            vol = float(w.std()) * np.sqrt(252) * 100 if len(w) > 1 else 1
1866	            score = 0
1867	            if cum_ret > 2: score += 1
1868	            elif cum_ret < -2: score -= 1
1869	            if wr > 55: score += 1
1870	            elif wr < 45: score -= 1
1871	            if avg_r > 0.1: score += 1
1872	            elif avg_r < -0.1: score -= 1
1873	            signal = "BUY" if score >= 2 else ("SELL" if score <= -2 else "HOLD")
1874	            return {"return": round(cum_ret, 2), "win_rate": round(wr, 1), "signal": signal, "strength": 
abs(score)}
1875	
1876	        short = _trend_view(5)
1877	        medium = _trend_view(20)
1878	        long_v = _trend_view(60)
1879	
1880	        monthly = {}
1881	        if "date" in ohlc_df.columns:
1882	            ohlc_df["date"] = pd.to_datetime(ohlc_df["date"])
1883	            ohlc_df["month"] = ohlc_df["date"].dt.to_period("M")
1884	            monthly_rets = ohlc_df.groupby("month")["close"].apply(lambda x: float((x.iloc[-1]/x.iloc[0]-1)*100) 
if len(x)>1 else 0)
1885	            best_month = monthly_rets.idxmax()
1886	            worst_month = monthly_rets.idxmin()
1887	            pos_months = int((monthly_rets > 0).sum())
1888	            total_months = len(monthly_rets)
1889	        else:
1890	            best_month = worst_month = None
1891	            pos_months = total_months = 0
1892	
1893	        insights = []
1894	        if win_rate > 55: insights.append(f"Strong win rate of {win_rate:.1f}% ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ consistent edge.")
1895	        elif win_rate < 45: insights.append(f"Win rate {win_rate:.1f}% below 50 ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ needs strong winners.")
1896	        if sharpe > 1.5: insights.append(f"Excellent risk-adjusted return (Sharpe {sharpe:.2f}).")
1897	        elif sharpe < 0: insights.append(f"Negative Sharpe ({sharpe:.2f}) ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ risk exceeds return.")
1898	        if profit_factor > 1.5: insights.append(f"Profit factor {profit_factor:.2f} ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ wins outsize 
losses.")
1899	        if max_dd < -20: insights.append(f"Max drawdown {max_dd:.1f}% ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ high risk profile.")
1900	        if volatility > 40: insights.append(f"High annualized volatility ({volatility:.0f}%) ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ volatile 
basket.")
1901	        elif volatility < 15: insights.append(f"Low volatility ({volatility:.0f}%) ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ stable basket.")
1902	        if max_streak_w >= 5: insights.append(f"Best winning streak: {max_streak_w} consecutive days.")
1903	        if short["signal"] == "BUY" and medium["signal"] == "BUY":
1904	            insights.append("Aligned bullish across short and medium term.")
1905	        if short["signal"] == "SELL" and medium["signal"] == "SELL":
1906	            insights.append("Aligned bearish ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ caution advised.")
1907	
1908	        return jsonify({
1909	            "total_days": total_days,
1910	            "win_rate": round(win_rate, 2),
1911	            "avg_return": round(avg_return, 4),
1912	            "avg_win": round(avg_win, 4),
1913	            "avg_loss": round(avg_loss, 4),
1914	            "profit_factor": round(profit_factor, 2),
1915	            "sharpe": round(sharpe, 2),
1916	            "sortino": round(sortino, 2),
1917	            "max_drawdown": round(max_dd, 2),
1918	            "best_day": round(best, 2),
1919	            "worst_day": round(worst, 2),
1920	            "max_streak_wins": max_streak_w,
1921	            "max_streak_losses": max_streak_l,
1922	            "annual_return": round(ann_ret, 2),
1923	            "calmar": round(calmar, 2),
1924	            "volatility": round(volatility, 2),
1925	            "short_term": short,
1926	            "medium_term": medium,
1927	            "long_term": long_v,
1928	            "best_month": str(best_month) if best_month else None,
1929	            "worst_month": str(worst_month) if worst_month else None,
1930	            "positive_months": pos_months,
1931	            "total_months": total_months,
1932	            "insights": insights,
1933	        })
1934	    finally:
1935	        conn.close()
1936

1938	import threading
1939	_BUILD_PROGRESS = {}
1940	_BUILD_LOCK = threading.Lock()
1941	
1942	def _update_progress(market, pct, stage, detail="", elapsed=0, total_dates=0, done_dates=0, rows=0):
1943	    _BUILD_PROGRESS[market] = {
1944	        "pct": pct, "stage": stage, "detail": detail,
1945	        "elapsed": round(elapsed, 1), "total_dates": total_dates,
1946	        "done_dates": done_dates, "rows": rows,
1947	        "eta": round((elapsed / max(done_dates, 1)) * (total_dates - done_dates), 1) if done_dates > 0 and 
total_dates > 0 else 0,
1948	    }
1949	
1950	
1951	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/progress")
1952	def api_basket_progress():
1953	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
1954	    return jsonify(_BUILD_PROGRESS.get(market, {"pct": 0, "stage": "idle", "detail": ""}))
1955	
1956	
1957	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/generate", methods=["POST"])
1958	def api_basket_generate():
1959	    data = request.json or {}
1960	    market = data.get("market", "US")
1961	    with _BUILD_LOCK:
1962	        if _BUILD_PROGRESS.get(market, {}).get("stage") in ("universe", "cache", "metrics", "historical"):
1963	            return jsonify({"error": "Already running"}), 409
1964	
1965	    def _run():
1966	        import time as _time
1967	        t0 = _time.time()
1968	        try:
1969	            _update_progress(market, 0, "universe", "Generating string universe...")
1970	            from dumbmoney.basket_screener import generate_string_universe, compute_current_metrics, 
update_historical_string_screener, build_close_pivot_cache
1971	            n = generate_string_universe(market, n=25000, force=True)
1972	            _update_progress(market, 3, "cache", f"Universe ready: {n} strings. Building close pivot cache...", 
elapsed=_time.time()-t0)
1973	            build_close_pivot_cache(market)
1974	            _update_progress(market, 8, "metrics", "Cache built. Computing metrics...", elapsed=_time.time()-t0)
1975	            compute_current_metrics(market)
1976	            _update_progress(market, 12, "historical", "Metrics done. Building historical data...", 
elapsed=_time.time()-t0)
1977	
1978	            def _hist_progress(pct, detail):
1979	                elapsed = _time.time() - t0
1980	                done_d, total_d = 0, 0
1981	                if "dates" in detail:
1982	                    try:
1983	                        part = detail.split(":")[-1].strip().split(" ")[0]
1984	                        done_d, total_d = [int(x) for x in part.split("/")]
1985	                    except Exception:
1986	                        pass
1987	                _update_progress(market, 10 + pct * 0.9, "historical", detail,
1988	                                 elapsed=elapsed, total_dates=total_d, done_dates=done_d)
1989	
1990	            update_historical_string_screener(market, force_rebuild=True, progress_callback=_hist_progress)
1991	            _update_progress(market, 100, "done", f"Complete! {n} strings, historical built.", 
elapsed=_time.time()-t0)
1992	        except Exception as e:
1993	            _update_progress(market, 0, "error", str(e), elapsed=_time.time()-t0)
1994	
1995	    threading.Thread(target=_run, daemon=True).start()
1996	    return jsonify({"started": True, "market": market})
1997

2042	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/timeframe-returns")
2043	def api_basket_timeframe_returns():
2044	    """Compute 1W and 1M returns for basket strings from cached OHLC data."""
2045	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
2046	    timeframe = request.args.get("timeframe", "1W")
2047	
2048	    conn = get_db(market)
2049	    try:
2050	        from dumbmoney.basket_screener import _load_composition, _load_close_pivot, _gather_einsum
2051	        sids, sym_list, indices, weights = _load_composition(market)
2052	        if not sids:
2053	            return jsonify({"returns": {}, "timeframe": timeframe})
2054	
2055	        _, dates, close = _load_close_pivot(market, sym_list)
2056	        if close.shape[1] == 0:
2057	            return jsonify({"returns": {}, "timeframe": timeframe})
2058	
2059	        V = _gather_einsum(close, indices, weights)
2060	        n_strings, n_dates = V.shape
2061	
2062	        if timeframe == "1W":
2063	            lookback = min(5, n_dates)
2064	        elif timeframe == "1M":
2065	            lookback = min(22, n_dates)
2066	        else:
2067	            lookback = min(1, n_dates)
2068	
2069	        returns = {}
2070	        for i, sid in enumerate(sids):
2071	            if n_dates >= lookback + 1:
2072	                cur = float(V[i, -1])
2073	                prev = float(V[i, -1 - lookback])
2074	                if prev > 0:
2075	                    returns[sid] = round((cur / prev - 1) * 100, 4)
2076	                else:
2077	                    returns[sid] = 0.0
2078	            else:
2079	                returns[sid] = 0.0
2080	
2081	        return jsonify({"returns": returns, "timeframe": timeframe, "strings": len(sids)})
2082	    finally:
2083	        conn.close()
2084

2086	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/leaderboard")
2087	def api_basket_leaderboard():
2088	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
2089	    sort_by = request.args.get("sort", "total_return")
2090	    top_n = min(int(request.args.get("top", 50)), 200)
2091	
2092	    conn = get_db(market)
2093	    try:
2094	        sort_map = {
2095	            "total_return": "weighted_alpha", "win_rate": "prob_up_1d", "sharpe": "confluence",
2096	            "avg_return": "change_pct", "best_day": "ai_matrix",
2097	        }
2098	        sql_sort = sort_map.get(sort_by, "weighted_alpha")
2099	
2100	        rows = conn.execute(f"""
2101	            SELECT m.string_id, m.name, m.price, m.change_pct, m.weighted_alpha,
2102	                   m.prob_up_1d, m.prob_up_5d, m.streak, m.confluence,
2103	                   m.ai_matrix, m.atr_signal, m.accel_signal, m.volume
2104	            FROM string_screener_metrics m
2105	            WHERE m.market = ? AND m.price > 0
2106	            ORDER BY m.{sql_sort} DESC
2107	            LIMIT ?
2108	        """, (market, top_n)).fetchall()
2109	
2110	        total = conn.execute(
2111	            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM string_screener_metrics WHERE market=? AND price > 0", (market,)
2112	        ).fetchone()[0]
2113	
2114	        data = []
2115	        for r in rows:
2116	            prob = float(r[5]) if r[5] else 50
2117	            chg = float(r[3]) if r[3] else 0
2118	            wa = float(r[4]) if r[4] else 0
2119	            conf = float(r[8]) if r[8] else 50
2120	            ai = float(r[9]) if r[9] else 50
2121	            data.append({
2122	                "string_id": r[0], "expression": (r[1] or r[0])[:60], "n_dates": 0,
2123	                "win_rate": round(prob, 2), "avg_return": round(chg, 4),
2124	                "total_return": round(wa, 2), "sharpe": round(conf, 2),
2125	                "max_drawdown": 0, "best_day": round(chg, 2),
2126	                "worst_day": 0, "avg_ai": round(ai, 1),
2127	            })
2128	
2129	        return jsonify({"data": data, "total": total, "sort": sort_by, "market": market})
2130	    finally:
2131	        conn.close()

2134	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/rebalance")
2135	def api_basket_rebalance():
2136	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
2137	    string_id = request.args.get("string_id", "")
2138	    if not string_id:
2139	        return jsonify({"error": "string_id required"}), 400
2140	
2141	    conn = get_db(market)
2142	    try:
2143	        cons = conn.execute(
2144	            "SELECT sc.symbol, sc.weight FROM string_constituents sc "
2145	            "WHERE sc.string_id=?", (string_id,)
2146	        ).fetchall()
2147	        if not cons:
2148	            return jsonify({"error": "String not found"}), 404
2149	
2150	        symbols = [c[0] for c in cons]
2151	        total_weight = sum(c[1] for c in cons)
2152	        target_pct = {c[0]: (c[1] / total_weight * 100) if total_weight > 0 else 0 for c in cons}
2153	
2154	        placeholders = ",".join("?" * len(symbols))
2155	        stats_rows = conn.execute(
2156	            f"SELECT symbol, price, change_pct, weighted_alpha, atr_signal, "
2157	            f"prob_up_1d, atr_crossed_above, atr_crossed_below, accel_signal, "
2158	            f"accel_crossed_up, accel_crossed_down FROM stats "
2159	            f"WHERE symbol IN ({placeholders})", symbols
2160	        ).fetchall()
2161	        current = {r[0]: dict(r) for r in stats_rows}
2162	
2163	        recommendations = []
2164	        for sym, weight in cons:
2165	            cur = current.get(sym, {})
2166	            price = float(cur.get("price") or 0)
2167	            chg = float(cur.get("change_pct") or 0)
2168	            wa = float(cur.get("weighted_alpha") or 0)
2169	            atr = int(cur.get("atr_signal") or 0)
2170	            accel = int(cur.get("accel_signal") or 0)
2171	            prob = float(cur.get("prob_up_1d") or 50)
2172	            cross_up = bool(cur.get("atr_crossed_above"))
2173	            cross_down = bool(cur.get("atr_crossed_below"))
2174	            accel_up = bool(cur.get("accel_crossed_up"))
2175	            accel_down = bool(cur.get("accel_crossed_down"))
2176	
2177	            current_pct = target_pct.get(sym, 0)
2178	            action = "HOLD"
2179	            urgency = 0
2180	            reasons = []
2181	
2182	            if cross_down or accel_down:
2183	                action = "REDUCE"
2184	                urgency = 3
2185	                reasons.append("SuperTrend/Accel cross down")
2186	            elif atr == -1 and wa < 0:
2187	                action = "REDUCE"
2188	                urgency = 2
2189	                reasons.append("Bearish trend + negative WA")
2190	            elif prob < 40:
2191	                action = "REDUCE"
2192	                urgency = 1
2193	                reasons.append(f"Low probability ({prob:.0f}%)")
2194	            elif cross_up and accel_up:
2195	                action = "ADD"
2196	                urgency = 3
2197	                reasons.append("Both SuperTrend & Accel crossed up")
2198	            elif atr == 1 and accel == 1 and wa > 20:
2199	                action = "ADD"
2200	                urgency = 2
2201	                reasons.append("Strong uptrend + high WA")
2202	            elif prob > 60 and wa > 10:
2203	                action = "ADD"
2204	                urgency = 1
2205	                reasons.append(f"High probability ({prob:.0f}%) + positive WA")
2206	            else:
2207	                action = "HOLD"
2208	                reasons.append("No strong signal")
2209	
2210	            recommendations.append({
2211	                "symbol": sym, "weight": round(weight, 4),
2212	                "current_pct": round(current_pct, 2),
2213	                "price": price, "change_pct": round(chg, 2),
2214	                "weighted_alpha": round(wa, 2),
2215	                "atr_signal": atr, "accel_signal": accel,
2216	                "prob_up_1d": round(prob, 2),
2217	                "action": action, "urgency": urgency,
2218	                "reasons": reasons,
2219	            })
2220	
2221	        recommendations.sort(key=lambda x: x["urgency"], reverse=True)
2222	        adds = sum(1 for r in recommendations if r["action"] == "ADD")
2223	        reduces = sum(1 for r in recommendations if r["action"] == "REDUCE")
2224	        holds = sum(1 for r in recommendations if r["action"] == "HOLD")
2225	
2226	        return jsonify({
2227	            "string_id": string_id, "market": market,
2228	            "recommendations": recommendations,
2229	            "summary": {"adds": adds, "reduces": reduces, "holds": holds,
2230	                        "total_stocks": len(recommendations)},
2231	        })
2232	    finally:
2233	        conn.close()
2234

1	import threading
2	import time

15	STEPS = [
16	    {"name": "Sync universe", "weight": 0.03},
17	    {"name": "Download bars", "weight": 0.25},
18	    {"name": "Vectorized stats", "weight": 0.20},
19	    {"name": "Fundamentals", "weight": 0.07},
20	    {"name": "Pre/post market", "weight": 0.05},
21	    {"name": "AI scores", "weight": 0.15},
22	    {"name": "Aggregates", "weight": 0.10},
23	    {"name": "Background (history + strings)", "weight": 0.15},

26	_refresh_threads = {}
27	_cancel_events = {}
28	_refresh_context = threading.local()
29	_refresh_lock = threading.Lock()

32	def _norm_market(market=None):
33	    return "INDIA" if str(market).upper() == "INDIA" else "US"
34	
35	
36	def _current_market():
37	    return _norm_market(getattr(_refresh_context, "market", "US"))
38	
39	
40	def _market_event(market=None):
41	    market = _norm_market(market or _current_market())
42	    if market not in _cancel_events:
43	        _cancel_events[market] = threading.Event()
44	    return _cancel_events[market]
45	
46	
47	def reset_stale_status():
48	    """Reset any persisted 'running'/'cancelling' status on startup (thread is dead)."""
49	    for market in DB_PATHS:
50	        try:
51	            status = get_refresh_status(market)
52	            if status.get("status") in ("running", "cancelling"):
53	                _persist_status({**status, "status": "idle", "phase": "Cancelled (server restart)"}, market)
54	        except Exception:
55	            pass
56	
57	
58	_last_persist_time = {}
59	_status_cache_lock = threading.Lock()
60	
61	def _persist_status(status_dict, market=None, force=False):
62	    try:
63	        market = _norm_market(market or status_dict.get("market") or _current_market())
64	        status_dict["market"] = market
65	        now = time.time()
66	        is_terminal = status_dict.get("status") in ("complete", "error", "cancelled", "idle")
67	        with _status_cache_lock:
68	            last_time = _last_persist_time.get(market, 0)
69	            if not force and not is_terminal and (now - last_time) < 1.0:
70	                return
71	            _last_persist_time[market] = now
72	        conn = get_db(market)
73	        conn.execute(
74	            "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (key, value) VALUES ('refresh_status', ?)",
75	            (json.dumps(status_dict),)
76	        )
77	        conn.commit()
78	        conn.close()
79	    except Exception as e:
80	        logger.warning(f"Failed to persist refresh status: {e}")
81	
82	
83	def get_refresh_status(market="US"):
84	    market = _norm_market(market)
85	    try:
86	        conn = get_db(market)
87	        row = conn.execute("SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key='refresh_status'").fetchone()
88	        conn.close()
89	        if row and row[0]:
90	            return json.loads(row[0])
91	    except Exception:
92	        pass
93	    return {
94	        "status": "idle", "market": market, "step_current": 0, "step_total": len(STEPS),
95	        "step_name": "", "phase": "", "symbols_total": 0, "symbols_done": 0,
96	        "overall_pct": 0.0, "step_pct": 0.0, "current_symbol": "",
97	        "elapsed_sec": 0, "eta_sec": 0, "started_at": 0, "errors": [],
98	    }
99	
100	
101	def _update_status(**kwargs):
102	    market = _norm_market(kwargs.get("market") or _current_market())
103	    kwargs["market"] = market
104	    status = get_refresh_status(market)
105	    old_overall = status.get("overall_pct", 0)
106	    status.update(kwargs)
107	    now = time.time()
108	    started = status.get("started_at", 0)
109	    if started and now > started:
110	        elapsed = now - started
111	        status["elapsed_sec"] = int(elapsed)
112	        new_overall = status.get("overall_pct", 0)
113	        if new_overall > 1 and elapsed > 5:
114	            rate = new_overall / elapsed
115	            status["eta_sec"] = int((100 - new_overall) / max(rate, 0.001))
116	        step = status.get("step_current", 0)
117	        step_pct = status.get("step_pct", 0)
118	        status["step_detail"] = f"Step {step+1}/{len(STEPS)}: {STEPS[step]['name']} ({int(step_pct)}%)"
119	    _persist_status(status, market)
120	    return status
121	
122	
123	def _compute_overall_pct(status):
124	    total_weight = sum(s["weight"] for s in STEPS)
125	    pct = 0.0
126	    for i, step in enumerate(STEPS):
127	        if i < status["step_current"]:
128	            pct += step["weight"] * 100
129	        elif i == status["step_current"]:
130	            pct += step["weight"] * status["step_pct"]
131	    return round(pct / total_weight, 1)
132	
133	
134	def _check_cancel():
135	    if _market_event().is_set():
136	        _update_status(status="cancelled")
137	        return True
138	    return False
139

141	def _last_weekday_cutoff(market=None):
142	    from datetime import date, timedelta
143	    d = date.today() - timedelta(days=1)
144	    us_holidays = set()
145	    if _norm_market(market) == "US":
146	        try:
147	            from pandas.tseries.holiday import USFederalHolidayCalendar
148	            cal = USFederalHolidayCalendar()
149	            start = d.replace(month=1, day=1)
150	            end = d.replace(month=12, day=31)
151	            us_holidays = {ts.date() for ts in cal.holidays(start=start, end=end)}
152	        except Exception:
153	            us_holidays = set()
154	    while d.weekday() >= 5:
155	        d -= timedelta(days=1)
156	    while d in us_holidays:
157	        d -= timedelta(days=1)
158	        while d.weekday() >= 5:
159	            d -= timedelta(days=1)
160	    return d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
161

163	def _normal_refresh_warmup_start(days=450):
164	    from datetime import date, timedelta
165	    return (date.today() - timedelta(days=days)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

168	def run_refresh(market="US"):
169	    global _refresh_threads, _cancel_events
170	    market = _norm_market(market)
171	    event = _market_event(market)
172	
173	    with _refresh_lock:
174	        status = get_refresh_status(market)
175	        if status.get("status") == "running":
176	            event.set()
177	            thread = _refresh_threads.get(market)
178	            if thread and thread.is_alive():
179	                thread.join(timeout=5)
180	            event.clear()
181	
182	        event.clear()
183	        _update_status(
184	            status="running", market=market, step_current=0, step_total=len(STEPS), overall_pct=0,
185	            started_at=time.time(), errors=[], step_name="Starting...",
186	            phase="Initializing...", symbols_total=0, symbols_done=0,
187	            step_pct=0, current_symbol="", elapsed_sec=0, eta_sec=0,
188	            new_stocks_count=0
189	        )
190	        _refresh_threads[market] = threading.Thread(target=_refresh_worker, args=(market,), daemon=True)
191	        _refresh_threads[market].start()
192	    return True
193	
194	
195	def cancel_refresh(market="US"):
196	    market = _norm_market(market)
197	    _market_event(market).set()
198	    _update_status(status="cancelled", market=market)
199	    return True
200

202	def _refresh_worker(market):
203	    _refresh_context.market = _norm_market(market)
204	    start_time = time.time()
205	
206	    try:
207	        if _check_cancel():
208	            return
209	
210	        _update_status(step_current=0, step_name="Sync universe", phase="Syncing assets...", step_pct=0)
211	        if market == "US":
212	            from dumbmoney.data_us import sync_assets
213	            n = sync_assets()
214	        else:
215	            from dumbmoney.data_india import sync_india_assets
216	            n = sync_india_assets()
217	        s = _update_status(symbols_total=n, symbols_done=n, step_pct=100)
218	        s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
219	        _persist_status(s)
220	
221	        if _check_cancel():
222	            return
223	
224	        _update_status(step_current=1, step_name="Download bars", phase="Downloading daily bars...", step_pct=0, 
symbols_done=0)
225	        updated_symbols = []
226	        if market == "US":
227	            updated_symbols = _download_us_bars_incremental(market)
228	        else:
229	            updated_symbols = _download_india_bars(market) or []
230	
231	        if _check_cancel():
232	            return
233	
234	        stats_symbols = updated_symbols
235	        label = f"{len(updated_symbols)} updated" if updated_symbols else "no changed"
236	        _update_status(step_current=2, step_name="Vectorized stats", phase=f"Computing indicators for {label} 
symbols...", step_pct=0)
237	
238	        def _stats_progress(done, total):
239	            if _check_cancel():
240	                return
241	            pct = round(done / total * 100, 1) if total else 100
242	            s = _update_status(step_pct=pct, symbols_done=done, symbols_total=total)
243	            s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
244	            _persist_status(s)
245	
246	        n_stats = vectorized_stats_pass(market, only_symbols=stats_symbols, progress_callback=_stats_progress)
247	        s = _update_status(symbols_done=n_stats, step_pct=100)
248	        s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
249	        _persist_status(s)
250	
251	        if _check_cancel():
252	            return
253	
254	        _update_status(step_current=3, step_name="Fundamentals", phase="Updating asset info...", step_pct=0)
255	
256	        def _asset_progress(pct, msg):
257	            if _check_cancel():
258	                return
259	            s = _update_status(step_pct=pct, phase=msg)
260	            s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
261	            _persist_status(s)
262	
263	        update_asset_info(market, progress_callback=_asset_progress, only_symbols=stats_symbols)
264	        s = _update_status(step_pct=100)
265	        s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
266	        _persist_status(s)
267	
268	        _update_status(step_current=4, step_name="Pre/post market", phase="Fetching snapshots...", step_pct=0)
269	
270	        if market == "US":
271	            from dumbmoney.data_us import update_pre_post_prices
272	            def _prepost_progress(pct, msg):
273	                if _check_cancel():
274	                    return
275	                s = _update_status(step_pct=pct, phase=msg)
276	                s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
277	                _persist_status(s)
278	            update_pre_post_prices(market, progress_callback=_prepost_progress, symbols=stats_symbols)
279	        else:
280	            _update_status(step_pct=100, phase="Skipped for India (no extended hours data)")
281	
282	        s = _update_status(step_pct=100)
283	        s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
284	        _persist_status(s)
285	
286	        if _check_cancel():
287	            return
288	
289	        _update_status(step_current=5, step_name="AI scores", phase="Computing AI analysis...", step_pct=0)
290	        s = _update_status(step_pct=100)
291	        s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
292	        _persist_status(s)
293	
294	        if market == "US":
295	            _update_status(step_pct=0, phase="Fetching earnings data...")
296	            from dumbmoney.data_us import update_profit_data
297	            def _profit_progress(pct, msg):
298	                if _check_cancel():
299	                    return
300	                s = _update_status(step_pct=pct, phase=msg)
301	                s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
302	                _persist_status(s)

304	            s = _update_status(step_pct=100)
305	            s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
306	            _persist_status(s)
307	
308	        _update_status(step_current=6, step_name="Aggregates", phase="Recomputing portfolios...", step_pct=0)
309	
310	        def _agg_progress(pct, msg):
311	            if _check_cancel():
312	                return
313	            s = _update_status(step_pct=pct, phase=msg)
314	            s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
315	            _persist_status(s)
316	
317	        compute_portfolio_aggregates(market, progress_callback=_agg_progress)
318	
319	        _update_status(status="running", step_current=7, step_name="Background", phase="Filling history...", 
step_pct=0, overall_pct=95)
320	
321	        def _bg_progress(pct, msg):
322	            if _check_cancel():
323	                return
324	            s = _update_status(step_pct=pct, phase=msg)
325	            s["overall_pct"] = _compute_overall_pct(s)
326	            _persist_status(s)
327	
328	        if updated_symbols:
329	            update_historical_screener(market, progress_callback=_bg_progress, only_symbols=updated_symbols, 
cancel_check=_check_cancel)
330	            _bg_progress(100, "History updated; signal matrix rebuild is explicit maintenance")
331	        else:
332	            _bg_progress(100, "Skipped history: no changed symbols")
333	
334	        # String basket screener: ensure universe exists, then recompute current metrics.
335	        try:
336	            from dumbmoney.basket_screener import compute_current_metrics, generate_string_universe, 
build_close_pivot_cache, update_historical_string_screener
337	            _update_status(step_current=7, step_name="Background", phase="Ensuring string basket universe...", 
step_pct=95, overall_pct=95)
338	
339	            def _count_strings():
340	                # NOTE: this used to reference an undefined `conn` (silently swallowed
341	                # NameError), so both counts were always 0 and the universe-changed
342	                # rebuild check below never fired.
343	                try:
344	                    c = get_db(market)
345	                    try:
346	                        return c.execute(
347	                            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM string_universe WHERE market=?", (market,)).fetchone()[0]
348	                    finally:
349	                        c.close()
350	                except Exception:
351	                    return 0
352	
353	            existing_strings = _count_strings()
354	            generate_string_universe(market)
355	            new_strings = _count_strings()
356	            _update_status(step_current=7, step_name="Background", phase="Rebuilding string basket cache...", 
step_pct=96, overall_pct=96)
357	            build_close_pivot_cache(market)
358	            _update_status(step_current=7, step_name="Background", phase="Updating string basket metrics...", 
step_pct=97, overall_pct=97)
359	            compute_current_metrics(market)
360	            _update_status(step_current=7, step_name="Background", phase="Adding today's string basket 
history...", step_pct=98, overall_pct=98)
361	            # Normal refresh only appends the latest date. A universe change needs a
362	            # full basket-history rebuild, but that scans/writes millions of rows and
363	            # must never hide inside normal refresh: surface it and let the explicit
364	            # /api/basket-screener/generate (or /historical) maintenance path do it.
365	            universe_changed = new_strings != existing_strings
366	            update_historical_string_screener(market, only_strings=None, force_rebuild=False, only_latest=True)
367	            done_phase = "String basket done."
368	            if universe_changed:
369	                done_phase = ("String basket universe changed "
370	                              f"({existing_strings} -> {new_strings}); run Basket Screener "
371	                              "generate for full history rebuild.")
372	            _update_status(step_current=7, step_name="Background", phase=done_phase, step_pct=99, overall_pct=99)
373	        except Exception as e:
374	            import traceback
375	            logger.warning(f"String basket metrics recompute failed: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
376	
377	        s = _update_status(step_pct=100, overall_pct=100, status="complete",
378	                           step_name="Complete", phase="All done!")
379	        _persist_status(s)
380	
381	    except Exception as e:
382	        if _check_cancel():
383	            return
384	        logger.error(f"Refresh error: {e}", exc_info=True)
385	        _update_status(status="error", errors=[str(e)], phase=f"Error: {e}")
386

390	    from dumbmoney.data_us import download_bars
