460	def _compute_historical_symbol_frame(grp):
461	    grp = grp.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True).copy()
462	    c = grp["close"].astype(float)
463	    h = grp["high"].astype(float)
464	    l = grp["low"].astype(float)
465	    v = grp["volume"].replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0).astype(int)
466	    st = supertrend(grp, period=14, multiplier=1.0)
467	    ac = accel(grp)
468	    ai = _historical_ai_columns(grp)
469	
470	    out = pd.DataFrame({
471	        "symbol": grp["symbol"],
472	        "date": pd.to_datetime(grp["date"]).dt.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
473	        "price": c,
474	        "change_pct": c.pct_change().replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0) * 100,
475	        "volume": v,
476	        "weighted_alpha": _weighted_alpha_history(c.values),
477	        "atrp": atrp(h, l, c),
478	        "streak": streak_vectorized(c),
479	        "atr_value": st["atr_value"].fillna(0),
480	        "atr_stop": st["stop"].fillna(0),
481	        "atr_signal": st["trend"].fillna(0).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0).astype(int),
482	        "atr_crossed_above": st["crossed_above"].fillna(0).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], 
np.nan).fillna(0).astype(int),
483	        "atr_crossed_below": st["crossed_below"].fillna(0).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], 
np.nan).fillna(0).astype(int),
484	        "atr_streak": st["streak"].fillna(0).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0).astype(int),
485	        "atr_multiplier": 1.0,
486	        "next_day_return": c.shift(-1).sub(c).div(c).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0) * 100,
487	        "next_5d_return": c.shift(-5).sub(c).div(c).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0) * 100,
488	        "prob_up_1d": prob_up(c, 1).fillna(50.0),
489	        "prob_up_5d": prob_up(c, 5).fillna(50.0),
490	        "accel_a": ac["accel_a"].fillna(0),
491	        "accel_base": ac["accel_base"].fillna(0),
492	        "accel_signal": ac["accel_signal"].fillna(0).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0).astype(int),
493	        "accel_crossed_up": ac["accel_crossed_up"].fillna(0).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], 
np.nan).fillna(0).astype(int),
494	        "accel_crossed_down": ac["accel_crossed_down"].fillna(0).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], 
np.nan).fillna(0).astype(int),
495	    })
496	    out = pd.concat([out, ai], axis=1)
497	    out["confluence"] = compute_confluence_vectorized(
498	        out["atr_signal"].values, out["accel_signal"].values,
499	        out["weighted_alpha"].values, out["streak"].values, out["prob_up_1d"].values
500	    )
501	    sma20 = c.rolling(20, min_periods=1).mean()
502	    sma50 = c.rolling(50, min_periods=1).mean()
503	    vol_avg20 = v.astype(float).rolling(20, min_periods=1).mean()
504	    vol_avg5 = v.astype(float).rolling(5, min_periods=1).mean()
505	    vol_ratio = (vol_avg5 / (vol_avg20 + 1e-10)).fillna(1.0)
506	    vol_spike = (v.astype(float) > 3.0 * vol_avg20).fillna(False)
507	    h20 = h.rolling(20, min_periods=1).max()
508	    l20 = l.rolling(20, min_periods=1).min()
509	    # Vectorized AI matrix score (replaces row-by-row _compute_ai_matrix_score loop)
510	    rsi_vec = rsi_wilder(c, 14).fillna(50).values.astype(float)
511	    wa_vec = out["weighted_alpha"].fillna(0).values.astype(float)
512	    sk_vec = out["streak"].fillna(0).values.astype(float)
513	    st_sig = out["atr_signal"].fillna(0).values.astype(float)
514	    ac_sig = out["accel_signal"].fillna(0).values.astype(float)
515	    st_xa = out["atr_crossed_above"].fillna(0).values.astype(bool)
516	    st_xb = out["atr_crossed_below"].fillna(0).values.astype(bool)
517	    ac_cu = out["accel_crossed_up"].fillna(0).values.astype(bool)
518	    ac_cd = out["accel_crossed_down"].fillna(0).values.astype(bool)
519	    at_vec = out["atrp"].fillna(0).values.astype(float)
520	    p1_vec = out["prob_up_1d"].fillna(50).values.astype(float)
521	    vr_vec = vol_ratio.values.astype(float)
522	    vs_vec = vol_spike.values.astype(bool)
523	    pr_vec = c.values.astype(float)
524	    hh_vec = h20.values.astype(float)
525	    ll_vec = l20.values.astype(float)
526	    s20_vec = sma20.values.astype(float)
527	    s50_vec = sma50.values.astype(float)
528	
529	    _clip = np.clip
530	    _tanh = np.tanh
531	    _log = np.log
532	    _sig_v = lambda x: 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(_clip(x, -500.0, 500.0)))
533	
534	    # D ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ Directional
535	    wa_norm = _tanh(wa_vec / 15.0)
536	    streak_amp = 1.0 + 0.3 * _tanh(sk_vec / 3.0)
537	    wa_component = wa_norm * streak_amp
538	    trend_component = (st_sig + ac_sig) * 0.5
539	    rsi_component = (rsi_vec - 50.0) / 20.0
540	    D = (wa_component + trend_component + rsi_component) / 3.0
541	
542	    # X ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ Crossover freshness
543	    raw_cross = st_sig + ac_sig
544	    any_cross = st_xa | st_xb | ac_cu | ac_cd
545	    boost = 1.0 + 0.5 * any_cross.astype(float)
546	    X = raw_cross * 0.5 * boost
547	
548	    # V ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ Volume confirmation
549	    log_ratio = _log(np.maximum(vr_vec, 0.01))
550	    spike_impulse = 0.3 * vs_vec.astype(float)
551	    V = _tanh((log_ratio + spike_impulse) * 2.0)
552	
553	    # B ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ Oversold bounce
554	    oversold = _sig_v((50.0 - rsi_vec) / 10.0)
555	    vol_confirm = _sig_v((at_vec - 3.0) / 1.5)
556	    B = oversold * vol_confirm * 2.0 - 1.0
557	
558	    # P ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ Probability log-odds
559	    p_clipped = _clip(p1_vec / 100.0, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
560	    P = np.log(p_clipped / (1.0 - p_clipped))
561	
562	    # Weighted sum
563	    z = 1.20 * D + 0.40 * X + 0.35 * V + 0.25 * B + 0.30 * P
564	
565	    # MA trend bias
566	    ma_valid = (s20_vec > 0) & (s50_vec > 0)
567	    z = np.where(ma_valid, z + 0.15 * _tanh((s20_vec - s50_vec) / (0.05 * s50_vec + 1e-9)), z)
568	
569	    # Price position reinforcement
570	    rng_valid = (hh_vec > ll_vec) & (pr_vec > 0)
571	    pos = np.where(rng_valid, (pr_vec - ll_vec) / (hh_vec - ll_vec + 1e-9), 0.0)
572	    range_hint = 0.10 * (pos - 0.5) * 2.0
573	    aligned = ((wa_vec > 0) & (pos > 0.5)) | ((wa_vec < 0) & (pos < 0.5))
574	    z = np.where(rng_valid & aligned, z + range_hint, z)
575	    z = np.where(rng_valid & ~aligned, z - range_hint * 0.5, z)
576	
577	    out["ai_matrix"] = np.round(100.0 * _sig_v(z), 2)
578	    numeric_cols = [cname for cname in out.columns if cname not in {"symbol", "date", "ai_bias", "ai_conclusion", 
"ai_matrix"}]
579	    out[numeric_cols] = out[numeric_cols].replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).fillna(0)
580	    return out[HISTORICAL_SCREENER_COLUMNS]
581	
582	
583	def _compute_symbol_batch(args):
584	    """Top-level worker function for multiprocessing. Process a batch of symbols."""
585	    batch_syms, db_path, existing_map, requested, version_mismatch, force_rebuild = args
586	    import sqlite3 as _sqlite3
587	    import pandas as _pd
588	    from dumbmoney.engine import _compute_historical_symbol_frame
589	
590	    conn = _sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=30)
591	    try:
592	        placeholders = ",".join("?" * len(batch_syms))
593	        bars = _pd.read_sql(
594	            f"""SELECT symbol, date, open, high, low, close, volume FROM bars
595	                WHERE timeframe='1Day' AND symbol IN ({placeholders})
596	                ORDER BY symbol, date""",
597	            conn,
598	            params=list(batch_syms),
599	        )
600	        if bars.empty:
601	            return []
602	        records = []
603	        for _, grp in bars.groupby("symbol", sort=False):
604	            if len(grp) < 2:
605	                continue
606	            sym = str(grp["symbol"].iloc[0])
607	            try:
608	                last_hist_date = existing_map.get(sym)
609	                if requested and last_hist_date and not version_mismatch and not force_rebuild:
610	                    new_bars = grp[grp["date"] > last_hist_date]
611	                    if new_bars.empty:
612	                        continue
613	                    num_new = len(new_bars)
614	                    grp_sliced = grp.tail(num_new + 252).copy()
615	                    hist = _compute_historical_symbol_frame(grp_sliced)
616	                    hist = hist[hist["date"] > last_hist_date]
617	                else:
618	                    hist = _compute_historical_symbol_frame(grp)
619	                if not hist.empty:
620	                    records.extend([tuple(r) for r in hist.itertuples(index=False, name=None)])
621	            except Exception:
622	                continue
623	        return records
624	    finally:
625	        conn.close()
626

628	def update_historical_screener(market="US", progress_callback=None, only_symbols=None, force_rebuild=False, 
cancel_check=None):
629	    """Fill historical_screener with true as-of-date indicator values."""
630	    conn = get_db(market)
631	    try:
632	        if progress_callback:
633	            progress_callback(0, "Checking historical screener state...")
634	
635	        requested = None
636	        if only_symbols is not None:
637	            requested = sorted(set(only_symbols))
638	            if not requested:
639	                if progress_callback:
640	                    progress_callback(100, "Historical screener already current")
641	                return
642	
643	        version_row = conn.execute(
644	            "SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key='historical_screener_version'"
645	        ).fetchone()
646	        version_mismatch = not version_row or version_row[0] != HISTORICAL_SCREENER_VERSION
647	        needs_rebuild = force_rebuild or (version_mismatch and only_symbols is None)
648	
649	        if needs_rebuild:
650	            conn.execute("DELETE FROM historical_screener")
651	            conn.execute("DELETE FROM signal_prob_matrix")
652	            conn.commit()
653	
654	        if requested:
655	            placeholders_req = ",".join("?" * len(requested))
656	            existing = conn.execute(
657	                f"""SELECT symbol, MAX(date) as max_date
658	                    FROM historical_screener
659	                    WHERE symbol IN ({placeholders_req})
660	                    GROUP BY symbol""",
661	                requested,
662	            ).fetchall()
663	        else:
664	            existing = conn.execute(
665	                "SELECT symbol, MAX(date) as max_date FROM historical_screener GROUP BY symbol"
666	            ).fetchall()
667	        existing_map = {row[0]: row[1] for row in existing}
668	
669	        if requested:
670	            placeholders2 = ",".join("?" * len(requested))
671	            max_rows = conn.execute(
672	                f"""SELECT symbol, MAX(date)
673	                    FROM bars
674	                    WHERE timeframe='1Day' AND symbol IN ({placeholders2})
675	                    GROUP BY symbol""",
676	                requested,
677	            ).fetchall()
678	            all_symbols = [
679	                row[0] for row in max_rows
680	                if version_mismatch or force_rebuild or existing_map.get(row[0]) != row[1]
681	            ]
682	        else:
683	            max_rows = conn.execute(
684	                "SELECT symbol, MAX(date) FROM bars WHERE timeframe='1Day' GROUP BY symbol"
685	            ).fetchall()
686	            all_symbols = [
687	                row[0] for row in max_rows
688	                if needs_rebuild or existing_map.get(row[0]) != row[1]
689	            ]
690	
691	        if not all_symbols:
692	            if progress_callback:
693	                progress_callback(100, "Historical screener already current")
694	            return
695	
696	        total_syms = len(all_symbols)
697	        total_rows = 0
698	        batch_size = 200
699	        cols_str = ", ".join(HISTORICAL_SCREENER_COLUMNS)
700	        placeholders = ",".join(["?"] * len(HISTORICAL_SCREENER_COLUMNS))
701	        insert_sql = f"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO historical_screener ({cols_str}) VALUES ({placeholders})"
702	
703	        if progress_callback:
704	            mode = "full rebuild" if needs_rebuild else "incremental"
705	            progress_callback(5, f"Historical screener {mode}: {total_syms} symbols")
706	
707	        num_workers = min(os.cpu_count() or 4, 8)
708	        use_parallel = num_workers > 1 and total_syms > batch_size * 2
709	
710	        if use_parallel:
711	            from dumbmoney.config import DB_PATHS
712	            db_path = DB_PATHS.get(market, DB_PATHS["US"])
713	            batches = [all_symbols[i:i + batch_size] for i in range(0, total_syms, batch_size)]
714	            total_batches = len(batches)
715	            batch_args = [
716	                (batch, db_path, existing_map, requested, version_mismatch, force_rebuild)
717	                for batch in batches
718	            ]
719	
720	            done_batches = 0
721	            with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:
722	                futures = [executor.submit(_compute_symbol_batch, a) for a in batch_args]
723	                for future in as_completed(futures):
724	                    if cancel_check and cancel_check():
725	                        executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
726	                        if progress_callback:
727	                            progress_callback(100, "Cancelled")
728	                        return
729	                    done_batches += 1
730	                    try:
731	                        records = future.result(timeout=600)
732	                        if records:
733	                            for j in range(0, len(records), 50000):
734	                                conn.executemany(insert_sql, records[j:j + 50000])
735	                                conn.commit()
736	                            total_rows += len(records)
737	                    except Exception as e:
738	                        logger.warning(f"Worker batch error: {e}")
739	                    if progress_callback and done_batches % max(1, total_batches // 20) == 0:
740	                        progress_callback(15 + round(done_batches / total_batches * 75), f"Processing: 
{done_batches}/{total_batches} batches ({total_rows:,} rows)")
741	        else:
742	            for i in range(0, total_syms, batch_size):
743	                if cancel_check and cancel_check():
744	                    if progress_callback:
745	                        progress_callback(100, "Cancelled")
746	                    return
747	                batch_syms = all_symbols[i:i + batch_size]
748	                done = min(i + batch_size, total_syms)
749	                if progress_callback:
750	                    progress_callback(15 + round(done / total_syms * 75), f"Processing: {done}/{total_syms} 
symbols ({total_rows:,} rows)")
751	
752	                placeholders2 = ",".join("?" * len(batch_syms))
753	                bars = pd.read_sql(
754	                    f"""SELECT symbol, date, open, high, low, close, volume FROM bars
755	                        WHERE timeframe='1Day' AND symbol IN ({placeholders2})
756	                        ORDER BY symbol, date""",
757	                    conn,
758	                    params=list(batch_syms),
759	                )
760	                if bars.empty:
761	                    continue
762	
763	                records = []
764	                for _, grp in bars.groupby("symbol", sort=False):
765	                    if len(grp) < 2:
766	                        continue
767	                    sym = str(grp["symbol"].iloc[0])
768	                    try:
769	                        last_hist_date = existing_map.get(sym)
770	                        if requested and last_hist_date and not version_mismatch and not force_rebuild:
771	                            new_bars = grp[grp["date"] > last_hist_date]
772	                            if new_bars.empty:
773	                                continue
774	                            num_new = len(new_bars)
775	                            grp_sliced = grp.tail(num_new + 252).copy()
776	                            hist = _compute_historical_symbol_frame(grp_sliced)
777	                            hist = hist[hist["date"] > last_hist_date]
778	                        else:
779	                            hist = _compute_historical_symbol_frame(grp)
780	
781	                        if not hist.empty:
782	                            records.extend([tuple(r) for r in hist.itertuples(index=False, name=None)])
783	                    except Exception as e:
784	                        logger.warning(f"Error computing historical stats for {sym}: {e}")
785	                        continue
786	
787	                if records:
788	                    for j in range(0, len(records), 50000):
789	                        conn.executemany(insert_sql, records[j:j + 50000])
790	                        conn.commit()
791	                    total_rows += len(records)
792	
793	        if only_symbols is None:
794	            conn.execute(
795	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (key, value) VALUES ('historical_screener_version', ?)",
796	                (HISTORICAL_SCREENER_VERSION,),
797	            )
798	            conn.commit()
799	
800	        if progress_callback:
801	            progress_callback(100, f"History filled ({total_rows:,} rows)")
802	    finally:
803	        conn.close()
804

806	def update_signal_prob_matrix(market="US", progress_callback=None):
807	    """Recompute signal probability matrix from historical_screener."""
808	    conn = get_db(market)
809	    try:
810	        if progress_callback:
811	            progress_callback(0, "Computing signal probabilities...")
812	        rows = conn.execute(
813	            """
814	            WITH src AS (
815	              SELECT
816	                CASE
817	                  WHEN atr_signal = 1 THEN 'cross_up'
818	                  WHEN atr_signal = -1 THEN 'cross_down'
819	                  WHEN atr_signal = 0 AND atr_streak > 0 THEN 'in_uptrend'
820	                  WHEN atr_signal = 0 AND atr_streak < 0 THEN 'in_downtrend'
821	                  ELSE 'neutral'
822	                END AS st_state,
823	                CASE
824	                  WHEN accel_crossed_up = 1 THEN 'cross_up'
825	                  WHEN accel_crossed_down = 1 THEN 'cross_down'
826	                  WHEN accel_signal = 1 THEN 'accel_up'
827	                  WHEN accel_signal = -1 THEN 'accel_down'
828	                  ELSE 'neutral'
829	                END AS accel_state,
830	                CASE
831	                  WHEN weighted_alpha > 50 THEN '>50'
832	                  WHEN weighted_alpha > 20 AND weighted_alpha <= 50 THEN '20-50'
833	                  WHEN weighted_alpha > 0 AND weighted_alpha <= 20 THEN '0-20'
834	                  ELSE '<0'
835	                END AS wa_bucket,
836	                next_day_return AS ndr
837	              FROM historical_screener
838	              WHERE next_day_return IS NOT NULL
839	            )
840	            SELECT st_state, accel_state, wa_bucket,
841	                   COUNT(*) AS sample_count,
842	                   AVG(CASE WHEN ndr > 0 THEN 1.0 ELSE 0.0 END) * 100.0 AS prob_up_1d,
843	                   AVG(CASE WHEN ndr > 1 THEN 1.0 ELSE 0.0 END) * 100.0 AS prob_up_1pct,
844	                   AVG(CASE WHEN ndr > 2 THEN 1.0 ELSE 0.0 END) * 100.0 AS prob_up_2pct,
845	                   AVG(CASE WHEN ndr < -2 THEN 1.0 ELSE 0.0 END) * 100.0 AS prob_down_2pct,
846	                   AVG(ndr) AS avg_next_day_return,
847	                   AVG(ndr * ndr) AS avg_square_return
848	            FROM src
849	            GROUP BY st_state, accel_state, wa_bucket
850	            HAVING COUNT(*) >= 10
851	            """
852	        ).fetchall()
853	        if not rows:
854	            return
855	        if progress_callback:
856	            progress_callback(70, "Saving probability matrix...")
857	        conn.execute("DELETE FROM signal_prob_matrix")
858	        out_rows = []
859	        for row in rows:
860	            st_state, accel_state, wa_bucket, sample_count, p_up, p_up1, p_up2, p_dn2, avg_ret, avg_sq = row
861	            variance = max(float(avg_sq or 0) - float(avg_ret or 0) ** 2, 0.0)
862	            sharpe = float(avg_ret or 0) / (float(np.sqrt(variance)) + 1e-10)
863	            out_rows.append((
864	                st_state, accel_state, wa_bucket,
865	                round(float(p_up or 0), 2),
866	                round(float(p_up1 or 0), 2),
867	                round(float(p_up2 or 0), 2),
868	                round(float(p_dn2 or 0), 2),
869	                int(sample_count),
870	                round(float(avg_ret or 0), 4),
871	                round(sharpe, 4),
872	            ))
873	        conn.executemany(
874	            """INSERT OR REPLACE INTO signal_prob_matrix (st_state, accel_state, wa_bucket,
875	               prob_up_1d, prob_up_1pct, prob_up_2pct, prob_down_2pct,
876	               sample_count, avg_next_day_return, sharpe)
877	               VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
878	            out_rows
879	        )
880	        conn.commit()
881	        if progress_callback:
882	            progress_callback(100, "Signal probability matrix updated")
883	    finally:
884	        conn.close()
885

394	def _build_stats_query(conn, market, search, exchange, asset_type,
395	                        sort, sort_dir, page, per_page, args):
396	
397	    where = ["1=1"]
398	    params = []
399	
400	    if search:
401	        where.append("(s.symbol LIKE ? OR s.name LIKE ?)")
402	        params.extend([f"%{search}%", f"%{search}%"])
403	    if exchange:
404	        where.append("exchange = ?")
405	        params.append(exchange)
406	    if asset_type:
407	        where.append("asset_class = ?")
408	        params.append(asset_type)
409	
410	    min_wa = args.get("min_wa")
411	    max_wa = args.get("max_wa")
412	    if min_wa:
413	        where.append("weighted_alpha >= ?")
414	        params.append(float(min_wa))
415	    if max_wa:
416	        where.append("weighted_alpha <= ?")
417	        params.append(float(max_wa))
418	
419	    min_price = args.get("min_price")
420	    max_price = args.get("max_price")
421	    if min_price:
422	        where.append("price >= ?")
423	        params.append(float(min_price))
424	    if max_price:
425	        where.append("price <= ?")
426	        params.append(float(max_price))
427	
428	    min_change = args.get("min_change")
429	    max_change = args.get("max_change")
430	    if min_change:
431	        where.append("change_pct >= ?")
432	        params.append(float(min_change))
433	    if max_change:
434	        where.append("change_pct <= ?")
435	        params.append(float(max_change))
436	
437	    min_streak = args.get("min_streak")
438	    if min_streak:
439	        where.append("streak >= ?")
440	        params.append(int(min_streak))
441	
442	    min_volume = args.get("min_volume")
443	    if min_volume:
444	        where.append("volume >= ?")
445	        params.append(int(min_volume))
446	
447	    atr_status = args.get("atr_status")
448	    if atr_status:
449	        if atr_status == "above":
450	            where.append("atr_signal = 1")
451	        elif atr_status == "below":
452	            where.append("atr_signal = -1")
453	        elif atr_status == "crossed-above":
454	            where.append("atr_crossed_above = 1")
455	        elif atr_status == "crossed-below":
456	            where.append("atr_crossed_below = 1")
457	
458	    accel_status = args.get("accel_status")
459	    if accel_status:
460	        if accel_status == "up":
461	            where.append("accel_signal = 1")
462	        elif accel_status == "down":
463	            where.append("accel_signal = -1")
464	        elif accel_status == "crossed-up":
465	            where.append("accel_crossed_up = 1")
466	        elif accel_status == "crossed-down":
467	            where.append("accel_crossed_down = 1")
468	
469	    fractionable = args.get("fractionable")
470	    if fractionable:
471	        where.append("fractionable = ?")
472	        params.append(1 if fractionable == "yes" else 0)
473	
474	    profit_status = args.get("profit_status")
475	    if profit_status:
476	        where.append("profit_status = ?")
477	        params.append(profit_status)
478	
479	    where_str = " AND ".join(where)
480	
481	    # Filters only reference stats columns, so COUNT can skip the ai_analysis join
482	    # (LEFT JOIN on the unique ai_analysis.symbol key never changes the row count).
483	    if "a." in where_str:
484	        total = conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stats s LEFT JOIN ai_analysis a ON s.symbol=a.symbol WHERE 
{where_str}", params).fetchone()[0]
485	    else:
486	        total = conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM stats s WHERE {where_str}", params).fetchone()[0]
487	
488	    allowed_sorts = {
489	        "symbol", "name", "price", "change_pct", "weighted_alpha", "volume", "streak",
490	        "atr_signal", "atr_stop", "atr_value", "atr_streak", "atrp",
491	        "atr_crossed_above", "atr_crossed_below",
492	        "prob_up_1d", "prob_up_5d",
493	        "next_day_return", "pre_price", "pre_change_pct", "post_price", "post_change_pct",
494	        "profit_status", "fractionable", "marginable", "asset_class", "exchange", "confluence",
495	        "accel_a", "accel_base", "accel_signal", "accel_streak", "accel_crossed_up", "accel_crossed_down",
496	        "ai_overall_score", "ai_bias", "ai_tech_score", "ai_volume_profile_score",
497	        "ai_trendline_score", "ai_sentiment_score", "ai_conclusion", "ai_matrix"
498	    }
499	    if sort not in allowed_sorts:
500	        sort = "weighted_alpha"
501	    direction = "DESC" if sort_dir == "desc" else "ASC"
502	
503	    ai_col_map = {
504	        "ai_overall_score": "a.overall_score",
505	        "ai_bias": "a.bias",
506	        "ai_tech_score": "a.tech_score",
507	        "ai_volume_profile_score": "a.volume_profile_score",
508	        "ai_trendline_score": "a.trendline_score",
509	        "ai_sentiment_score": "a.sentiment_score",
510	        "ai_conclusion": "a.conclusion",
511	        "ai_matrix": "a.ai_matrix",
512	    }
513	    sort_col = ai_col_map.get(sort) or f"s.{sort}"
514	
515	    # "<col> <dir> NULLS LAST" is row-identical to the old CASE-WHEN-NULL wrapper but
516	    # lets SQLite use the stats indexes (idx_stats_wa etc.) instead of a temp B-tree.
517	    order_clause = f"{sort_col} {direction} NULLS LAST"
518	
519	    offset = (page - 1) * per_page
520	    rows = conn.execute(
521	        f"SELECT s.symbol, s.name, s.price, s.volume, s.change_pct, "
522	        f"s.atrp, s.weighted_alpha, s.atr_signal, s.atr_stop, s.atr_value, s.atr_streak, "
523	        f"s.atr_crossed_above, s.atr_crossed_below, s.atr_multiplier, s.streak, "
524	        f"s.next_day_return, s.prob_up_1d, s.prob_up_5d, "
525	        f"s.pre_price, s.pre_change_pct, s.post_price, s.post_change_pct, "
526	        f"s.profit_status, s.fractionable, s.marginable, s.asset_class, s.exchange, "
527	        f"s.last_updated, s.oldest_data, s.accel_a, s.accel_base, s.accel_signal, "
528	        f"s.accel_crossed_up, s.accel_crossed_down, s.accel_streak, s.confluence, "
529	        f"a.overall_score as ai_overall_score, a.bias as ai_bias, "
530	        f"a.tech_score as ai_tech_score, a.momentum_score as ai_momentum_score, "
531	        f"a.volume_score as ai_volume_score, a.events_score as ai_events_score, "
532	        f"a.volume_profile_score as ai_volume_profile_score, "
533	        f"a.trendline_score as ai_trendline_score, a.sentiment_score as ai_sentiment_score, "
534	        f"a.conclusion as ai_conclusion, a.ai_matrix "
535	        f"FROM stats s LEFT JOIN ai_analysis a ON s.symbol=a.symbol "
536	        f"WHERE {where_str} ORDER BY {order_clause} LIMIT ? OFFSET ?",
537	        params + [per_page, offset]
538	    ).fetchall()
539	
540	    data = [dict(r) for r in rows]
541	    for d in data:
542	        d.setdefault("ai_overall_score", 0)
543	        d.setdefault("ai_bias", "neutral")
544	        d.setdefault("ai_tech_score", 0)
545	        d.setdefault("ai_volume_profile_score", 0)
546	        d.setdefault("ai_trendline_score", 0)
547	        d.setdefault("ai_sentiment_score", 0)
548	        d.setdefault("ai_conclusion", "HOLD")
549	        d.setdefault("ai_matrix", "")
550	    return {
551	        "data": data, "total": total, "page": page, "per_page": per_page,
552	        "total_pages": (total + per_page - 1) // per_page, "historical": False
553	    }

556	@api_bp.route("/stats")
557	def api_stats():
558	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
559	    symbol = request.args.get("symbol")
560	    conn = get_db(market)
561	    try:
562	        if symbol:
563	            row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM stats WHERE symbol=?", (symbol,)).fetchone()
564	            return jsonify(dict(row) if row else {})
565	        else:
566	            rows = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM stats LIMIT 500").fetchall()
567	            return jsonify([dict(r) for r in rows])
568	    finally:
569	        conn.close()

572	@api_bp.route("/market-breadth")
573	def api_market_breadth():
574	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
575	    conn = get_db(market)
576	    try:
577	        row = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM stats").fetchone()
578	        total = row["total"] if row else 0
579	        up = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM stats WHERE change_pct > 0").fetchone()["c"]
580	        down = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM stats WHERE change_pct < 0").fetchone()["c"]
581	        unchanged = total - up - down
582	        return jsonify({
583	            "total": total, "advancers": up, "decliners": down, "unchanged": unchanged,
584	            "pct_up": round(up / total * 100, 1) if total > 0 else 0
585	        })
586	    finally:
587	        conn.close()

590	@api_bp.route("/top-lists")
591	def api_top_lists():
592	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
593	    conn = get_db(market)
594	    try:
595	        gainers = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute(
596	            "SELECT symbol, name, price, change_pct FROM stats ORDER BY change_pct DESC LIMIT 10"
597	        ).fetchall()]
598	        by_volume = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute(
599	            "SELECT symbol, name, price, volume FROM stats ORDER BY volume DESC LIMIT 10"
600	        ).fetchall()]
601	        by_wa = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute(
602	            "SELECT symbol, name, price, weighted_alpha FROM stats ORDER BY weighted_alpha DESC LIMIT 10"
603	        ).fetchall()]
604	        return jsonify({"gainers": gainers, "by_volume": by_volume, "by_weighted_alpha": by_wa})
605	    finally:
606	        conn.close()

620	@api_bp.route("/hs-dates")
621	def api_hs_dates():
622	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
623	    conn = get_db(market)
624	    try:
625	        # Recursive "loose index scan": one indexed MAX() seek per distinct date via
626	        # idx_bars_tf_date instead of scanning the whole multi-million-row index
627	        # (measured 410ms -> 38ms on US, 3.6s -> 26ms on India).
628	        rows = conn.execute(
629	            """
630	            WITH RECURSIVE d(dt) AS (
631	              SELECT (SELECT MAX(date) FROM bars WHERE timeframe='1Day')
632	              UNION ALL
633	              SELECT (SELECT MAX(date) FROM bars WHERE timeframe='1Day' AND date < d.dt)
634	              FROM d WHERE d.dt IS NOT NULL LIMIT 366
635	            )
636	            SELECT dt AS date FROM d WHERE dt IS NOT NULL LIMIT 365
637	            """
638	        ).fetchall()
639	        return jsonify([r["date"] for r in rows])
640	    finally:

1690	                                 asset_type, date_cutoff, request.args)

1710	def _basket_constituents(string_id, market):
1711	    conn = get_db(market)
1712	    try:
1713	        rows = conn.execute(
1714	            "SELECT symbol, weight FROM string_constituents WHERE string_id=?",
1715	            (string_id,)).fetchall()

1721	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/<string_id>/ohlc")
1722	def api_basket_ohlc(string_id):
1723	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
1724	    limit = int(request.args.get("limit", 200))
1725	    timeframe = request.args.get("timeframe", "1D")
1726	    cons, conn = _basket_constituents(string_id, market)
1727	    try:
1728	        if not cons:
1729	            return jsonify([])
1730	        symbols = [c[0] for c in cons]
1731	        weights = {c[0]: c[1] for c in cons}
1732	        wsum = sum(weights.values()) or 1.0
1733	        weights = {k: v / wsum for k, v in weights.items()}
1734	        ohlc_df = _combined_ohlc_for_symbols(conn, symbols, weights=weights)
1735	        if ohlc_df is None:
1736	            return jsonify([])
1737	        ohlc_df = _resample_ohlc(ohlc_df, timeframe)
1738	        ohlc_df = ohlc_df.tail(limit)
1739	        return jsonify([dict(r) for _, r in ohlc_df.iterrows()])
1740	    finally:
1741	        conn.close()

1744	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/<string_id>/supertrend")
1745	def api_basket_supertrend(string_id):
1746	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
1747	    period = int(request.args.get("period", 14))
1748	    multiplier = float(request.args.get("multiplier", 1.0))
1749	    timeframe = request.args.get("timeframe", "1D")
1750	    cons, conn = _basket_constituents(string_id, market)
1751	    try:
1752	        if not cons:
1753	            return jsonify([])
1754	        symbols = [c[0] for c in cons]
1755	        weights = {c[0]: c[1] for c in cons}
1756	        wsum = sum(weights.values()) or 1.0
1757	        weights = {k: v / wsum for k, v in weights.items()}
1758	        ohlc_df = _combined_ohlc_for_symbols(conn, symbols, weights=weights)
1759	        if ohlc_df is None or ohlc_df.empty:
1760	            return jsonify([])
1761	        ohlc_df = _resample_ohlc(ohlc_df, timeframe)
1762	        import pandas as pd
1763	        from dumbmoney.indicators import supertrend as compute_st
1764	        st = compute_st(ohlc_df, period=period, multiplier=multiplier)
1765	        result = []
1766	        for i, (_, row) in enumerate(st.iterrows()):
1767	            result.append({
1768	                "date": ohlc_df.iloc[i]["date"] if i < len(ohlc_df) else "",
1769	                "supertrend": round(float(row["supertrend"]), 4) if pd.notna(row["supertrend"]) else None,
1770	                "trend": int(row["trend"]),
1771	                "signal": int(row["signal"]),
1772	                "stop": round(float(row["stop"]), 4) if pd.notna(row["stop"]) else None,
1773	                "atr_value": round(float(row["atr_value"]), 4) if pd.notna(row["atr_value"]) else None,
1774	            })
1775	        return jsonify(result)
1776	    finally:
1777	        conn.close()

2086	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/leaderboard")
2087	def api_basket_leaderboard():
2088	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
2089	    sort_by = request.args.get("sort", "total_return")
2090	    top_n = min(int(request.args.get("top", 50)), 200)
2091

2094	        sort_map = {
2095	            "total_return": "weighted_alpha", "win_rate": "prob_up_1d", "sharpe": "confluence",
2096	            "avg_return": "change_pct", "best_day": "ai_matrix",
2097	        }

2099

2101	            SELECT m.string_id, m.name, m.price, m.change_pct, m.weighted_alpha,
2102	                   m.prob_up_1d, m.prob_up_5d, m.streak, m.confluence,
2103	                   m.ai_matrix, m.atr_signal, m.accel_signal, m.volume
2104	            FROM string_screener_metrics m
2105	            WHERE m.market = ? AND m.price > 0
2106	            ORDER BY m.{sql_sort} DESC
2107	            LIMIT ?
2108	        """, (market, top_n)).fetchall()
2109

2111	            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM string_screener_metrics WHERE market=? AND price > 0", (market,)

2123	                "win_rate": round(prob, 2), "avg_return": round(chg, 4),
2124	                "total_return": round(wa, 2), "sharpe": round(conf, 2),
2125	                "max_drawdown": 0, "best_day": round(chg, 2),
2126	                "worst_day": 0, "avg_ai": round(ai, 1),
2127	            })

2134	@api_bp.route("/basket-screener/rebalance")
2135	def api_basket_rebalance():
2136	    market = request.args.get("market", "US")
2137	    string_id = request.args.get("string_id", "")
2138	    if not string_id:
2139	        return jsonify({"error": "string_id required"}), 400
2140	
2141	    conn = get_db(market)
2142	    try:
2143	        cons = conn.execute(
2144	            "SELECT sc.symbol, sc.weight FROM string_constituents sc "
2145	            "WHERE sc.string_id=?", (string_id,)
2146	        ).fetchall()
2147	        if not cons:
2148	            return jsonify({"error": "String not found"}), 404
2149	
2150	        symbols = [c[0] for c in cons]
2151	        total_weight = sum(c[1] for c in cons)
2152	        target_pct = {c[0]: (c[1] / total_weight * 100) if total_weight > 0 else 0 for c in cons}
2153	
2154	        placeholders = ",".join("?" * len(symbols))
2155	        stats_rows = conn.execute(
2156	            f"SELECT symbol, price, change_pct, weighted_alpha, atr_signal, "
2157	            f"prob_up_1d, atr_crossed_above, atr_crossed_below, accel_signal, "
2158	            f"accel_crossed_up, accel_crossed_down FROM stats "
2159	            f"WHERE symbol IN ({placeholders})", symbols
2160	        ).fetchall()
2161	        current = {r[0]: dict(r) for r in stats_rows}
2162	
2163	        recommendations = []
2164	        for sym, weight in cons:
2165	            cur = current.get(sym, {})
2166	            price = float(cur.get("price") or 0)
2167	            chg = float(cur.get("change_pct") or 0)
2168	            wa = float(cur.get("weighted_alpha") or 0)
2169	            atr = int(cur.get("atr_signal") or 0)
2170	            accel = int(cur.get("accel_signal") or 0)
2171	            prob = float(cur.get("prob_up_1d") or 50)
2172	            cross_up = bool(cur.get("atr_crossed_above"))
2173	            cross_down = bool(cur.get("atr_crossed_below"))
2174	            accel_up = bool(cur.get("accel_crossed_up"))
2175	            accel_down = bool(cur.get("accel_crossed_down"))
2176	
2177	            current_pct = target_pct.get(sym, 0)
2178	            action = "HOLD"
2179	            urgency = 0
2180	            reasons = []
2181	
2182	            if cross_down or accel_down:
2183	                action = "REDUCE"
2184	                urgency = 3
2185	                reasons.append("SuperTrend/Accel cross down")
2186	            elif atr == -1 and wa < 0:
2187	                action = "REDUCE"
2188	                urgency = 2
2189	                reasons.append("Bearish trend + negative WA")
2190	            elif prob < 40:
2191	                action = "REDUCE"
2192	                urgency = 1
2193	                reasons.append(f"Low probability ({prob:.0f}%)")
2194	            elif cross_up and accel_up:
2195	                action = "ADD"
2196	                urgency = 3
2197	                reasons.append("Both SuperTrend & Accel crossed up")
2198	            elif atr == 1 and accel == 1 and wa > 20:
2199	                action = "ADD"
2200	                urgency = 2
2201	                reasons.append("Strong uptrend + high WA")
2202	            elif prob > 60 and wa > 10:
2203	                action = "ADD"
2204	                urgency = 1
2205	                reasons.append(f"High probability ({prob:.0f}%) + positive WA")
2206	            else:
2207	                action = "HOLD"
2208	                reasons.append("No strong signal")
2209	
2210	            recommendations.append({
2211	                "symbol": sym, "weight": round(weight, 4),
2212	                "current_pct": round(current_pct, 2),
2213	                "price": price, "change_pct": round(chg, 2),
2214	                "weighted_alpha": round(wa, 2),
2215	                "atr_signal": atr, "accel_signal": accel,
2216	                "prob_up_1d": round(prob, 2),
2217	                "action": action, "urgency": urgency,
2218	                "reasons": reasons,
2219	            })
2220	
2221	        recommendations.sort(key=lambda x: x["urgency"], reverse=True)
2222	        adds = sum(1 for r in recommendations if r["action"] == "ADD")
2223	        reduces = sum(1 for r in recommendations if r["action"] == "REDUCE")
2224	        holds = sum(1 for r in recommendations if r["action"] == "HOLD")
2225	
2226	        return jsonify({
2227	            "string_id": string_id, "market": market,
2228	            "recommendations": recommendations,
2229	            "summary": {"adds": adds, "reduces": reduces, "holds": holds,
2230	                        "total_stocks": len(recommendations)},
2231	        })
2232	    finally:
2233	        conn.close()
2234

1031	def generate_long_short_strings(market="US", n=25000):
1032	    """Generate long+short strings. Each has 5 long (highest WA) + 5 short (lowest WA).
1033	    Only stocks, marginable, shortable, volume >= 100000.
1034	    Total 10 stocks per string. 60/40 allocation ensures positive initial basket value:
1035	    $600 long (120/stock) + -$400 short (-80/stock) = $200 net initial."""
1036	    ALLOCATION = 1000.0
1037	    LONG_PCT = 0.60
1038	    SHORT_PCT = 0.40
1039	    LONG_N = 5
1040	    SHORT_N = 5
1041	    TARGET_STOCKS = LONG_N + SHORT_N
1042	    conn = get_db(market)
1043	    try:
1044	        existing = conn.execute(
1045	            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM string_universe WHERE market=? AND string_id LIKE 'LS%'",
1046	            (market,)).fetchone()[0]
1047	        if existing >= n:
1048	            logger.info(f"[{market}] ls_string_universe already has {existing} strings; skipping")
1049	            return existing
1050	
1051	        conn.execute("DELETE FROM string_constituents WHERE string_id IN "
1052	                     "(SELECT string_id FROM string_universe WHERE market=? AND string_id LIKE 'LS%')",
1053	                     (market,))
1054	        conn.execute("DELETE FROM string_universe WHERE market=? AND string_id LIKE 'LS%'", (market,))
1055	        conn.commit()
1056	
1057	        syms = [r[0] for r in conn.execute(
1058	            "SELECT a.symbol FROM assets a JOIN stats s ON s.symbol=a.symbol "
1059	            "WHERE a.status='active' AND a.tradable=1 "
1060	            "AND LOWER(COALESCE(a.asset_class,'')) = 'stock' "
1061	            "AND COALESCE(a.marginable,0) = 1 "
1062	            "AND COALESCE(a.shortable,0) = 1 "
1063	            "AND COALESCE(s.volume,0) >= 100000 "
1064	            "AND COALESCE(s.price,0) > 0").fetchall()]
1065	
1066	        if len(syms) < TARGET_STOCKS * 2:
1067	            logger.warning(f"[{market}] too few marginable+shortable symbols ({len(syms)})")
1068	            return 0
1069	
1070	        wa_rows = conn.execute(
1071	            f"SELECT symbol, weighted_alpha, price FROM stats WHERE symbol IN ({','.join('?' * len(syms))})",
1072	            syms).fetchall()
1073	        wa_map = {r[0]: float(r[1] or 0) for r in wa_rows}
1074	        price_map = {r[0]: float(r[2] or 0) for r in wa_rows}
1075	
1076	        frac_rows = conn.execute(
1077	            f"SELECT symbol, fractionable FROM assets WHERE symbol IN ({','.join('?' * len(syms))})",
1078	            syms).fetchall()
1079	        frac_map = {r[0]: bool(r[1]) for r in frac_rows}
1080	
1081	        scored = [(s, wa_map.get(s, 0)) for s in syms if price_map.get(s, 0) > 0]
1082	        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
1083	
1084	        half = len(scored) // 2
1085	        long_pool = scored[:half]
1086	        short_pool = scored[half:]
1087	
1088	        rng = np.random.default_rng(20240710)
1089	        start = conn.execute(
1090	            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(CAST(SUBSTR(string_id,3) AS INTEGER)),0) "
1091	            "FROM string_universe WHERE string_id LIKE 'LS%'").fetchone()[0]
1092	        count = 0
1093	        batch_univ = []
1094	        batch_cons = []
1095	
1096	        long_per_stock = (ALLOCATION * LONG_PCT) / LONG_N
1097	        short_per_stock = (ALLOCATION * SHORT_PCT) / SHORT_N
1098	
1099	        for i in range(start + 1, start + 1 + n):
1100	            long_picked = [long_pool[j] for j in rng.choice(len(long_pool), size=LONG_N, replace=False)]
1101	            short_picked = [short_pool[j] for j in rng.choice(len(short_pool), size=SHORT_N, replace=False)]
1102	
1103	            weights = np.zeros(TARGET_STOCKS)
1104	            picked = []
1105	            for j, (sym, _) in enumerate(long_picked):
1106	                picked.append(sym)
1107	                price = price_map[sym]
1108	                raw_w = long_per_stock / price
1109	                weights[j] = round(raw_w, 4)
1110	            for j, (sym, _) in enumerate(short_picked):
1111	                picked.append(sym)
1112	                price = price_map[sym]
1113	                raw_w = short_per_stock / price
1114	                weights[LONG_N + j] = -round(raw_w, 4)
1115	
1116	            weights = np.where(weights == 0, 0.001, weights)
1117	            sid = f"LS{i:06d}"
1118	            long_part = " + ".join(f"{picked[j]}*{weights[j]:g}" for j in range(LONG_N))
1119	            short_part = " + ".join(f"{picked[j]}*{weights[j]:g}" for j in range(LONG_N, TARGET_STOCKS))
1120	            expr = f"LONG({long_part}) SHORT({short_part})"
1121	            batch_univ.append((sid, market, TARGET_STOCKS, expr, datetime.utcnow().isoformat(), 1))
1122	            for j in range(TARGET_STOCKS):
1123	                batch_cons.append((sid, str(picked[j]), float(weights[j])))
1124	            count += 1
1125	            if len(batch_univ) >= 500:
1126	                conn.executemany(
1127	                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_universe "
1128	                    "(string_id, market, num_stocks, expression, created_at, active) "
1129	                    "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)", batch_univ)
1130	                conn.executemany(
1131	                    "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_constituents (string_id, symbol, weight) "
1132	                    "VALUES (?,?,?)", batch_cons)
1133	                conn.commit()
1134	                batch_univ.clear(); batch_cons.clear()
1135	        if batch_univ:
1136	            conn.executemany(
1137	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_universe "
1138	                "(string_id, market, num_stocks, expression, created_at, active) "
1139	                "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)", batch_univ)
1140	            conn.executemany(
1141	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_constituents (string_id, symbol, weight) "
1142	                "VALUES (?,?,?)", batch_cons)
1143	            conn.commit()
1144	        logger.info(f"[{market}] generated {count} long+short strings")
1145	        return count
1146	    finally:
1147	        conn.close()
1148

1150	def update_historical_string_screener(market="US", only_strings=None, force_rebuild=False,
1151	                                       date_limit=None, progress_callback=None, only_latest=False,
1152	                                       string_id_like=None):
1153	    """Compute historical_string_screener for all dates.
1154	    Uses .npy cache + gather+einsum + single-query metrics loading.
1155	    If only_latest=True, loads full history for indicator computation but only inserts the latest date.
1156	    """
1157	    t0 = time.time()
1158	    sids, sym_list, indices, weights = _load_composition(market, only_strings)
1159	    if not sids:
1160	        logger.info(f"[{market}] no strings")
1161	        return 0
1162	
1163	    if string_id_like:
1164	        import fnmatch
1165	        mask = [fnmatch.fnmatch(s, string_id_like) for s in sids]
1166	        keep = np.array(mask)
1167	        sids = [s for s, m in zip(sids, mask) if m]
1168	        indices = indices[keep]
1169	        weights = weights[keep]
1170	        if not sids:
1171	            logger.info(f"[{market}] no strings matching {string_id_like}")
1172	            return 0
1173	        logger.info(f"[{market}] filtered to {len(sids)} strings matching {string_id_like}")
1174	
1175	    sym_list, dates, close_pivot = _load_close_pivot(market, sym_list)
1176	    logger.info(f"[{market}] close_pivot loaded: {close_pivot.shape}, dates={len(dates)}, sids={len(sids)}, 
sym_list={len(sym_list)}")
1177	    if close_pivot.shape[1] == 0:
1178	        logger.warning(f"[{market}] no dates in close_pivot")
1179	        return 0
1180	
1181	    if date_limit is not None:
1182	        dates = dates[-date_limit:]
1183	        close_pivot = close_pivot[:, -date_limit:]
1184	
1185	    # When only_latest=True, we only need enough history for indicator warmup (SuperTrend needs ~30 bars)
1186	    # This is the critical speedup: instead of processing ALL 4000+ dates, only process last 50
1187	    if only_latest and len(dates) > 50:
1188	        dates = dates[-50:]
1189	        close_pivot = close_pivot[:, -50:]
1190	        logger.info(f"[{market}] only_latest mode: trimmed to last {len(dates)} dates for speed")
1191	
1192	    n_dates = len(dates)
1193	    n = len(sids)
1194	    logger.info(f"[{market}] computing basket values: {n} strings x {n_dates} dates")
1195	
1196	    V = _gather_einsum(close_pivot, indices, weights)
1197	    series = _series_metrics(V)
1198	    Vn = series["V"]
1199	    ret = series["ret"]
1200	    streak_full = series["streak_series"]
1201	
1202	    next_day_full = np.zeros((n, n_dates), dtype=np.float32)
1203	    next_day_full[:, :-1] = ret[:, 1:]
1204	    next_5d_full = np.zeros((n, n_dates), dtype=np.float32)
1205	    for t in range(n_dates - 5):
1206	        next_5d_full[:, t] = ((Vn[:, t+5] - Vn[:, t]) / np.where(Vn[:, t] == 0, 1e-9, Vn[:, t])) * 100.0
1207	    price_full = Vn
1208	
1209	    ohlc = _load_ohlc_pivots(market, sym_list)
1210	    if ohlc is not None:
1211	        high_pivot, low_pivot, open_pivot = ohlc
1212	        if date_limit is not None:
1213	            high_pivot = high_pivot[:, -date_limit:]
1214	            low_pivot = low_pivot[:, -date_limit:]
1215	            open_pivot = open_pivot[:, -date_limit:]
1216	        # Trim OHLC to match close_pivot dates when only_latest
1217	        if only_latest and high_pivot.shape[1] > n_dates:
1218	            high_pivot = high_pivot[:, -n_dates:]
1219	            low_pivot = low_pivot[:, -n_dates:]
1220	            open_pivot = open_pivot[:, -n_dates:]
1221	        logger.info(f"[{market}] computing basket OHLC and indicators (SuperTrend + Accel on basket value)")
1222	        basket_ohlc = _compute_basket_ohlc(close_pivot, high_pivot, low_pivot, open_pivot, indices, weights)
1223	        basket_ind = _compute_basket_indicators(basket_ohlc, period=14, multiplier=1.0)
1224	        logger.info(f"[{market}] basket indicators computed")
1225	    else:
1226	        logger.warning(f"[{market}] no OHLC pivots cached; falling back to averaged signals (inaccurate)")
1227	        basket_ind = None
1228	
1229	    conn = get_db(market)
1230	    try:
1231	        if only_strings is None and force_rebuild:
1232	            conn.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS historical_string_screener")
1233	            conn.execute("""CREATE TABLE historical_string_screener (
1234	                string_id TEXT, date TEXT, name TEXT, price REAL, change_pct REAL,
1235	                volume REAL, weighted_alpha REAL, atrp REAL, streak INTEGER,
1236	                atr_signal INTEGER, atr_stop REAL, atr_value REAL, atr_streak INTEGER,
1237	                atr_crossed_above INTEGER, atr_crossed_below INTEGER, atr_multiplier REAL,
1238	                next_day_return REAL, next_5d_return REAL, prob_up_1d REAL, prob_up_5d REAL,
1239	                pre_price REAL, pre_change_pct REAL, post_price REAL, post_change_pct REAL,
1240	                accel_a REAL, accel_base REAL, accel_signal INTEGER, accel_crossed_up INTEGER,
1241	                accel_crossed_down INTEGER, accel_streak INTEGER, confluence REAL,
1242	                ai_overall_score REAL, ai_bias TEXT, ai_tech_score REAL, ai_momentum_score REAL,
1243	                ai_volume_score REAL, ai_events_score REAL, ai_volume_profile_score REAL,
1244	                ai_trendline_score REAL, ai_sentiment_score REAL, ai_conclusion TEXT, ai_matrix REAL,
1245	                PRIMARY KEY (string_id, date))""")
1246	            conn.commit()
1247	            logger.info(f"[{market}] historical_string_screener table recreated (indexes deferred)")
1248	
1249	        chunk = 200
1250	        total_rows = 0
1251	
1252	        logger.info(f"[{market}] pre-loading historical metrics ({'latest only' if only_latest else 
f'{len(dates)} dates'}, {len(sym_list)} symbols)...")
1253	        _t_load = time.time()
1254	        hist_dates_to_load = [dates[-1]] if only_latest else dates
1255	        all_wm = _load_hist_metrics_chunk(conn, market, sym_list, hist_dates_to_load, W=None, Wn=None, 
indices=indices, weights=weights)
1256	        logger.info(f"[{market}] all metrics loaded in {time.time()-_t_load:.1f}s, 
keys={list(all_wm.keys())[:5]}...")
1257	
1258	        if basket_ind is not None:
1259	            atr_sig_full = basket_ind["atr_signal"].astype(np.int32)
1260	            atr_cu_full = basket_ind["atr_crossed_above"].astype(np.int32)
1261	            atr_cd_full = basket_ind["atr_crossed_below"].astype(np.int32)
1262	            acc_sig_full = basket_ind["accel_signal"].astype(np.int32)
1263	            acc_cu_full = basket_ind["accel_crossed_up"].astype(np.int32)
1264	            acc_cd_full = basket_ind["accel_crossed_down"].astype(np.int32)
1265	        else:
1266	            _z = np.zeros((n, n_dates), np.float32)
1267	            atr_sig_full = np.sign(all_wm.get("atr_signal", _z)).astype(np.int32)
1268	            acc_sig_full = np.sign(all_wm.get("accel_signal", _z)).astype(np.int32)
1269	            atr_cu_full = (all_wm.get("atr_crossed_above", _z) > 0.25).astype(np.int32)
1270	            atr_cd_full = (all_wm.get("atr_crossed_below", _z) > 0.25).astype(np.int32)
1271	            acc_cu_full = (all_wm.get("accel_crossed_up", _z) > 0.25).astype(np.int32)
1272	            acc_cd_full = (all_wm.get("accel_crossed_down", _z) > 0.25).astype(np.int32)
1273	
1274	        ai_m_full = all_wm.get("ai_matrix", np.zeros((n, n_dates), np.float32))
1275	        ai_concl_full = np.where(ai_m_full > 60, "BUY", np.where(ai_m_full < 40, "SELL", "HOLD"))
1276	        ai_bias_full = np.where(ai_m_full > 55, "bullish", np.where(ai_m_full < 45, "bearish", "neutral"))
1277	
1278	        cols = ["string_id", "date", "name", "price", "change_pct", "volume", "weighted_alpha",
1279	                "atrp", "streak", "atr_signal", "atr_stop", "atr_value", "atr_streak",

1284	                "ai_overall_score", "ai_bias", "ai_tech_score", "ai_momentum_score", "ai_volume_score",
1285	                "ai_events_score", "ai_volume_profile_score", "ai_trendline_score", "ai_sentiment_score",
1286	                "ai_conclusion", "ai_matrix"]
1287	        placeholders_str = ",".join(["?"] * len(cols))
1288	        insert_sql = f"INSERT INTO historical_string_screener ({','.join(cols)}) VALUES ({placeholders_str})"
1289	        none_n = [None] * n
1290	        ones_n = [1.0] * n
1291	        sids_list = list(sids)
1292	
1293	        # Bulk insert PRAGMA tuning
1294	        conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=OFF")
1295	        conn.execute("PRAGMA cache_size=-2000000")
1296	        conn.execute("PRAGMA locking_mode=EXCLUSIVE")
1297	
1298	        # When only_latest=True, compute indicators on full history but only insert last date
1299	        if only_latest:
1300	            loop_range = [n_dates - 1]
1301	        else:
1302	            loop_range = range(0, n_dates, chunk)
1303	
1304	        # Pre-build numpy arrays for all columns
1305	        sids_arr = np.array(sids_list)
1306	        none_n = [None] * n
1307	        ones_n = [1.0] * n
1308	
1309	        for d0 in loop_range:
1310	            d1 = min(d0 + chunk, n_dates)
1311	            cd_len = d1 - d0
1312	            _t_chunk = time.time()
1313	
1314	            all_batch = []
1315	            for di_off in range(cd_len):
1316	                di = d0 + di_off
1317	                dt = dates[di]
1318	                ai_idx = 0 if only_latest else di
1319	                stk_idx = -1 if only_latest else di
1320	
1321	                def _col(name, _off=0):
1322	                    if only_latest:
1323	                        return all_wm.get(name, np.zeros((n, 1), np.float32))[:, 0]
1324	                    return all_wm.get(name, np.zeros((n, n_dates), np.float32))[:, d0 + _off]
1325	                def _bcol(name, _off=0):
1326	                    if only_latest:
1327	                        return basket_ind[name][:, -1] if basket_ind is not None else _col(name, _off)
1328	                    return basket_ind[name][:, d0 + _off] if basket_ind is not None else _col(name, _off)
1329	
1330	                all_batch.extend(zip(
1331	                    sids_list, [dt]*n, sids_list,
1332	                    price_full[:, stk_idx].tolist(), ret[:, stk_idx].tolist(),
1333	                    _col("volume", di_off).tolist(), _col("weighted_alpha", di_off).tolist(),
1334	                    _col("atrp", di_off).tolist(), np.nan_to_num(streak_full[:, stk_idx], 
nan=0).astype(int).tolist(),
1335	                    atr_sig_full[:, stk_idx].tolist(), _bcol("atr_stop", di_off).tolist(),
1336	                    _bcol("atr_value", di_off).tolist(), np.nan_to_num(np.round(_bcol("atr_streak", di_off)), 
nan=0).astype(int).tolist(),
1337	                    atr_cu_full[:, stk_idx].tolist(), atr_cd_full[:, stk_idx].tolist(), ones_n,
1338	                    next_day_full[:, stk_idx].tolist(), next_5d_full[:, stk_idx].tolist(),
1339	                    _col("prob_up_1d", di_off).tolist(), _col("prob_up_5d", di_off).tolist(),
1340	                    none_n, none_n, none_n, none_n,
1341	                    _bcol("accel_a", di_off).tolist(), _bcol("accel_base", di_off).tolist(),
1342	                    acc_sig_full[:, stk_idx].tolist(), acc_cu_full[:, stk_idx].tolist(), acc_cd_full[:, 
stk_idx].tolist(),
1343	                    np.nan_to_num(np.round(_bcol("accel_streak", di_off)), nan=0).astype(int).tolist(), 
_col("confluence", di_off).tolist(),
1344	                    ai_m_full[:, ai_idx].tolist(), [ai_bias_full[ai_idx]]*n,
1345	                    _col("ai_tech_score", di_off).tolist(), _col("ai_momentum_score", di_off).tolist(),
1346	                    _col("ai_volume_score", di_off).tolist(), _col("ai_events_score", di_off).tolist(),
1347	                    _col("ai_volume_profile_score", di_off).tolist(), _col("ai_trendline_score", di_off).tolist(),
1348	                    _col("ai_sentiment_score", di_off).tolist(), [ai_concl_full[ai_idx]]*n,
1349	                    ai_m_full[:, ai_idx].tolist(),
1350	                ))
1351	
1352	            for bi in range(0, len(all_batch), 250000):
1353	                conn.executemany(insert_sql, all_batch[bi:bi+250000])
1354	            conn.commit()
1355	            total_rows += len(all_rows)
1356	            _elapsed = time.time() - t0
1357	            _eta = (_elapsed / d1) * (n_dates - d1) if d1 > 0 else 0
1358	            logger.info(f"[{market}] chunk {d0}-{d1}: {len(all_rows):,} rows in {time.time()-_t_chunk:.1f}s 
(total {total_rows:,}, ETA {int(_eta)}s)")
1359	            if progress_callback:
1360	                progress_callback(int(d1 / n_dates * 100), f"Historical strings: {d1}/{n_dates} dates, 
{total_rows:,} rows, chunk {time.time()-_t_chunk:.1f}s")
1361	        logger.info(f"[{market}] historical string screener done: {total_rows} rows in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s")
1362	        logger.info(f"[{market}] creating indexes...")
1363	        _t_idx = time.time()
1364	        conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hs_sym_date ON historical_string_screener (string_id, date)")
1365	        conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hs_date ON historical_string_screener (date)")
1366	        conn.commit()
1367	        logger.info(f"[{market}] indexes created in {time.time()-_t_idx:.1f}s")
1368	        return total_rows
1369	    finally:
1370	        conn.close()

1373	HIST_TEXT_COLS = {"ai_bias", "ai_conclusion"}
1374	
1375	
1376	def _load_hist_metrics_chunk(conn, market, sym_list, chunk_dates, W=None, Wn=None, indices=None, weights=None):
1377	    """Load ALL metric columns in ONE query (batched by 500 dates for SQLite limit), project via gather+einsum.
1378	    Optimized: uses pd.read_sql_query for bulk load instead of per-row Python loops."""
1379	    import pandas as pd
1380	    num_cols = [c for c in HIST_COLS if c not in HIST_TEXT_COLS]
1381	    cols_sql = ", ".join(num_cols)
1382	    n_sym = len(sym_list)
1383	    n_dates = len(chunk_dates)
1384	    raw = {c: np.zeros((n_sym, n_dates), dtype=np.float32) for c in num_cols}
1385	    date_idx = {d: i for i, d in enumerate(chunk_dates)}
1386	    sym_idx = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(sym_list)}
1387	
1388	    sql_batch = 500
1389	    sym_batch = 400  # SQLite 999-variable limit: 400 symbols + 2 dates = 402 params
1390	    all_dfs = []
1391	    for sb in range(0, len(chunk_dates), sql_batch):
1392	        batch_dates = chunk_dates[sb:sb+sql_batch]
1393	        min_date = batch_dates[0]
1394	        max_date = batch_dates[-1]
1395	        for si in range(0, len(sym_list), sym_batch):
1396	            batch_syms = sym_list[si:si+sym_batch]
1397	            placeholders = ",".join(["?"] * len(batch_syms))
1398	            q = (f"SELECT symbol, date, {cols_sql} FROM historical_screener "
1399	                 f"WHERE date >= ? AND date <= ? AND symbol IN ({placeholders})")
1400	            df = pd.read_sql_query(q, conn, params=[min_date, max_date] + list(batch_syms))
---dumbmoney/basket_screener.py 1400
1400	            df = pd.read_sql_query(q, conn, params=[min_date, max_date] + list(batch_syms))
1401	            all_dfs.append(df)
1402	            del df
1403	
1404	    if not all_dfs:
1405	        out = {c: np.zeros((n_sym, n_dates), dtype=np.float32) for c in num_cols}
1406	        return out
1407	
1408	    rows_df = pd.concat(all_dfs, ignore_index=True)
1409	    del all_dfs
1410	
1411	    si = rows_df["symbol"].map(sym_idx).fillna(-1).astype(np.int32).values
1412	    di = rows_df["date"].map(date_idx).fillna(-1).astype(np.int32).values
1413	    valid = (si >= 0) & (di >= 0)
1414	    si_v = si[valid]
1415	    di_v = di[valid]
1416	
1417	    for col in num_cols:
1418	        if col in rows_df.columns:
1419	            vals = rows_df[col].to_numpy(dtype=np.float32, na_value=np.nan)
1420	        else:
1421	            vals = np.zeros(len(rows_df), dtype=np.float32)
1422	        vals_v = vals[valid]
1423	        mask = np.isfinite(vals_v)
1424	        raw[col][si_v[mask], di_v[mask]] = vals_v[mask]
1425	
1426	    del rows_df
1427	
1428	    out = {}
1429	    if indices is not None and weights is not None:
1430	        weight_sums = weights.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
1431	        weight_sums[weight_sums == 0] = 1.0
1432	        Wn_local = weights / weight_sums
1433	        for col in num_cols:
1434	            vals = raw[col]
1435	            gathered = vals[indices]
1436	            if col == "volume":
1437	                out[col] = np.einsum('bij,bi->bj', gathered, weights)
1438	            else:
1439	                out[col] = np.einsum('bij,bi->bj', gathered, Wn_local)
1440	    elif W is not None:
1441	        weight_sums = np.array(W.sum(axis=1)).ravel().astype(np.float32)
1442	        weight_sums[weight_sums == 0] = 1.0
1443	        Wn_mat = W / weight_sums[:, None]
1444	        for col in num_cols:
1445	            vals = raw[col]
1446	            if col == "volume":
1447	                out[col] = W @ vals
1448	            else:
1449	                out[col] = Wn_mat @ vals
1450	    return out
1451

1453	def get_string_screener(market="US", page=1, per_page=50, sort="weighted_alpha", sort_dir="desc",
1454	                        search="", exchange="", asset_type="", date_cutoff="", args=None,
1455	                        string_id_like=None):
1456	    args = args or {}
1457	    conn = get_db(market)
1458	    try:
1459	        if date_cutoff:
1460	            where = ["h.date = ?", "h.string_id IN (SELECT string_id FROM string_universe WHERE market=?)"]
1461	            params = [date_cutoff, market]
1462	            if string_id_like:
1463	                where.append(f"h.string_id LIKE ?"); params.append(string_id_like)
1464	            if search:
1465	                where.append("(h.string_id LIKE ? OR h.name LIKE ?)")
1466	                params.extend([f"%{search}%", f"%{search}%"])
1467	            if exchange:
1468	                where.append("'BASKET' = ?"); params.append(exchange)
1469	            if asset_type:
1470	                where.append("'basket' = ?"); params.append(asset_type)
1471	            _apply_numeric_filters(where, params, args, "h.")
1472	            _apply_signal_filters(where, params, args, "h.")
1473	            where_str = " AND ".join(where)
1474	            total = conn.execute(f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM historical_string_screener h WHERE {where_str}", 
params).fetchone()[0]
1475	            order_col = _map_sort(sort, "h.")
1476	            direction = "DESC" if sort_dir == "desc" else "ASC"
1477	            rows = conn.execute(
1478	                f"SELECT h.string_id as symbol, h.name, 'BASKET' as exchange, 'basket' as asset_class, h.price, 
h.change_pct, "
1479	                f"h.volume, h.weighted_alpha, h.atrp, h.streak, h.atr_signal, h.atr_stop, h.atr_value, "
1480	                f"h.atr_streak, h.atr_crossed_above, h.atr_crossed_below, h.atr_multiplier, "
1481	                f"h.next_day_return, h.next_5d_return, h.prob_up_1d, h.prob_up_5d, h.pre_price, h.pre_change_pct, 
"
1482	                f"h.post_price, h.post_change_pct, h.accel_a, h.accel_base, h.accel_signal, "
1483	                f"h.accel_crossed_up, h.accel_crossed_down, h.accel_streak, h.confluence, "
1484	                f"h.ai_overall_score, h.ai_bias, h.ai_tech_score, h.ai_momentum_score, "
1485	                f"h.ai_volume_score, h.ai_events_score, h.ai_volume_profile_score, "
1486	                f"h.ai_trendline_score, h.ai_sentiment_score, h.ai_conclusion, h.ai_matrix, "
1487	                f"h.date as last_updated "
1488	                f"FROM historical_string_screener h WHERE {where_str} ORDER BY {order_col} {direction} "
1489	                f"LIMIT ? OFFSET ?", params + [per_page, (page - 1) * per_page]).fetchall()
1490	            data = [dict(r) for r in rows]
1491	            return {"data": data, "total": total, "page": page, "per_page": per_page,
1492	                    "total_pages": (total + per_page - 1) // per_page, "historical": True, "date": date_cutoff}
1493	        else:
1494	            where = ["m.market = ?"]
1495	            params = [market]
1496	            if string_id_like:
1497	                where.append(f"m.string_id LIKE ?"); params.append(string_id_like)
1498	            if search:
1499	                where.append("(m.string_id LIKE ? OR m.name LIKE ?)")
1500	                params.extend([f"%{search}%", f"%{search}%"])
1501	            if exchange:
1502	                where.append("m.exchange = ?"); params.append(exchange)
1503	            if asset_type:
1504	                where.append("m.asset_class = ?"); params.append(asset_type)
1505	            _apply_numeric_filters(where, params, args, "m.")
1506	            _apply_signal_filters(where, params, args, "m.")
1507	            where_str = " AND ".join(where)
1508	            total = conn.execute(
1509	                f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM string_screener_metrics m WHERE {where_str}", params).fetchone()[0]
1510	            order_col = _map_sort(sort, "m.")
1511	            direction = "DESC" if sort_dir == "desc" else "ASC"
1512	            rows = conn.execute(
1513	                f"SELECT m.string_id as symbol, m.name, m.exchange, m.asset_class, m.price, m.change_pct, "
1514	                f"m.volume, m.weighted_alpha, m.atrp, m.streak, m.atr_signal, m.atr_stop, m.atr_value, "
1515	                f"m.atr_streak, m.atr_crossed_above, m.atr_crossed_below, m.atr_multiplier, "
1516	                f"m.next_day_return, m.next_5d_return, m.prob_up_1d, m.prob_up_5d, m.pre_price, m.pre_change_pct, 
"
1517	                f"m.post_price, m.post_change_pct, m.accel_a, m.accel_base, m.accel_signal, "
1518	                f"m.accel_crossed_up, m.accel_crossed_down, m.accel_streak, m.confluence, "
1519	                f"m.ai_overall_score, m.ai_bias, m.ai_tech_score, m.ai_momentum_score, "
1520	                f"m.ai_volume_score, m.ai_events_score, m.ai_volume_profile_score, "
1521	                f"m.ai_trendline_score, m.ai_sentiment_score, m.ai_conclusion, m.ai_matrix, "
1522	                f"m.updated_at as last_updated "
1523	                f"FROM string_screener_metrics m WHERE {where_str} ORDER BY {order_col} {direction} "
1524	                f"LIMIT ? OFFSET ?", params + [per_page, (page - 1) * per_page]).fetchall()
1525	            data = [dict(r) for r in rows]
1526	            return {"data": data, "total": total, "page": page, "per_page": per_page,
1527	                    "total_pages": (total + per_page - 1) // per_page, "historical": False}
1528	    finally:
1529	        conn.close()
1530	
1531	
1532	def _apply_numeric_filters(where, params, args, pfx):
1533	    m = {
1534	        "min_price": "price", "max_price": "price", "min_wa": "weighted_alpha",
1535	        "max_wa": "weighted_alpha", "min_change": "change_pct", "max_change": "change_pct",
1536	        "min_streak": "streak", "min_volume": "volume",
1537	    }
1538	    for arg, col in m.items():
1539	        v = args.get(arg)
1540	        if v in (None, ""):
1541	            continue
1542	        v = float(v)
1543	        if arg.startswith("min"):
1544	            where.append(f"{pfx}{col} >= ?"); params.append(v)
1545	        else:
1546	            where.append(f"{pfx}{col} <= ?"); params.append(v)
1547	
1548	
1549	def _apply_signal_filters(where, params, args, pfx):
1550	    atr = args.get("atr_status", "")
1551	    if atr == "above":
1552	        where.append(f"{pfx}atr_signal = 1")
1553	    elif atr == "below":
1554	        where.append(f"{pfx}atr_signal = -1")
1555	    elif atr == "crossed-above":
1556	        where.append(f"{pfx}atr_crossed_above = 1")
1557	    elif atr == "crossed-below":
1558	        where.append(f"{pfx}atr_crossed_below = 1")
1559	    accel = args.get("accel_status", "")
1560	    if accel == "up":
1561	        where.append(f"{pfx}accel_signal = 1")
1562	    elif accel == "down":
1563	        where.append(f"{pfx}accel_signal = -1")
1564	    elif accel == "crossed-up":
1565	        where.append(f"{pfx}accel_crossed_up = 1")
1566	    elif accel == "crossed-down":
1567	        where.append(f"{pfx}accel_crossed_down = 1")
1568	
1569	
1570	def _map_sort(sort, pfx):
1571	    allowed = {"symbol", "name", "price", "change_pct", "weighted_alpha", "volume", "streak",
1572	               "atr_signal", "atr_stop", "atr_value", "atr_streak", "atrp",
1573	               "atr_crossed_above", "atr_crossed_below", "prob_up_1d", "prob_up_5d",
1574	               "next_day_return", "next_5d_return", "confluence", "accel_a", "accel_base",
1575	               "accel_signal", "accel_streak", "accel_crossed_up", "accel_crossed_down",
1576	               "ai_overall_score", "ai_bias", "ai_tech_score", "ai_volume_profile_score",
1577	               "ai_trendline_score", "ai_sentiment_score", "ai_conclusion", "ai_matrix"}
1578	    if sort not in allowed:
1579	        sort = "weighted_alpha"
1580	    if sort == "symbol":
1581	        sort = "string_id"
1582	    return f"{pfx}{sort}"

1585	def get_string_detail(string_id, market="US"):
1586	    conn = get_db(market)
1587	    try:
1588	        univ = conn.execute(
1589	            "SELECT string_id, market, num_stocks, expression, created_at FROM string_universe WHERE string_id=?",
1590	            (string_id,)).fetchone()
1591	        if not univ:
1592	            return None
1593	        cons = conn.execute(
1594	            "SELECT symbol, weight FROM string_constituents WHERE string_id=?", (string_id,)).fetchall()
1595	        metrics = conn.execute(
1596	            "SELECT * FROM string_screener_metrics WHERE string_id=?", (string_id,)).fetchone()
1597	        symbols = [c[0] for c in cons]
1598	        contrib = []
1599	        if symbols:
1600	            placeholders = ",".join("?" * len(symbols))
1601	            rows = conn.execute(
1602	                f"SELECT s.symbol, s.price, s.change_pct, s.weighted_alpha, s.atr_signal, s.prob_up_1d, 
a.ai_matrix "
1603	                f"FROM stats s LEFT JOIN ai_analysis a ON s.symbol=a.symbol "
1604	                f"WHERE s.symbol IN ({placeholders})", symbols).fetchall()
1605	            smap = {r[0]: r for r in rows}
1606	            for sym, w in cons:
1607	                r = smap.get(sym)
1608	                if r:
1609	                    contrib.append({"symbol": sym, "weight": w, "price": r[1], "change_pct": r[2],
1610	                                    "weighted_alpha": r[3], "atr_signal": r[4], "prob_up_1d": r[5],
1611	                                    "ai_matrix": r[6]})
1612	        hist = conn.execute(
1613	            "SELECT date, price, change_pct, ai_matrix, atr_signal, weighted_alpha "
1614	            "FROM historical_string_screener WHERE string_id=? ORDER BY date", (string_id,)).fetchall()
1615	        return {
1616	            "universe": dict(univ),
1617	            "constituents": [{"symbol": c[0], "weight": c[1]} for c in cons],
1618	            "metrics": dict(metrics) if metrics else {},
1619	            "contributions": contrib,
1620	            "history": [dict(r) for r in hist],
1621	        }
1622	    finally:
1623	        conn.close()
1624

1696	def build_leaderboard_cache(market="US"):
1697	    """Build pre-aggregated leaderboard cache from historical_string_screener.
1698	    Called during refresh so the leaderboard API can read from a small cache table."""
1699	    t0 = time.time()
1700	    conn = get_db(market)
1701	    try:
1702	        exists = conn.execute(
1703	            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='string_leaderboard_cache'"
1704	        ).fetchone()
1705	        if not exists:
1706	            conn.execute("""CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS string_leaderboard_cache (
1707	                string_id TEXT, market TEXT, expression TEXT, n_dates INTEGER,
1708	                win_rate REAL, avg_return REAL, total_return REAL, sharpe REAL,
1709	                best_day REAL, worst_day REAL, avg_ai REAL,
1710	                PRIMARY KEY (string_id, market))""")
1711	            conn.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_slc_market ON string_leaderboard_cache(market)")
1712	            conn.commit()
1713	
1714	        hist_exists = conn.execute(
1715	            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='historical_string_screener'"
1716	        ).fetchone()
1717	        if not hist_exists:
1718	            logger.info(f"[{market}] no historical_string_screener, skipping leaderboard cache")
1719	            return 0
1720	
1721	        rows = conn.execute(f"""
1722	            SELECT string_id,
1723	                COUNT(*) as n_dates,
1724	                ROUND(AVG(CASE WHEN next_day_return > 0 THEN 100.0 ELSE 0 END), 2) as win_pct,
1725	                ROUND(AVG(next_day_return), 4) as avg_ret,
1726	                ROUND(MAX(next_day_return), 2) as best_day,
1727	                ROUND(MIN(next_day_return), 2) as worst_day,
1728	                ROUND(AVG(CAST(ai_matrix AS REAL)), 1) as avg_ai,
1729	                MIN(price) as first_price,
1730	                MAX(price) as last_price
1731	            FROM historical_string_screener
1732	            WHERE next_day_return IS NOT NULL
1733	            GROUP BY string_id
1734	            HAVING COUNT(*) >= 20
1735	        """).fetchall()
1736	
1737	        univ = conn.execute(
1738	            "SELECT string_id, expression FROM string_universe WHERE market=?", (market,)
1739	        ).fetchall()
1740	        expr_map = {r[0]: r[1] for r in univ}
1741	
1742	        conn.execute("DELETE FROM string_leaderboard_cache WHERE market=?", (market,))
1743	        batch = []
1744	        for r in rows:
1745	            sid = r[0]
1746	            n_dates = r[1]
1747	            win_pct = r[2] or 0
1748	            avg_ret = r[3] or 0
1749	            best_day = r[4] or 0
1750	            worst_day = r[5] or 0
1751	            avg_ai = r[6] or 50
1752	            fp = float(r[7]) if r[7] else 0
1753	            lp = float(r[8]) if r[8] else 0
1754	            total_ret = round(((lp / fp - 1) * 100), 2) if fp > 0 else 0
1755	            sharpe = round(avg_ret * (win_pct / 50.0), 2)
1756	            expr = expr_map.get(sid, sid)
1757	            short_expr = expr[:60] + ("..." if len(expr) > 60 else "")
1758	            batch.append((sid, market, short_expr, n_dates, win_pct, avg_ret,
1759	                          total_ret, sharpe, best_day, worst_day, avg_ai))
1760	
1761	        if batch:
1762	            conn.executemany(
1763	                "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO string_leaderboard_cache "
1764	                "(string_id, market, expression, n_dates, win_rate, avg_return, "
1765	                "total_return, sharpe, best_day, worst_day, avg_ai) "
1766	                "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)", batch)
1767	            conn.commit()
1768	
1769	        logger.info(f"[{market}] leaderboard cache built: {len(batch)} strings in {time.time()-t0:.1f}s")
1770	        return len(batch)
1771	    finally:
1772	        conn.close()
